vaḷḷum 
mukavuri
tirukkuṟaḷ naṇparkaḷē!
vaṇakkam
vaḷḷuvam eṉap peyariya nūl paṉṉiraṇṭu coṟ poḻivukaḷik koṇṭatu. ivi yāṇṭum yār muṉṉum pēcappaṭṭaṉa valla; yāṉē tiruvaḷḷuvari muṉ vittup pēciṉāṟpōl eḻutiya kaṟpaṉic coṟ poḻivukaḷ. iḵtōr ilakkiyap putuneṟi. uṅkaṭkum eṉakkum neñcak kalappu - cuṟṟappiṇippu - uṇṭākum eṉṟa nōkkattāl, kaṟpaṉip poḻivu neṟiyi mēṟkoṇṭēṉ.
ceṉṟa kāla nammoḻittāyiṉ iyaṟki vaḷam, nikaḻkālat tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ moḻiyaṟivuk kuṟivu, varuṅkālat tamiḻakattiṉ tūya peruñ ceyalkaḷ eṉa ivvellām nīḷa niṉintēṉ. in niṉivuc cūl mutircciyāl piṟantatu vaḷḷuvat tamiḻk kuḻavi.
vaḷḷuva nūliṉ naṭi kaṟpavari niṟuttik kaṟkac ceyyum; vēkap paṭippik kuṟittu viḷaṅkip paṭikkat tūṇṭum; col tōṟum oṉṟi niṉṟu maṉam vittu aṟivu celutti uṇmi kāṇac collum. ōraḷavēṉum tamiḻ ilakkiyam payiṉṟārkku - moḻiyaṟivu peṟa viḻivārkku - vaḷḷuvam ariya naṭiyataṉṟu; karuttukku uriya naṭiyatu eṉat tamiḻaṟiñar uṭaṉpaṭuvar.
vaḷḷuvam tokuppu nūlaṉṟu; mutaṟkaṇ muḻutum variyaṟuttuk koṇṭu muraṇaṟuttu eḻutiya muṟinūl. ātaliṉ, kaṟkum perumakkaḷ cōṟṟuppatam kāṇpatu pōl iṭiyiṭiyē oru pakkatti virittut taḷḷikkallātu, mutal toṭaṅki varaṉmuṟiyāyk kaṟpārāka, iḵtu eṉ viḻivu.
tūya kiḻamiyīr! vaḷḷuvanūṟ karuttukkaḷip piṟar ēṟṟal vēṇṭum; ēṟkumāṟu valintēṉum urittal vēṇṭum eṉpatu eṉ kuṟikkōḷ aṉṟu. yāṉ kaṇṭa vaḷḷuvaṅkaḷi - avaṟṟukku nilikkaḷaṉākak koṇṭa ulakiyal viḷakkaṅkaḷi - piṟar aṟital vēṇṭum; aṟiyumāṟu aḻuttam peṟa moḻital vēṇṭum eṉpatuvē eṉ kuṟikkōḷ. koḷḷuvatum taḷḷuvatum num aṟiviṉ taṉiyurimi. taḷḷuṅkāl āciriyaṉukku mācu kaṟpikka muyala vēṇṭā eṉṟu vēṇṭuval.
vaḷḷuvanūl iyaṟkiyaḻakoṭu veḷivaraṟkup patipputaviyum pārviyutaviyum ceytaḷitta cātu accakattārkkum, pāri niliyattārkkum uḷḷaṉpu peritu uṭiyēṉ. 
				va.cupa. māṇikkam
kārikkuṭi,
1-4-1953	


tamiḻkkātal 
mukavuri
kātal uṭalukku nallatu. uḷḷattukku nallatu, ūrukku nallatu, ulakattukkē nallatu. an nalla kātal etu?
kātal eṉpatu uṭaṟpaci, uḷḷappaci, uyirppaci, piṟavip paci, appaci tīrkkum uṇavu neṟi etu?
aḵṟiṇiyuyirkaḷiṉ kātalāvatu kallāk kāmam, iyaṟki vīṟu. moḻi pēcum makkaḷiṉattiṉ kātal niṉivil iṉittu, aṟivil viḷaṅkik kalviyil vaḷarvatu, av vaḷarccik kalvi etu?
kātaliyum ataṉ uṇarviyum ataṉ kalviyiyum teḷiya vēṇṭiṉ, teḷivukku vēṟiṭam illi. tamiḻp pēriṉam kaṇṭa akattiṇiyi nāṭuka. tolkāppiyam caṅkavilakkiyam tirukkuṟaḷ eṉṟa muttamiḻaka nūlkaḷik kaṟka muntuka. kātal cāṉṟa tamiḻiyaṅkaḷiyellām teḷintu nukarntu kuṭitaḻittu vāḻka.
neṟiyākavum aḷavākavum uramākavum nāṇamākavum kaṟpākavum kāmakkūṟukaḷic cevvaṉam kāṭṭum ōr ulaka ilakkiyam tamiḻiṟṟāṉ uṇṭu. atuvē akattiṇi. it tiṇikkalvi paruvam vantuṟṟa nampiyar naṅkiyark kellām vēṇṭum, vēṇṭum. aṉṉavar tirumaṇam naṟumaṇam peṟum. ak kātalartam vāḻvil uḷḷappūcal irātu. ūṭaṟpūcalē irukkum; veṅkaṭciṉam irātu, ceṅkaṭ ciṉamē irukkum.
akattiṇi kaṟṟa kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyum piṟanta ulaki matippar. eṭutta uṭampi matippar, koṇṭa maṇatti matippar, oruvar oruvartam kāma nāṭikaḷip purivar, kāmakkuṟivu kaṭamik kuṟivām eṉṟu uṇarvar, vittillāk kāmap paḻatti uṇpar, meyyiṉpatti uyiriṉpamākap pōṟṟi oḻukuvar, akattiṇi vāḻkkiyi aṉputtiṇi vāḻkkiyākak kāṇpar.
ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakattiṉ ṭākṭar paṭṭattukku yāṉ eḻutiya āṅkila nūliṉ muṭipukaḷ ittamiḻ nūṟkaṇ virivākavum viḷakkamākavum amintuḷa.
innūliṉ cemmikku utaviya tirukkuṟaḷ uri vēṟṟumi āciriyar irā. cāraṅkapāṇi em.ē. avarkaṭku naṉṟiyaṉ.
innūlic cevvaṉam patippitta kārikkuṭi teṉṉintiya accakattārkku naṉṟiyuṭiyēṉ.
innūlik kaṟkat toṭaṅkuvōr iṟutiyil uḷḷa akattiṇikkalvi eṉṉum pakutiyi mutaṟkaṇ kaṟka vēṇṭuvaṉ.
		va.cupa. māṇikkam
katirakam, kārikkuṭi, 15.6.1962.



eṉ aṉpuṟi 
makā makōpāttiyāya 
ṭākṭar u.vē. cāminātiyar avarkaḷukku
pārkāttār āyirampēr; pacittārk kākap
	payirkāttār āyirampēr; pālark kāka
mārkāttār āyirampēr vāḻnta nāṭṭil
	maṉaṅkātta tamiḻttāy “eṉ uṭimi yellām
yārkāttār” eṉakkēṭka, oruvaṉ ammā
	yāṉkāppēṉ eṉaveḻuntāṉ cāmi nātaṉ
nīrkātta tamiḻakattār neñciṉ uḷḷāṉ
	nilikātta maliyimaya neṟṟi mēlāṉ.
poyyāta tamiḻkkumari īṉṟa cāmi!
	pōkāta pukaḻkkaṉṉi maṇanta nāta!
ceyyāta moḻittoṇṭu ceyta aiya!
	ciṟukāta neñcattēm vaṇaṅku cemmal!
eyyāta tiruvaṭiyāl eṇpat tāṇṭum
	iḷiyāta uḷḷattāl eṅkuñ ceṉṟu
neyyāta toṉṉūlkaḷ nilikka vitta
	nīṅkāta tamiḻkkuyirē! niṉtāḷ vāḻka.
va. cupa. māṇikkam

kampar
muṉṉuri
tamiḻp pērāciriyar mūtaṟiñar irā. pi. cētuppiḷḷi tam aṉṉiyār niṉivākac coruṇāmpāḷ nitic coṟ poḻivu eṉṉum ōr aṟakkaṭṭaḷiyi aṇṇāmalip palkalikkaḻakattil niṟuviṉar. iv vamippiṉkīḻ kampar eṉṟa pulavar perumāṉ talippil 1964 pippiravari 21-22-23ām nāṭkaḷil, kāppiyap pārvi, kāppiyak kaḷaṅkaḷ, kāppiya nērmi eṉṟa muttiṟap poruḷ paṟṟic coṟpoḻintēṉ.
aṇṇāmalip palkalikkaḻakattil tamiḻ māṇavaṉākap paṭipputavi peṟṟuc cērntu 1940il vittuvāṉ paṭṭam eytiyatum, āyvu māṇavaṉāka iruntapiṉ virivuriyāḷaṉākap paṇi toṭarntatum, neṭṭiṭikkuppiṉ, muttuṟiyum oruṅkiṇinta tamiḻp peruntuṟikku 1970 mutal ēḻāṇṭuk kālam tamiḻp pērāciriyaṉākavum intiyap pula mutaṉmiyarākavum paṇiyil iruntatum, palkalikkaḻakam 1979il poṉ viḻāk koṇṭāṭiya ñāṉṟu mūtaṟiñar (ṉu. ṭuviva) eṉṟa ciṟappup paṭṭam eṉakku vaḻaṅkiyatum ellām eṉ vāḻviṉ naṟkūṟukaḷākum. vaḷḷal aṇṇāmaliyaracar mēl, ‘nillāta celvatti nilikkac ceytāṉ’ eṉṟu yāṉ pāṭiya eṇcīrp pāṭṭum, aracar muttiyavēḷ mēl, ‘maṇi kāṭṭum kōpuram pōl uyarnta neñcum’ eṉṟu toṭaṅkum eṉatu eṇcīrp pāṭṭum palkalikkaḻaka vāḻttup pāṭalāka ēṟṟukkoḷḷap peṟṟaṉa. ippāṭalkaḷ eṉ naṉṟiyuṇarvup patikaṅkaḷākum.
kampar eṉṟa muppoḻivukaḷum kāppiyavilakkiyattiṉ muḻup pārviyi valiyuṟuttuvaṉa. peruṅkāppiyac ceyyuṭ kaḷit taṉippāṭaṟ ṟiraṭṭāka matittut tokukkum kuṟum pārvi muḻuvuṇmiyaṅkaḷip pulappaṭutta māṭṭātu. kāppiyaṅkaḷil pala ceyyuṭkaḷit tērntu potuvilakkiyap parappukkākat tokuttut tiraṭṭu veḷiyiṭuvatiyum, taṉip pāṭalkaḷil ilakkiya nayaṅ kāṇpataṉiyum nāṉ kuṟiyākak karutavilli. avi oru vaki ilakkiyat toṇṭukaḷ. eṉiṉum kāppiyam eṉṟa ilakkiyaviṉattip poṟuttavari, muḻup pārviyē uriya neṟiyākum. āḻntu niṉṟu toṭarntu ōṭikkiṭakkum kaviyuḷḷattiyum aṉittup pāṭalkaḷilum pāyntōṭum uṇarcciyorumiyiyum kaviñaṉiṉ niṉivāṟṟaliyum amippuk kaṭṭumāṉaṅ kaḷiyum kāṇpataṟku muḻuppārviyē talippārviyākum eṉa aṟika. muḻuppārviyam eṉṟa ilakkiyakkaṇ eṉik kāppiyaṅkaṭkum emmoḻik kāppiyaṅkaṭkum poruntum eṉavum uṇarka.
‘mayiluṭic cāya lāḷi vañciyā muṉṉam nīṇṭa
	eyiluṭi ilaṅki nātaṉ itayamāñ ciṟiyil vittāṉ’					āraṇiya. (3151)
‘kaḷḷirukkum malark kūntaṟ cāṉakiyi
		maṉacciṟiyiṟ karanta kātal
	uḷḷirukkum eṉakkaruti uṭalpukuntu
		taṭaviyatō oruvaṉ vāḷi’ 							uyutta. 	(9940)
itayamāñ ciṟiyil cītiyi vittāṉ irāvaṇaṉ eṉṟa āraṇiyak karuttukkum maṉaccitiyiṟ karanta kātal cuvaṭaṟṟu aṟavē oḻintatu eṉṟa uyuttak kāṇṭak karuttukkum iṭippaṭṭu 6589 ceyyuṭkaḷ kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. ivvaḷavu periya tokic ceyyuṭkaḷiṉ uḷḷōṭṭamum payaṉum irāvaṇaṉ tūymiyākkam eṉṟa muḻuppārvi yiṉāṟṟāṉ veḷippaṭum.   
iṉṟiya kalviyulakilum āyvukkaḷattilum muḻup pārviyam eṉṟa tiṟappāṭu kaṭippiṭikkappaṭavilli. ataṉāl peruṅkāppiyarkaḷiṉ cintaṉi yōṭṭaṅkaḷ veḷippaṭāmal tirumaṟik kāṭāyiṉa. itaṉ viḷiveṉṉa? cilappatikāram, peruṅkati, cīvakacintāmaṇi, periyapurāṇam, irāmāyaṇam pōṉṟa peruṅkāppiyam iyaṟṟum paṭip pāṟṟali ikkālak kaviñarkaḷ iḻantu varukiṉṟaṉar. muḻu nūṟpaṭippum muḻuc cintaṉiyum vāyātapōtu, taṉicceyyuḷ  vēṭkiyum cintaṉik kuṟukkamum mēlōṅkumpōtu, akal neṭuṅkāppiyap paṭippukkut takka cūḻnili eṅkē varum?
muṉṉik kāppiyaṅkaḷi muḻuppārviyōṭu tiṟaṉāṭuvataṟkum, varuṅkālattup peruṅkāppiyap paṉuvalkaḷ muḻuppārviyōṭu piṟappataṟkum kampar eṉṟa ittiṟaṉūl tūṇṭukōlākum eṉṟu nampukiṉṟēṉ. ippoḻivukaḷiṉ muḻunōkkam ituvē.
maṇanta ñāṉṟiṉum makkaḷi īṉṟa ñāṉṟu vayāvum varuttamum uṟṟa peṇkulam peritum uvakkiṉṟatu. atupōla nūlkaḷi varinteḻutiya kālattik kāṭṭilum avi mukavaḻakum mutukaḻakum peṟṟup patippuruvam koṇṭapōtu, eḻuttukkulam cemmāppu eytukiṉṟatu; tam karuttukkaḷ makkaṭ cantiyil aṅkāṭumpōtu eḻuttāṇmiyar ētō oruvakik koṭi ulakiṟkuc ceytatu pōṉṟa perumitavuṇarvu peṟukiṉṟaṉar. ivvuṇarvē eḻuttiṉ uṇmic celvam.
eṉ eḻuttup palavaṟṟic cevvaricippaṭutti yāṉ kaṇṭu makiḻavum maṉpatiyiṉar kaṟṟu makiḻavum patippittu varum maṇivācakar patippakattiṟkum patippuc cemmal meyyappa ṉārkkum naṉṟiyuṇarvoṭu vāḻttuk kūṟukiṉṟēṉ. patippakam pala paricu peṟṟu nāṭu matikkum nūlakamāka mēlum vaḷarntōṅkuka eṉṟu vāḻttuvaṉ.
eṉ varici nūlkaḷ piḻitiruttam illāta patippākac ceppamoṭu veḷivarutaṟku uṟutuṇi ceytu varum eṉ vakupput tōḻar naṇpar muṉṉi mutalvar tiru. ci. celvat tāṇṭavarkku naṉṟi kūṟal naṭpukku mikiyāki viṭātā? eṉiṉum naṉṟiyi uṇarkiṉṟēṉ.
va. cupa. māṇikkam
katirakam, kārikkuṭi
āvaṇi 2018


oppiyal nōkku
 muṉṉuri
oppiyal tamiḻukkup putiyatuṟiyō eṉiṉ, illavē yilli. tolkāppiyam kaḷavuk kūṭṭattiṉik kantaruva vaḻakkattōṭu oppiṭak kāṇkiṉṟōm. oli niliyil antaṇar maṟiyōṭum col niliyil vaṭamoḻiyōṭum tolkāppiyattil cila oppiṭalkaḷ uḷa. `nūlōr tokuttavaṟṟuḷ ellām tali’ eṉavum, `yāmeyyāk kaṇṭavaṟṟuḷ illi’ eṉavum varum tirukkuṟaḷkaḷil oppiyaṟpārvi mukiḻkkiṉṟatu. eṉiṉum oppīṭuṇmik kālattuttāṉ oru perun taṉittuṟiyāka vērōṭik kiḷi virittu varuvati aṟakiṉṟōm. ittuṟiyilum nam tamiḻ vaḷarcci peṟal vēṇṭum. avvaḷarcci āḻap paranta aṟivu niliyilum cūḻacceṟinta ākkaniliyilum vaḷarumāyiṉ tamiḻc celvaṅkaḷ ulakak kaṇṇiṟkup pulappaṭum.
oru pulavaṉ taṉṉuḷ oppu, pulavar tammuḷ oppu, ōr ilakkiyan taṉṉuḷ oppu, ilakkiyaṅkaḷ tammuḷ oppu, oru moḻi taṉṉuḷoppu, moḻikaḷ tammuḷ oppu, orukālan taṉṉuḷ oppu, kālaṅkaḷ tammuḷ oppu eṉavāṅku oppiyal nilaṅkaḷ pala varappukkaḷ uṭiyavi eṉṟālum pulamiyulakiṉ potut taṭaṅkaḷiyum ovvoruvariṉtaṉic cuvaṭukaḷiyum āṟum nīrum pōla eṭuttukkāṭṭuvatē oppiyaliṉ mutaṟ payaṉākum.
oppiyal nōkku eṉṟa innūliṉkaṇ uḷḷa patiṉāṉku kaṭṭurikaḷil perum pālāṉavi orupuṭi oppumippāl uṭiyavi. muḻutum vēṟupaṭṭaṉavum, muṟṟum oṟṟumip paṭṭaṉavum oppīṭu vaṭṭattukkuc cālātaṉa. etaṉiyum etaṉōṭum oppiṭuvataṟku avaṟṟiṉkaṇ ottakūṟu, ovvākkūṟu eṉṟa iru taṉmiyum  vēṇṭum. vēṟṟīṭṭiyum uḷḷaṭakkiyatu oppīṭu eṉṟa kuṟiyīṭu. ivvoppiyal neṟikaḷ innūṟ kaṭṭurikaḷil tiṉiyaḷavu viḷakkam peṟukiṉṟaṉa.
maṇivācakar patippakam tamiḻukku ariya uriya periya ākka nūlkaḷi veḷiyiṭum perumita marapu koṇṭatu. ataṉ uṟutuṇiyāḷar tiru. ca.meyyappaṉ marapu kuṉṟā ākka nūlkaḷi veḷiyiṭum ūkkap patippāḷar. avar toṇṭukku naṉṟi.
	va.cupa. māṇikkam
kārikkuṭi, 26-1-78


tolkāppiyak kaṭal 
muṉṉuri
ōr eḻuttāciriyaṉ tāṉ cīrāka vaḷarntu varum aṟivup paruva kālaṅkaḷil kaṭṭurikaḷiyum nūlkaḷiyum eḻuti veḷiyiṭukiṉṟāṉ. muppatu vayatil eḻutiyavaṟṟiṟkum eṇpatu vayatil eḻutuvaṉavaṟṟiṟkum vēṟupāṭu iruppatē aṟiviyalpu. collāl naṭiyāl poruḷāl pulamiyāl koḷkiyāl ēṟṟam irukkalām; iṟakkam irukkalām; iraṇṭum kalanta maṟukkam irukkalām. eppaṭiyum māṟṟam iruntākum; eṉiṉum immāṟṟattūṭē ivaṉ iṉṉāṉ eṉṟu cuṭṭat takka nilināṭiyoṉṟu avveḻuttāḷaṉ paṭippil iḻin tōṭum piṟanta kuḻantiyiṉ eṉpu vaṭivamippu piṉṉip paruvaṅkaḷilum vēṟupaṭuvatilliyē. ovvoṉṟukkum niliyiyam uṇṭu.
eṉ nāṟpattiyāṇṭuk kāla eḻuttut tokutikaḷi maṟittu nōkki, ōriṉap patippukkaḷākak koṇṭu varum vāyppiṉi maṇivācakar patippakam eṉakku vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷatu. entap patippakattāriṭamiruntum yārukkum kiṭṭāta iṉiya vāyppiṉi nāṉ peṟukiṉṟēṉ. iḵtu aṟivaṟinta pēṟaṉṟō!
cintaṉik kaḷaṅkaḷ, tolkāppiya putumi, ilakkiya viḷakkam, oppiyal nōkku, entac cilampu, tolkāppiyat tiṟaṉ eṉṟa eṉ āṟu nūlkaḷilum palavēṟu poruṭ kaṭṭurikaḷ palacarakkup pōl kalantu viravik kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. avvappōtu appatippukkaḷ uruppeṟṟamiyiṉ, paltuṟik kalappiṉit tavirkka muṭiyavilli; eṉiṉum ataṉāl iru payaṉ uṇṭu. kaṟpavarkkuk kalappu palcuvi payantatu. eṉ kaṭṭurikaḷ taṉi iḻittāḷākak kiṭavāmal patippāṭi peṟṟuk kāppuṟuti eytaṉa. āciriyaṉ eḻuttukkaḷ uṭaṉukkuṭaṉ patippaṭakkam peṟātiruntāl, avaṟṟiṉ ūḻviṉi eṉṉāṅkol?
mēlē kuṟitta eṉatu āṟu nūlkaḷil uḷḷa kaṭṭuri yāyvukaḷ tolkāppiyam paṟṟiyavi, tirukkuṟaḷ paṟṟiyavi, caṅkavilakkiyam paṟṟiyavi, kāppiyaṅkaḷ paṟṟiyavi, tiṟaṉ paṟṟiyavi, kalivāḻvu paṟṟiyavi eṉavāṅku iṉavaki ceyyappaṭṭup putiya talippuk koṇṭa āṟu nūlkaḷākap patippup peṟukiṉṟaṉa. ōriṉa malart toṭiyalpōla ivi ōriṉap poruṭ patippukaḷām. ivvakip patippukkaḷāl kaṟpavarkku oru tuṟiyaṟivu kikūṭum. cintaṉic celvam tiraṇṭu kiṭikkum.
ip putuvariciyil tolkāppiyak kaṭal eṉpatu mutal nūlākum. tamiḻukkum tolkāppiyamē mutaṉūlaṉṟō! ki.pi. 12-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil nēmināta āciriyar kuṇavīra paṇṭitar tolkāppiyattik kaṭalākavum nēmināta nūli akkaṭaṟparappil paṉmuṟi ōṭum ciṟu paṭakākavum koḷvar.
tolkāp piyakkaṭaliṟ coṟṟīpac cuṟṟaḷakkap
palkāṟ koṇṭōṭum paṭakeṉpa
eṉpatu pārāṭṭu veṇpā. peruṅkaṭalil oru paṭaku tāṉā ōṭum? tirukkuṟaḷ vaṅkam, cilappatikāram maṇimēkali cintāmaṇi peruṅkati periyapurāṇam irāmāyaṇam mutalāṉa kalaṅkaḷ, vīra cōḻiyam veṇpāmāli naṉṉūl toṉṉūl mutalāṉa nāvāykaḷ, ciṟṟilakkiyat tōṇikaḷ, vaḷḷalār pāratiyār pāratitācaṉ kaṇṇatācaṉ ōṭaṅkaḷ ellām tolkāppiyak kaṭalil mitantu karikaṭantavi yallavā?
tolkāppiyattiṉ iṉṟiya nili oru nūṟṟāṇṭukku muntiyiṉum vēṟupaṭṭatu. tolkāppiyak kaḻakaṅkaḷ nāṭṭiṟ pala, tolkāppiyaṉ peyar koṇṭōr palaruḷar. paḷḷic ciṟārkaḷ kūṭat tamiḻt toṉṉūl tolkāppiyam eṉpataṉi aṟiyum naṟkālam itu. araciyal pāliyal illiyal ulakiyal pōriyal nilaviyal maṉpatiyiyal meyyiyal moḻiyiyal nāṭakaviyal uḷaviyal eṉa ettuṟikkaṇṇum tolkāppiyac cintaṉi vaḷarvatik kāṇkiṉṟōm. ulaka moḻiñarkaḷ nam tolkāppiyatti ulaka moḻip pērilakkaṇamāka matikkiṉṟaṉar ēṉ? palar karuttuppaṭi, tamiḻ ulaka mutaṉ moḻiyallavā? mūvāyiram āṇṭukku muntiya nūlāyiṉum karuttālum moḻinaṭiyālum paḻami eṉṟu collutaṟkilli. ‘cīriḷamit tiṟam’ eṉṟu viyantār maṉōṉmaṇiyam cuntaraṉār. palakōṭiyāṇṭu paṭinta curaṅkak kaṉimaṅkaḷ paḻamiyā? toḻiṟpuraṭci tarum putumiyaṉṟō? cintippār ciṟappuk kāṇpar.
tolkāppiyak kaṭal eṉap peyariya innūl muppattoru talippukkaḷ koṇṭatu. tolkāppiyattiṉ toṉmi putumi perumi ākkam arumi āḻam nayam celvākku eḷimi iṉimi etirkāla nōkku ellām ivaṟṟāl aṟiyalām. mali paḻami eṉṟu malikkavā ceykiṉṟōm? imayattiṉ muṭiyēṟip pāratak koṭiyi nāṭṭiṉōmaṉṟē. tolkāppiyam kālattāṟ paḻami yāyiṉum, ataṉip pōl naṭiyeḷimiyum karuttiṉimiyum koṇṭa nūl vēṟu etuvum illi. mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷāka muḻumiyāka nili peṟṟu varuvataṟku ataṉ eḷimiyum iṉimiyum teḷivumē cāṉṟām. nāmiruvar namakkoruvar eṉṟu kuṭumpanalat tiṭṭattil iṉṟu oru toṭar viḷamparap paṭuttukiṉṟōmē; oruvar eṉpataṟkuriya ilakkaṇam aṉṟē tolkāppiyar kūṟiyuḷḷār. tamiḻ pakuttaṟivu moḻi, iyaṟki moḻiyātaliṉ, kāla vīḻcci ataṟkilli. ataṉāṟṟāṉ, eṉṟum uḷa teṉtamiḻ eṉṟu uṇmi kāṭṭiṉār kampar.
kaṟpavartam moḻittaram, cintaṉittaram, neñcut taraṅkaḷi uyarttuvatē eḻuttiṉ payaṉ. cintaṉic celvaṅkaḷ oḷiṟum tolkāppiyak kaṭalāṭuvār ākkavaṟivum ūkka neñcum muṟpōkku nōkkum peṟuvar. etaṟkum oru ciṟu payiṟciyāvatu vēṇṭum. mitivaṇṭi ōṭṭuvatāyiṉum paḻaki viḻuntu, puṇpaṭum payiṟci vēṇṭāmā? kuḻanti naṭappataṟkum, uṇpataṟkum, uṭuttuvaṟkumē evvaḷavu āṇṭukaḷ payilkiṉṟatu. keṭutalkaḷukkuttāṉ payiṟci tēviyilli. kavarcciyāl avi tāmē vantu kavvum. nallatu etaṟkum akkaṟiyum muyaṟciyum kippiṭiyaḷavāvatu vēṇṭum. ātaliṉ tolkāppiyam caṅkavilakkiyam mutalāṉa ivvulaka vāḻvuc cintaṉi nūlkaḷik kaṟkum payilvip peṟṟuk koḷḷuṅkaḷ. ciṉṉāṭ payiṟciyum vāḻvu nalam tarum.
tamiḻ yākki peṟṟa evarukkum tamiḻttāykku mutaṉūlāṉa tolkāppiyak kalvi potuppaṭiyāka vēṇṭum. tamiḻiṉa taṉit tūymi aṟiyavum vaḷarkkavum, nalla kuṭumpam naṭattavum, ulakiyal  oḻuṅku peṟavum, tīmi kuṟintu naṉmi perukavum, cāṉṟōr ākavum vēṇṭumēl, vēṇṭuvatu tolkāppiya aṟivu. ivvaṟivuc celvaṅkaḷit tolkāppiyak kaṭal eṉṟa in nūl vaḻaṅkum.
maṇivācakar patippakam moḻit taramum aṟivut taramum vācakar taramum uyartaṟ poruṭṭu, naṉṉaṭi nūlkaḷiyum āḻntakaṉṟa nūlkaḷiyum vaḻikāṭṭum nūlkaḷiyum varuvāy nōkkiṉṟi veḷiyiṭum tuṇivuṭip patippakam. patippakaṅkaḷ eḻuttāḷariṉ tāyakaṅkaḷ. ātaliṉ eṉ nūlkaḷ maṇivācakar patippakattiṉ karuvaṟiyiliruntu veḷi varuvati naṉṟiyoṭu matikkiṉṟēṉ, vāḻttoṭu makiḻkiṉṟēṉ.
innūṟpatippu niṟi cemmiyōṭu veḷivaruvataṟkup pērutavi ceyta eṉ vakupput tōḻar muṉṉi mutalvar tiru. ci. celvattāṇṭavaṉārkku naṉṟiyaṉ.
		va. cupa. māṇikkam
katirakam, kārikkuṭi
cittiri 2018



tirukkuṟaṭ cuṭar
muṉṉuri
nam tirukkuṟaḷiṉ perumikaḷ palavaṟṟi nām aṟintiruntālum, taliyāṉa uṇmiyāṉa oru perumi maṭṭum nam uḷḷattiṟ patintu vēr viṭavilli. vērōṭu māṟu nam neñcap pāttikaḷi īrappaṭuttikkoḷḷavilli. atutāṉ ceyalvēr.
tirukkuṟaḷ tōṉṟiya nāḷmutal tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷik karuttālum collālum vaḷañceytu varukiṉṟatu. kāppiyaṅkaḷ, ciṟṟilakkiyaṅkaḷ, aruṭpāraṅkaḷ, nītiyilakki yaṅkaḷ, purāṇaṅkaḷ, ciṟukati putiṉa nāṭakaṅkaḷ eṉa vaki yetuvāyiṉum, kuṟaḷ nīr pāyāta ilakkiyakkuḷam illi. urikaḷ, uri vēṟṟumikaḷ, uri vaḷaṅkaḷ, urik kaḷañciyaṅkaḷ, āyvurikaḷ tirukkuṟaṭku amutacurapi pōla mēṉmēlum poṅki varukiṉṟaṉa. moḻi peyarppukkaḷ muḻuvaḷavilum ciṟuvaḷavilum ulakap paṉmoḻikaḷil vanta vaṇṇam uḷa. aṇmiyil eḻuttu vaṭivu peṟṟa curāṭṭira moḻiyilum tirukkuṟaḷ peyarppāyiṟṟu. vaḷḷuvar nūlakam eṉat taṉiperu nūṟkaṭṭiṭam kāṇumaḷa viṟkut tirukkuṟaḷ kuṟitta nūlkaḷum ōviyaṅkaḷum piṟa poruṭkaḷum kuvintu kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. vaḷḷuvar peyar koṇṭa makkaḷ palar, maṉṟaṅkaḷum, kaḻakaṅkaḷum, niṟuvaṉaṅ kaḷum, kōṭṭaṅkaḷum, pōkkuvarattukkaḷum, kūṭṭuṟavuc caṅkaṅkaḷum palap pala.
vaḷḷuvaṉ taṉṉi ulakiṉukkē tantu vāṉ pukaḻ koṇṭu viṭṭōm; ām! avaṉāl nām pukaḻ koṇṭō mēyaṉṟi, nammāl avaṉ pukaḻ koṇṭāṉā? urikaḷ moḻi peyarppukkaḷ kōṭṭaṅkaḷ maṉṟaṅkaḷ ellām puṟap perumikaḷēyaṉṟi nūlōṭu uṭaṉpiṟanta uḷḷīṭāṉa akappaṇpalla. tiruvaḷḷuvar taṉ nūl paṉmoḻiyil piṉṉoru kālattup peyarkkappaṭavēṇṭum eṉṟu etir nalam nōkki eḻutavilli. tirukkuṟaḷiṉ akappayaṉ yātu?

pukaḻntavi pōṟṟic ceyalvēṇṭum; ceyyātu
ikaḻntārk keḻumiyum il
pārāṭṭiya neṟikaḷi vāḻviṟ kaṭippiṭikka vēṇṭum; piṭikkātu viṭṭavarkku enta muṉṉēṟṟamum illi eṉpar vaḷḷuvap perumāṉ. ōr eṭuttukkāṭṭu: nalla maruttuvaṉ ilavacamākac cōtittu ilavacamāka māttirikaḷit tarukiṉṟāṉ. avaṉ takutiyip palariṭam pārāṭṭukiṉṟōm. avaṉ tanta māttirikaḷi maṭṭum uṭkoḷvatilli. ammaruttuvaṉ uṭkoṇṭīrā eṉṟu kaṇṭu kēṭṭāl, ā ā uṭkoṇṭēṉ eṉṟu poyyum colli makiḻkiṉṟōm. namakkum arumit tirukkuṟaṭkum uḷḷa toṭarpu ivvaḷavutāṉ.
viyattuḷ vāḻvāṅku vāḻa vaḻikāṭṭiya tirukkuṟaḷ ilakkiya ātikkam ceytu varukiṉṟatēyaṉṟi vāḻvātik kamō araciyalātikkamō ulakātikkamō ceyyavilli. atu piṟanta nāḷ toṭṭē tamiḻp pulavarkaḷ ilakkiyap payaṉ koḷḷat tavaṟavilli. pulavar kuḻām ituvum ceytirāviṭṭāl, āyvaṟiñar mayili cīṉi vēṅkaṭacāmi eḻutiya ‘maṟintu pōṉa tamiḻ nūlkaḷ’ eṉṟa tokuppil tirukkuṟaḷum oṉṟākiyirukkum. pulavariṉamum uriyiṉamum pōṟṟiya marapāl muppāl uyti peṟṟatu. itu naṉṟikkuriya varalāṟu. itaṉ mēṟ ciṟappu tirukkuṟaḷiṉ ceyalātikkamē. makkaḷ tattam nāḷ vāḻvilum maṉṉarkaḷ tam araciyal naṭappilum tirukkuṟaḷic ceyal vaḻikāṭṭiyākavō cintaṉiyiyakka mākavō koḷḷat tavaṟi viṭṭaṉar. ic ceyal maṟi iṉṟum kaṟṟaṟkum coṟpoḻitaṟkum uriya perumip potumaṟi yākap pārāṭṭappaṭuvataṉṟi, ovvoruvarum viṉippaṭut taṟkut takum vāḻvut taṉi maṟiyāka eṇṇappaṭuvatilli.
tirukkuṟaḷik kaṟkat toṭaṅkiya nāḷtoṭṭu, ceyal nōkkē eṉ mutaṟ pārviyāyiṟṟu. taṉit toṭarpu, maṉpatiyuṟavu, poruḷiyal, aṟiviyal, paṇpiyal, illaṟam eṉap pala vakiyilum poruntiya kuṟaṭkaḷ eṉṉi neṟippaṭuttiyuḷḷaṉa. muppatāṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē ‘vaḷḷuvam’ eṉṉum eṉ nūlil, tirukkuṟaḷiṉ muḻuvamip piyum taṉiyatikārattiyum col toṭar naṭimāṇpu kaḷiyum ceyaṟkaḷattaṉavāka, naṭappiyal aṟaṅkaḷāka valiyuṟutti yuḷḷēṉ. tirukkuṟaṭcuṭar eṉṟa innūlum ataṉ toṭarcciyē. ceyal vāḻ viṉi nōkkatti aḻuntak kāṭṭuvatē tirukkuṟaḷ kuṟitta eṉ eḻuttup payaṉ. oruvar vāḻviṉ naṭappu pala nilippaṭṭatu. ataṟkēṟpa aṟakkūṟu kaḷum palavākum. ātaliṉ kuṟaṭkaḷum pala niliyaṟam kūṟuvaṉa. ippōtu taṉ nili etu eṉṟu eṇṇi annilik kuṟaḷit tērntu viṉiyāḷum pakuttaṟivu vēṇṭum. pakuttaṟiviṉṟip potumiyāka, potu niliyākat tirukkuṟaḷip pārppatu ellārkkum ellā nōykkum oru maruntu colvatu pōlām. attakiya potu maruntu uṇṭā? maruntup paṉmiyē ulakiyal.
tirukkuṟaṭ poruṭpāl valuvāṉa ceyalāṟṟal koṇṭatu. iṉṟiya ñālavaraciyalukkum, ciṟappāka nam intiyavaraciyalukkum aracu vaḷḷuvaṅkaḷ iṉṟum uyirp puṭiyaṉa. ātaliṉ intiya vaḻikāṭṭi, tēciya maṟi tirukkuṟaḷ, nikaḻkālap pārvi eṉṟa mūṉṟu kaṭṭurikaḷum pāratattiṉ valimiyum vaḷarcciyum matippum nōkki eḻutap peṟṟavi. tirukkuṟaṭ cuṭaril ikkaṭṭurikaḷ muccuṭarākum. araciṉ naṭappōṭṭaṅkaḷ nāṭṭaṉpōṭu ivaṟṟil tiṟaṉāyappaṭṭuḷa. nam pāratam valnallaracāka vēṇṭum eṉṟu viḻiyāta intiyar uṇṭō?
eṉ naṭi paṟṟi oru kuṟippu: eṉ eḻuttukkaḷ kaṭṭaḷic coṟkaḷāl kaṭṭaḷi naṭiyāl amintavi. coṟ palkutal eṉṟa mikikku iṭamillātavi; ceṟivu mikuntavi; vēṇṭuṅkāl putiya collākkaṅkaḷum putiya col vaṭivukaḷum putiya toṭarāṭcikaḷum uṭiyavi. nīṇṭa centamiḻukku etaṉiyum niṟivākat tūymiyākac colla valla taṟkiḻamittiṟam uṇṭu; ammoḻit tiṟattip payaṉpaṭuttum payil tiṟam eḻuttāṇmiyarkku vēṇṭum; tūya centamiḻāl oruvar eḻutavilliyeṉiṉ, atu tamiḻk kuṟipāṭaṉṟu; teḷivum uṟutiyum payilvum illā eḻutukiyar kuṟipāṭu eṉṟu uṇarvatē meyyuṇarvu.
ilakkiya viḷakkam, entac cilampu, oppiyal nōkku, tolkāppiyat tiṟaṉ, cintaṉik kaḷaṅkaḷ eṉṟa pala talippuṭi nūlkaḷil iṭimaṭuttuk kiṭanta tirukkuṟaṭ kaṭṭurikaḷum iraṇṭu putiya kaṭṭurikaḷum tirukkuṟaṭ cuṭar eṉṉum putup peyar koṇṭa innūliṉkaṇ oru poruḷ māliyākat toki peṟṟuḷa. eṉ muṉṉik kaṭṭuri ellām oru poruḷ nutaliyaṉavākap putiya tokippāṭu peṟavēṇṭum eṉṟu tiru.ca.meyyappaṉ viḻintār. uyarvuḷḷalum uḷḷiyapaṭi ceytalum ākiya aruntiṟal vāynta meyyappaṉāriṉ karuttiṉi varavēṟṟēṉ. ataṉpaṭi murukaṉiya āṟu putu nūlkaḷ ippōtu veḷivarukiṉṟaṉa. maṇivācakar patippakattārkku eṉ vāḻttum patippuc cemmal meyyappaṉārkku eṉ naṉṟiyum uriya.
	va.cupa.māṇikkam
katirakam, kārikkuṭi                                            
vikāci 2018


caṅkaneṟi
muṉṉuri
pērāciriyar aṟiñar irāmāṉucaṉār ai. nā. ātaravil veḷiyiṭṭa ‘Poems of Love and war’ eṉṟa nūliṉkaṇ, caṅkap pāṭalkaḷ palavaṟṟit tērnteṭuttup patiṉintāṇṭu uḻittu āṅkilak kaviyākap paṭittuḷḷār. innūl ulaka varavēṟpukku uriyatāka viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. caṅkat tamiḻ ilakkiyatti aṟiyātār intiya nākarikattiṉ taṉic ciṟappuṭiya ilakkiya niṟivi aṟiyātavar eṉavum, ivi camayañ cārāta ilakkiyat tokikaḷ ātalāl camayac cārpu mikka piṟkālattār ivaṟṟik kallātu otukki viṭṭaṉar eṉavum, uṇmi kūṟappukiṉ, caṅkap paṉuvalkaḷ kālatti veṉṟu maṉṉiya cevvilakkiyam eṉavum pērāciriyar irāmāṉucar āṅkilattil vallamiyākak kūṟiyiruppatu intiyak kaṇkaḷiyum ulakak kaṇkaḷiyum viḻikkac ceyyum eṉṟu etirpārkkiṉṟēṉ.
tamiḻk kātal kuṟitta eṉ āṅkila nūlilum tamiḻ nūlilum, pērāciriyar cōtimuttu marapu kuṉṟāmal āṅkilattil moḻi peyartta aiṅkuṟunūṟṟiṉ eṉ aṇinturi yilum caṅkavilakkiyam ulakamāṭci koṇṭatu eṉṟa kuṟikkōḷip pulappaṭuttiyuḷḷēṉ. ivvilakkiyam kālattāṟ paḻamiyē yaṉṟi, muṉṉēṟṟak karuttukkaḷāl, kāliyil malarum nāṇmalar pōṉṟatu. tamiḻ nākarikam eṉpatu ivvulakak kaḷam kaṇṇiyatu; paciyum piṇiyum pakiyum nīṅki vāḻum nalappāṭu koṇṭatu; viyattuḷ vāḻvāṅku vāḻum uṟuti kāṭṭuvatu; cātikkaṭṭu camayakkaṭṭu matakkaṭṭu kaṭcikkaṭṭu piṟappukkaṭṭu eṉṟiṉiya ikkaṭṭukkaḷ pukāta makkaṭ camaṉmi paṟṟiyatu; poruḷā tārattiṟ kūṭak koṭiyāl celvaccamam kāṇpatu; ceṅkōl āṭciyil pulamic cāṉṟōrkaḷiṉ paṅku taḻuviyatu; āḷvāric cevi kippa iṭitturittut tiruttum aṟivuṭiyatu.
iṅṅaṉam naṟkūṟupala potinta tamiḻ nākarika māṇpiṉic caṅkavilakkiyaṅkaḷ kaṇṇāṭi pōlak kāṭṭi niṟkiṉṟaṉa. ivi iṉṟiya intiyak kuṭiyaraciṉ otturimik kōṭpāṭṭiṟku ottuvarum pētamilā ilakkiyaṅkaḷ ākum. cātiyaṭikaḷō camaya veṟikaḷō iṉak kalavaramō illāta nallulakatti ippaṉuvalkaḷ pōṟṟukiṉṟaṉa. kātalum vīramumē ivaṟṟiṉ pāṭu poruḷkaḷ eṉpatu muḻuvuṇmiyaṉṟu. ivaṟṟip pāṭuṅkāl karupporu ḷākavum ceytip poruḷākavum pala tuṟiyaṟivukaḷ kaṭṭumāṉa mākiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē caṅkavilakkiyaṅkaḷ tamiḻt tēciyavilakkiyaṅkaḷ eṉavum pāratattiṉ ivvulaka nākarika maṟikaḷ eṉavum ulakiṉ makkaṭ paṇpilakkiyaṅkaḷ eṉavum karutaṟkuriyavi.
caṅkappaṉuvalkaḷ viḻumiya paṇpiṉāl ulakam taḻuviyavi eṉiṉum moḻiyāl tamiḻiṉattukku nerukkamāṉavi. ivvakiyil tamiḻarkaḷ naṟpēṟṟiṉar. moḻiyāl naṭiyāl kaṭiṉamāṉavi. viḷaṅkātavi eṉṟa mikat tavaṟāṉa mayakkam oru cārāriṭi eppaṭiyō paravik kiṭakkiṉṟatu. ettuṇiyō aṟivil mayakkaṅkaḷuḷ ituvum oṉṟu. kaṭiṉamāṉa vēṟṟu moḻic coṟkaḷ niṟinta ciṟukati putiṉaṅkaḷ eṅkaṭkup purikiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu pakarum ūṉat tamiḻmakkaḷ tūya tamiḻc coṟkaḷāl ākiya nam caṅkap pāṭalkaḷ puriyavilli eṉpatu putirākat tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. enta moḻi varalāṟṟilum illātapaṭi, nāṉūṟu pulavarkaḷ makkaḷ nalam nōkkik kaṭṭuk kōppākap pāṭiya ceyyuṭkaḷē caṅkavilakkiyam ām. ivaṟṟit taṇṇeṉṟa oḻukkam uṭiyaṉa eṉavum ippulavarkaḷic cāṉṟōr eṉavum kampar kāppiyap pārāṭṭuc ceyvar. ‘nīṇṭa tamiḻ’ eṉavum ‘eṉṟumuḷa teṉṟamiḻ’ eṉavum kampar pōṟṟip pukaḻvataṟkuc caṅkavilakkiyac celvamē kāraṇamām.
innūlkaḷ paḻamippaṭṭaṉa, viḷaṅkā eṉṟu colvōri nampavēṇṭā. kuṟuntoki eṉṟa nūl 400 ceyyuṭkaḷ, 2500 aṭikaḷ koṇṭatu. accu vaṭivil 100 pakkam varum. akkaṟiyōṭu oru tiṅkaḷ mummuṟi toṭarntu paṭittāl, kuḷirnīr, iḷanīr pōla eḷimiyum iṉcuviyum tōṉṟuvatōṭu, perumitamum cemmāppum uyarum. tamiḻppiṟappu eṭutta tamiḻarkaḷ taṅkaḷ vāḻnāḷil orutiṅkaḷ caṅkavilakkiyak kalvikkup payaṉpaṭuttak kūṭātā? payaṉpaṭutti ariya cintaṉik kaḷañciyattit tēṭik koḷḷak kūṭātā? kuṟuntoki oṉṟōṭu uṟavu koṇṭālē, caṅkat tamiḻ nākarikam nammōṭu paṭintuviṭum. maṉimāṭci yillāviṭṭāl eṉimāṭciyum illi eṉṟu tirukkuṟaḷ kūṟuvatu pōla, caṅkak kalvi peṟāviṭṭāl tamiḻaṉukku ēṉik kalviyāl iṉap perumiyilli eṉṟu kūṟuvēṉ.
tamiḻkkātal eṉṉum eṉ āyvuṭi nūliṉkaṇ akattiṇi neṟikaḷ virivākavum nuṇukkamākavum collap paṭṭuḷa. caṅka neṟi eṉṟa innūliṉkaṇ perumpālum puṟattiṇi paṟṟiya kaṭṭurikaḷ uḷa. puṟat toki nūlkaḷuḷ patiṟṟup pattu pala vakiyālum taṉittaṉmi koṇṭa tākaliṉ, ataṉ peṟṟikaḷ aintu kaṭṭuriyāka vaḷarntaṉa. puṟanāṉūṟu paṟṟiyum varampāṉa cila putu āyvukaḷik kāṇalām. tamiḻ vāṉmīkiyār, paṟampuppōr vañcaṉiyā? muttoki vāḻttukkaḷ eṉpaṉa āyvuk kiḷarccik koṇṭavi.
marapiṉ vanta eṭṭut toki pattuppāṭṭukkaḷ perumpālum aṭiyaḷavāl koṇṭa tokikaḷ ākum. camaracam aritiṉ muyaṉṟu patippitta caṅkavilakkiyam āciriya muṟiyāl aminta tokuppākum. ip patippukkaḷ cilavakiyāyvukkup payaṉkaḷamāyiṉa. kāla muṟippatippu eṉa oṉṟu vantāl, āyvuk kaḷaṅkaḷ iṉṉum palkum; muṭivukaḷum uṟutiyākum. ippaṇi etirkālat tamiḻākkap paṇikaḷuḷ mutaṉmi peṟum eṉa eṇṇukiṟēṉ.
ilakkiya viḷakkam, tolkāppiyap putumi, tolkāppiyat tiṟaṉ, entac cilampu, cintaṉik kaḷaṅkaḷ eṉṟa eṉ muṉṉi urinaṭi nūlkaḷil paravik kiṭanta caṅkavilakkiyam paṟṟiya kaṭṭurikaḷ caṅka neṟi eṉṟa ipputut talippuṭi nūliṉkaṇ oruṅku kuṭikoṇṭuḷa. oru poruṭpaṭṭa eṉ kaṭṭuriyellām iṇintu oru nūṟpatippātal nallatu eṉṟu tiru ca. meyyappaṉār virumpiya vaṇṇam, tolkāppiyak kaṭal, tirukkuṟaṭ cuṭar, caṅka neṟi, kāppiyap pārvi, ilakkiyaccāṟu, tamiḻkkatir eṉṟāṅku  eṉ kaṭṭurikaḷ āṟu vaṇṇam peṟṟup putuppolivu eytukiṉṟaṉa. eṉ eḻuttukkaḷi putiya vaṭivākki uyartaramākap patippikkum maṇivācakar patippakattiṟkum patippuc cemmal tiru. ca. meyyappa ṉārkkum uḷamārnta naṉṟik kaṭappāṭuṭiyēṉ.
	va. cupa. māṇikkam
katirakam, kārikkuṭi
vikāci (2018)
tolkāppiyattiṟaṉ 
muṉṉuri
tolkāppiyam tamiḻukkum tamiḻiṉattukkum mutaṉūl; iṉṟu tamiḻāyvukkum muntunūlāka viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. urivaḷaṅkaḷ palkukiṉṟaṉa. paḻiya putiya tuṟikaṭkellām tolkāppiya vēr kāṇat tuṭikkiṉṟōm. intiyap perunāṭu paṉṉūṟāṇṭu aṭimip paṭuviruḷiṟ kiṭantu innūṟṟāṇṭil viṭutalip puttoḷi peṟṟatupōla, ayaliruḷ paṭinta tol kāppiyamum iññāṉṟu āyvoḷi katirttu ilaṅkukiṉṟatu.
kālap paḻamiyāṉa tolkāppiyattiṉ poruṭ karukkaḷik kaṇṭu teḷitaṟku iṭiyaṟā āyviṉikaḷ vēṇṭum; putiya neṟiyaṅkaḷi vakuttuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum; itukāṟum maṇṭik kiṭakkum karutturikaḷi kaṭṭaḷikka vēṇṭum. tolkāppiyat tiṟaṉ eṉṟa innūlil āṟu kaṭṭurikaḷ tolkāppiyam paṟṟiyaṉa. ivaṟṟil putiya neṟikkōṭukaḷiyum maṟuttu niṟuviya muṭipukaḷiyum cila nūṟpāviṉa teḷivukaḷiyum aṟiyalām. ēṉik kaṭṭurikaḷ piṟa poruḷavāyiṉum āṅkāṅku puḷḷi nīḻalpōlat tolkāppiyac cāyal uṇṭu.
tolkāppiyap putumi eṉṟa eṉ muṉṉiya nūl pōlavē innūlum tamiḻk karuvūlamām tolkāppiyap perumiyi uṇarttum eṉṟu nampukiṉṟēṉ.
ākkamum āḻamum iṇinta nūlkaḷi veḷiyiṭum maṇivācakar patippakam aṉput toṭarccipōla eṉ nūlkaḷip patipput toṭarcciyāka veḷiyiṭṭu varukiṉṟatu. ittoṭarpukku tiru. meyyappaṉ eṉpālum eṉ eḻuttiṉ mēlum koṇṭirukkum īṭupāṭē kāraṇamutalākum. nūlakam parantōṅkic ceḻittu vaḷarka eṉṟu naṉṟiyoṭu vāḻttuvaṉ.
va. cupa. māṇikkam
katirakam, kārikkuṭi,15.3.1984
kāppiyap pārvi
muṉṉuri
ilakkiya vakikaḷuḷ mikapperiya parappuṭiyatu kāppiyam ākum. kati nīḷam, pāttirappaṉmi, puṉivuccūḻal ivaṟṟāl kāppiyattukkut taṉineṟikaḷ palavākiṉṟaṉa; eṉiṉum ilakkiyam eṉṟa poruṇmiyil vakivaṭivu etuvāyiṉum potuvuttikaḷum uḷa. ātaliṉ potuvuttikaḷ ciṟapputtikaḷ eṉṟa irupāṅkiṉiyum ovvōr ilakkiyattum pakuttaṟintu kāṇpatu tiṟaṉiyiṉ kaṭaṉākum.
nilakkāppiyam, taḻuvu kāppiyam eṉap perumpōkkākak kāppiyatti irupāṟ paṭuttalām. cilappatikāram maṇimēkali periyapurāṇam ūṉṟukōl eṉpaṉa nilakkāppiyaṅkaḷ ākum. kāntippurāṇamum nilakkāppiyamē. taḻuvu kāppiyaṅkaḷē mikutiyākap perukum. taḻuval eṉpataṉāl ivi kuṟimatippiṉa eṉpatu karuttaṉṟu. taḻuval eṉpatu nuṇukkamāṉa virivuṭiyatu; mūlamoḻi, peyarppu moḻiyeṉa irumoḻic cārpu kāṭṭuvatu. iru moḻiyum irumoḻikkuriya iṉamakkaḷiṉ paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷum ammoḻip pulavarkaḷiṉ pulamiyāṟṟalkaḷum taḻuvu kāppiyattil orumippāṭu uṟukiṉṟaṉa. ituvē ‘moḻi peyarttu ataṟpaṭa yāttal’ eṉṟa tolkāppiyattiṉ kuṟippākum.
uṉperun tēviyeṉṉum urimikkum uṉṉippeṟṟa
maṉperu marukiyeṉṉum vāymikkum mitilimaṉṉaṉ
taṉperun taṉayiyeṉṉum takimikkum talimicāṉṟāṉ
eṉṟa anumaṉ kūṟṟil taḻuvu kāppiyattiṉ oruneṟi oḷintu kiṭakkiṉṟatu. ōr naṅki tāṉ makaḷākap piṟanta kulattiṟkum marumakaḷākap pukunta kulattiṟkum ceyyum nalluṟaviyum kāṭṭum naṉmatippiyum taḻuvu kāppiyaṅkaḷ kaṭippiṭikkiṉṟaṉa. cīvakacintāmaṇi, peruṅkati, cūḷāmaṇi, pāratam, irāmā yaṇam, tēmpāvaṇi, cīṟāppurāṇam mutaliyaṉa iru piñ ñilattukkum ēṟpap pataṅkoṇṭa taḻuvu kāppiyaṅkaḷām.
kāppiyap pārvi eṉṟa innūliṉkaṇ iruvakit tamiḻk kāppiyaṅkaḷum pārvi ceyyappaṭṭuḷa. eṉṟālum cilappatikāram, maṇimēkali irāmāyaṇam mūṉṟum perum paṅkukoḷkiṉṟaṉa. innūl muṉpu veḷiyiṭṭa kāppiyak kaṭṭurikaḷiṉ ōraṇit tokuppātaliṉ ivvāṟu amiyalāyiṉa. kaṭṭuri ovvoṉṟum kāppiyattiṉ oruvakip pārviyip pulappaṭuttum. cilappatikārat tiṟaṉ, maṇimēkalit tiṟaṉ eṉṟa irupakutikaḷilum palvēṟu tiṟaṉeṟikaḷik kāṇalām. entac cilampu, mātaviyiṉ maṟuvōli, cilappatikāram muṟṟum eṉṟa mūṉṟu āyvukaḷum paḻiya taṭattiliruntu māṟupaṭṭavi.
kampar eṉap peyariya eṉ poḻivut taṉi nūlil kāppiyap pārvi, kāppiyak kaḷam, kāppiya nērmi eṉṟa muppāl neṟikaḷ paṭṭāṅku viḷakkam peṟṟuḷa. ‘kampaṉ iṟutiyaṭikaḷ’ eṉṟa putiya kaṭṭuri kāppiyattiṉ yāppuneṟiyi namakkuk kaṟpikkiṉṟatu.
kāviyap pēccu, ilakkiya viḷakkam, ilakkiyakkali, ilakkiyattaḷam eṉṟa kaṭṭurikaḷ paṭippilakkiyarkku vaḻikāṭṭum eṉa etirpārkkiṉṟēṉ. iruvakic cilappatikāram, miruccakaṭikamum cilappatikāramum eṉṟa kaṭṭurikaḷ oppāyattiṉ pālaṉa.
eṉ palvēṟu urinaṭi nūlkaḷiṟ pirintu kiṭanta kāppiyak kaṭṭurikaḷ iṉam iṉattōṭu cērvatutāṉ aḻaku eṉṟa vaṉappukkēṟpa, ‘kāppiyap pārvi’ eṉṟa putiya nūṟṟalippil oṉṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ittokiyāl kāppiya neṟikaḷ palavum oru cērap pulaṉākum kaṟpavarkku. iṅṅaṉam oru poruḷ kuṟitta kaṭṭurikaḷiyellām oru nūṟṟoki yākkuvatu nallatu eṉṟa karuttiyaḷittavar patippuccemmal tiru.meyyappaṉār. tamiḻvaḷarcciyil putumi nōkkuṭiya meyyappaṉārkkum maṇivācakar patippakattiṟkum eṉ cevvāḻttu palappala.
va.cupa. māṇikkam
katirakam,  kārikkuṭi


entac cilampu
mukavuri
ilakkiya vaḷarcciyil taṉik kaṭṭurikaḷukku oru naṉmatippu uṇṭu. avi poruḷāl vēṟupaṭutaliṉ kaṟpavartam aṟivi vaḷarkkiṉṟaṉa; kālattāl vēṟu paṭutaliṉ kaṭṭuriyāḷaṉ taṉ naṭi vaḷarvik kāṭṭu kiṉṟaṉa. pirintu kiṭanta kaṭṭurikaḷ oṉṟukūṭa nuṟpuṇarcci peṟumpōtu, āciriyaṉ nilipēṟāṉa iṉpam eytu kiṉṟāṉ.
entac cilampu mutalāṉa paṉṉiru kaṭṭurikaḷ koṇṭatu. ittokuti oṉpatu, pattu patiṉoṉṟueṉṟu mūṉṟum vāṉolittavi. piṟa pala malarkaḷil veḷi yāṉavi.
tirukkuṟaḷi ilakkiya nūlākavē matippatiṉum nāṭṭukku nalvaḻi kāṭṭum iyakka nūlāka matikkavēṇṭum. ilakkiya pārvi tirukkuṟaḷiṉ uyirppi orukkālum veḷippaṭuttātu. naṭappup pārviyē ataṉ nāṭippārvi. ikkaṭṭuriyil viḷakkappaṭum araciyaṟ karuttukkaḷil vēṟupaṭuvār paṭuka. intiya vaḻikāṭṭi eṉṉum kaṭṭuri tirukkuṟaḷi eṅṅaṉam kaṟka vēṇṭum eṉpataṟku oru vaḻikāṭṭi; attuṇiyē.
		     va.cupa. māṇikkam
kārikkuṭi, katirakam
1-12-1964.


ilakkiyaccāṟu 
muṉṉuri
patiṉeṇkaṭṭurikaḷ koṇṭa innūl `ilakkiyac cāṟu’  eṉṟa iṉiya putiya peyart talippiṉip peṟukiṉṟatu. itu mikap putiya toṭaraṉṟu. karuppañcāṟu eṉṟa vaḻakkiṉi nām palakāl kēṭṭirukkiṉṟōm. paḻac cāṟṟukkaṭikaḷ eṅkum peruki varum kālam itu.
cāṟu eṉpataṟkuc ceṟivu eṉpatu poruḷ. `cāṟayarkaḷam’ eṉavum `cāṟutalik koṇṭeṉa’ eṉavum `vēṟu vēṟu kaṭavuḷar cāṟu ciṟantorupāl’ eṉavum viḻā eṉṟa poruḷil iccol caṅka kālattup peruvaḻakkāka iruntatu. makkaḷ ceṟivākak kūṭiya nerukkam eṉpatu itaṉ karuttu.
cāṟu eṉpataṟkuc cattiṉ piḻivu eṉṟa poruḷum uṇṭu. `āliccāṟu pāy ōti’ eṉpar kampar. `tiruntu cāṟu aṭuvuḻip piṟanta tīmpuki’ eṉpar tiruttakka tēvar.
arumpeṟal yākkiyip peṟṟa payattāṟ
perumpayaṉum āṟṟavē koḷka - karumpūrnta
cāṟupōṟ cālavum payaṉutavi maṟṟataṉ
kōtupōṟ pōkum uṭampu
eṉṟa nālaṭiyil karumpiṉ piḻivu cāṟeṉavum ataṉ cakki kōteṉavum kuṟikkappaṭutal kāṇka. `naṟavup piḻintiṭṭa kōtuṭic citaṟal’ eṉṟa puṟanāṉūṟum niṉiyat takum. eṉavē ilakkiyac cāṟu eṉṟa  coṟṟoṭarukku ilakkiyap piḻivu eṉpatu karuttām.
irumpaṉiyiṉ kurumpi nīrum
pūṅkarumpiṉ tīñcāṟum
ōṅkumaṇaṟ kuvavut tāḻi
tīnīrō ṭuṭaṉvirāay
munnīruṇṭu munnīrp pāyum
tāṅkā vuṟiyuḷ nallūr

eṉṟa māṅkuṭikiḻār puṟanāṉūṟṟuc ceyyuḷāl, paṉinuṅkiṉ nīr, karumpiṉ tēṟal, teṉṉi iḷanīr mūṉṟum viraviya kalavic cāṟu kuṭitta paḻakkam paṇṭik kālattu iruntati aṟiyalām. taṉip paḻaccāṟṟōṭu palvakip paḻaṅkaḷ piḻinta kalappuccāṟum iṉṟu kaṭikaḷil kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa.
ilakkiyaccāṟu eṉṟa innūl pala ilakkiyaṅkaḷiṉ piḻivākum. tiruvācakam, tirukkōvi, kōviyilakkiyam, tiruppāvi, tiruvempāvi, tippāvikaḷ, pala ciṟṟilakkiyaṅkaḷ, tāyumāṉavar kaviti, kāntik kaviti, pāratikaviti eṉṟa ilakkiya vakikaḷi ovvōr kūṟupaṟṟi vaṭitteṭutta karuttuc cāṟukaḷi ittokuppiṟ kāṇalām. iṭinūṟṟāṇṭu ilakkiyaṅkaḷum innūṟṟāṇṭu ilakkiyaṅkaḷum marapuccuṭar kuṉṟāmal puttoḷi nantāmal tiṟappāṭu ceyyappaṭṭuḷa.
taṟciṟumi, tēvarkaḷ paṭumpāṭu, kāntikkaviti, pāratīyam eṉṟiṉiya kaṭṭuric cāṟukaḷ aṟivu maṟumalarcciyip paṇpaṭuttum eṉṟu nampukiṉṟēṉ. paṭippilakkiyap pāṅku eṉṟa mutaṟcāṟu kavarcci karutip peṇṇiṉattiṉ perumi mācu paṭakkūṭātu eṉṟa nōkkil piḻiyap peṟṟatu. tamiḻc collāṭciyum tamiḻmiyākap pōṟṟappaṭavēṇṭum eṉavum tamiḻāṟṟali ayalmācu paṭuttalākātu eṉavum ikkaṭṭuri teḷivupaṭuttukiṉṟatu. putiṉam, ciṟukati, nāṭakam paṭikkum putteḻuttāḷarkaḷ poruṇmiyilum coṉmiyilum makkaḷ nalaṅkaḷiyē nayattakka nākarikac cālpōṭu kaṭippiṭikkavēṇṭum eṉpatum, taṅkaḷ maṟupatippukkaḷi moḻit tūymippaṭutta vēṇṭum eṉpatum ikkaṭṭuriyiṉ cāṟākum. paḻamikkuḷ mūḻkiṉālum putumimēl nīntiṉālum, nāṭṭiṉ etirkāla muṉaṉēṟṟamum nikaḻkāla nalvāḻvumē eṉ eḻuttukkaḷiṉ uḷḷoḷi. eṉ ēṉivaki nūlkaḷilum ivvoḷikkatir piṟaṅkak kāṇalām.
eṉ nūlkaḷi varicip patippākkit tamiḻmakkaṭku vaḻaṅkavēṇṭum eṉṟu tiṭṭappaṭuttiṉār patippuc cemmal meyyappaṉār. avar eṇṇiyāṅku paṭṭut tukilpōlak kuṟukiya kālattil eṉpaṉuvalkaḷ acciḻi peṟṟuk kaṇkavar vaṉappōṭu vantu koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. taṉ kuḻanti varici variciyākap paricu vāṅkumpōtu makiḻum tāyuṇarvi nāṉ peṟukiṉṟēṉ. maṇivācakar patippakattārkku eṉ vāḻttum naṉṟiyum uriya.
eṉ vakupput tōḻar muṉṉi mutalvar tiru. ci. celvattāṇṭavaṉāriṉ aṉput toṇṭiṉāl eṉ patippukkaḷ piḻiyūṉam peṟavilli. avarkku eṉ naṉṟikkaṭaṉ marapāyiṟṟu.
	va.cupa. māṇikkam
katirakam, kārikkuṭi
āvaṇi 2018


tolkāppiyap putumi
mukavuri
tolkāppiyam tamiḻnāṭṭukku mutaṉūl, tamiḻmoḻikku mutaṉūl, tamiḻarkku mutaṉūl. in nūlaṟivu ettuṟit tamiḻaṉukkum vēṇṭum. tolkāppiya aṟivuṭiya tamiḻaṉ eṇṇattilum vāḻvilum ēṟṟamāka viḷaṅkuvāṉ. palamoḻik kalappuṭiya iṉṟit tamiḻarkkut tamiḻmoḻi mūvaracāṇṭa  naṉṉāḷil tōṉṟiya tolkāppiyam eṭuttavuṭaṉ puriyātutāṉ. āyiṉum tolkāppiyam viḷaṅkum naṟṟamiḻarkaḷāka nām uyara vēṇṭāmā? uyarntu tamiḻi mēlum uyartta vēṇṭāmā?
tolkāppiyap putumi eṉpatu innūliṉ peyar. tolkāppiya nūl kālattāl mikap paḻamiyāka irukkalām. kālap paḻamikkākak karuttum paḻami eṉṟu mayaṅki eṭuttu eṟintu ikaḻntu viṭakkūṭātu eṉṟu viḻippūṭṭavē `tolkāppiyap putumi’ eṉṟa peyari innūṟku vaḻaṅkiṉēṉ.
tolkāppiya niḻalpaṭāta tamiḻ nūlkaḷ illi. iṉṟu piṟakkum nūlkaḷuṅkūṭa inniḻal paṭintaṉavē. iṉṟiya tamiḻmoḻi vaḻakkiṉic ciṟitu nōkkiṉum, oliyum toṭarum piṟa amippum tolkāppiya neṟippaṭṭaṉavē eṉpatu teḷivu. parantu vaḷarum moḻiyiyal ivvuṇmiyi meyppikkiṉṟatu. kātalulakilum kaṟpulakilum tolkāppiya vuḷḷamē tamiḻmakkaḷiṉ uḷḷamāka iṉṟum vāḻkiṉṟatu. eṉavē nam parampari cemmiyāṉatu, vaḻi neṟi varuvatu, kālam poruntiyatu, cālac ciṟantatu eṉṟu ovvoru tamiḻaṉum cemmāppuk koḷka.
itaṉ muṉṉip patippukkaḷ 14 kaṭṭuri koṇṭavi. ippatippil caṅka neṟikaḷ, tolkāppiya neṟikaḷ eṉṟa irupoḻivukaḷ mēlum kūṭiyuḷḷaṉa. nūliṉ peyarukkut taka 16 kaṭṭurikaḷilum tolkāppiya nīrōṭṭattip parukalām.
kaltōṉṟi maṇtōṉṟāk kālattē mūtta maṉitakkuṭi eṉap pukaḻappaṭum tamiḻiṉam mūttum mūvāt tolkāppiyat tamiḻmaṟiyik kimaṇakka vāymaṇakka vāḻvumaṇakkak kaṟka muṉvaruka.
	va.cupa. māṇikkam
kārikkuṭi
1-3-80


ilakkiya viḷakkam
muṉṉuri
ilakkiya viḷakkam eṉṟa innūl patiṉāṉku kaṭṭurikaḷ koṇṭatu. cempākam ēḻu kaṭṭurikaḷ tirukkuṟaḷ paṟṟiyavi. naṭimuṟiyum ceyalum teḷivum nōkki eḻutappaṭṭavi. tirukkuṟaḷum ilakkiyam ātaliṉ ivvēḻiṉiyum ilakkiya viḷakkamākavē karutalām.
ēṉik kaṭṭurikaḷ puṟam, akam, kāppiyam, ciṟṟilakkiyam, nītiyilakkiyam eṉṟa nūl vakikaḷi ovvoru kūṟupaṟṟi ārāypavi. ivaṟṟil cila ilakkiyap pāṅkukaḷ putuviḷakkam peṟṟuḷa. nītiviḷakkam eṉṟa iṟutikkaṭṭuri `ciṟiyōr ellām ciṟiyarum allar, eṉṟapaṭi ciṟu nūlkaḷum ilakkiyattiṟaṉukku uriya āyvut taḷaṅkaḷām eṉpati meyppikkum. ikkaṭṭurikkaṇ ēḻiḷantamiḻ eṉṟa putut tokinūl paṭikkappaṭṭiruppati niṉivukoḷḷa vēṇṭukiṉṟēṉ.
ikkaṭṭuriyiṟ cila vāṉoliyil eḻuntavi cila malaril malarntavi. vevvēṟu kālattil eḻutappaṭṭavi eṉiṉum oru nūl vaṭivam peṟum ippōtu vēṇṭum oḻuṅku ceytu veḷiyiṭukiṉṟēṉ. vevvēṟu āṇṭiṟ piṟanta makkaḷi oruṅku kāṇum kuṭumpak kāṭciyiṉ iṉpam innūṟ kāṭciyilum tōṉṟukiṉṟatu.
kaṭṭurit taṉimalarkaḷi nūṉmāliyākap patippikka aṉpōṭu ēṟṟukkoṇṭa citamparam maṇivācaka nūlakattārkku naṉṟiyaṉ.
	va.cupa.māṇikkam
kārikkuṭi.,, 1-12-1971 


cintaṉik kaḷaṅkaḷ 
muṉṉuri
cintaṉik kaḷattiṟ pukumuṉ
ellā iṭattum nīr ūṟumā? ellāp pakutiyilum pāl curakkumā? ilakkiyattilum ilakkaṇattilum varalāṟṟilum camayattilum ellā iṭaṅkaḷum āyvākumā? nīr eṅkum ūṟum; pāl eṅkum curakkum; āyvu eṅkum nikaḻum eṉpatu aṟiviṉ mutaṟkōṇal ākum. maṇṇaṟiñaṉ nīr uḷḷa iṭam kāṇpatu pōlavum, maruttuvaṉ uṭalil valiyuḷḷa iṭam kāṇpatu pōlavum, āyvāḷaṉ ilakkiyam mutalāṉa ettuṟik kaṇṇum āyvukku uriya iṭaṅkaḷi aṟivu matukiyāl kaṇṭu koḷḷa vēṇṭum.
ciṟappiṭaṅkaḷē cintaṉik kaḷaṅkaḷ. oru nūli, oru tuṟiyip potuvākak kaṟṟukkoṇṭu celluṅkāli, kālukku mēṭu paḷḷam tōṉṟuvatu pōla, aṟivukkum karuttōṭṭattil, naṭippōkkil, amippu muṟiyil, muṭivu niliyil muṭṭumuraṇkaḷum etirkutirkaḷum pulappaṭum. ivviṭaṅkaḷic curaṅkaṅkaḷāka matittu, maṇ mēṭiṭṭa patukkalkaḷit tolliyalār tōṇṭik kāṇpatu oppa. āyvāḷar aṟivi niṟuttit tīṭṭi āyviṉi ceyya vēṇṭum. paṭittuk koṇṭē cellum aṟiviṟku akalam ēṟpaṭum; niṉṟu ucāvi nuṉittut tiṟam kāṇum aṟiviṟku āḻam ēṟpaṭum. niṉṟu tuḷikkum aṟivē, pāratiyār colliyāṅku, āḻntu niṟkum kaviyuḷattip pārkka muṭiyum. itaṟku orē vaḻi ilakkiyamāyiṉum vēṟu ettuṟiyāyiṉum cintaṉi eḻuppum cilavāya kaḷaṅkaḷit tērntu mēṟkoḷvatu. kaḷattērvu ceyyātu ceyyum muyaṟci yellām kivarunta umik kuttuvatākum.
innūlil 37 cintaṉik kaḷaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ivi oru tuṟikkaḷam alla. tolkāppiyakkaḷam, caṅka ilakkiyak kaḷam, kāppiyakkaḷam, urikkaḷam, camayakkaḷam, varalāṟṟuk kaḷam eṉap palatiṟattavi. ovvoru tuṟiyilum muṭṭiya muraṇiya mōtiya pōliya meliya valiya pakutikaḷi āyvut tiṭṭukkaḷākak kōlikkoṇṭu yāṉ kaṇṭa muṭipukaḷi avi vaḻivaḻik karuttukku māṟu eṉiṉum kāraṇampaṭak kāṭṭiyuḷḷēṉ. eṉ cintaṉik kaḷaṅkaḷ nuṇṇiyōrkkuc cintaṉik kuḷaṅkaḷāka irukkum eṉṟu etirpārkkiṉṟēṉ.
taṉakku muṉtōṉṟiya nūlkaḷum uṭaṉ tōṉṟiya nūlkaḷum ēṉ, piṉ tōṉṟiya nūlkaḷum, pala peyarc cuvaṭum teriyāmal maṟintoḻiya, tolkāppiyam oṉṟu maṭṭum kālapperuṅ kaṭalik kaṭantu nīnti nilittiruppak kāraṇam yātu? ellāt tokikaḷilum tiṇivarici nūlukku oṉṟāka māṟāka irukkum pōtu ‘colliyamuṟi’ eṉṟa tolkāppiyat toṭarukkut tiṇivarici muṟi eṉṟu uri kaṇṭatu poruntuṅkol? mayakkam eṉṟa kiḷavikkup puṇarcci eṉpataṉṟō paṇṭipporuḷ? piṉṉiyōr vaḻu eṉṟu poruḷ koṇṭataṉāl āyiram āṇṭukaḷāka vanta ilakkaṇattīmi eṉṉa? uriyāciriyar tam karuttukkaḷi vāṅkikkoṇṭu nūṟpā vaṭivil ākkiya pavaṇanti mutaliyōr nūlāciriyar eṉiṉ, urivaṭivil eḻutiyataṟkāka iḷampūraṇar mutaliyōr nūlāciriyar āka māṭṭārkaḷā? pāviṉ iṉaṅkaḷ eṉappaṭum tāḻici tuṟi viruttam oru varampiṟku uṭpaṭṭaṉavāka illi. avaṟṟi eṅṅaṉam cīrtiruttam ceyyalām? piṟa moḻiyil tamiḻc coṟkaḷi eḻutumpōtu tamiḻ eḻuttāḷar kaṭippiṭikkum olinērmiyit tamiḻ moḻiyil piṟa coṟkaḷi eḻutum pōtu avarkaḷ kaṭip piṭikka vēṇṭāmā? iviyaṉiyaṉa ilakkaṇattuṟiyiṉ cintaṉik kaḷaṅkaḷ.
pāriyi mūvēntarum vañcittuk koṉṟaṉar eṉṟa muṭipukkup puṟanāṉūṟṟu uriyāciriyar tarum oru nayak kuṟippu cāṉṟākātu. caṅka mūlam vēṇṭum. ‘pāviyaṉṉa nallōḷ’ eṉṟa patiṟṟuppattut toṭar cēraṉ tēviyik kuṟikkumē yaṉṟi vaḻivaḻik kūṟivaruvatu pōlap pārimaṉiviyik kuṟikkātu. patiṟṟuppattu iṟṟit tamiḻar eṉpārkku malinaṭiyākat tōṉṟiṉum ilakkiyap paṇpu eṉṉum naḷircuṉiyil kuṟintatilli. tamiḻ moḻiyil ellāc camayattukkum kāppiyaṅkaḷ iruppiṉum uṭpaṇpukaḷi nōkkuṅkāl, avi tamiḻc camutāyak kāppiyaṅkaḷē. ‘maṇimēkali mēl uripporuḷ muṟṟiya cilappatikāram muṟṟum’ eṉa nīṇṭa iṟutit toṭaril maṇimēkali eṉpatu maṇimēkalik kumariyic cuṭṭumēyaṉṟip paṉṉūṟāṇṭu kaḷākak karutivarum maṇimēkalik kāppiyattic cuṭṭavē cuṭṭātu. ivi pōlvaṉa ilakkiyat tuṟic cintaṉik kaḷaṅkaḷ.
‘piṟappokkum ellā uyirkkum’ eṉṟa tirukkuṟaḷ atikārat talippiṉpaṭi perumi kāṭṭuvatā? potumi kāṭṭuvatā? ‘naṟperun tavattaḷāya naṅkiyik kaṇṭēṉal lēṉ’ eṉṟa irāmāyaṇat toṭari evvāṟu pirittup poruḷ koṇṭāl cītikku ēṟṟam ākum? puṟattu, puṟattōr eṉṟa tolkāppiyac coṟkaḷukku muraṇaṟṟa poruḷ yāvi? ‘peruñcōṟṟu mikupatam’ eṉṟu muṭinākar kūṟuvatu pitirar paṭippā? ivvellām uri vaḻic cintaṉik kaḷaṅkaḷ.
iṟivaṉ perumipāṭum teyvap pācuraṅkaḷil aṭiyavarkaḷ taṟciṟumiyi nāṇātu pakarak kāṇkiṉṟōm. nalviṉiyāṟṟi uyarnta tēvarkaḷit tiruvācakam kaṇṭapaṭi iḻitturikkiṉṟatu. iṟivaṉip pāṭṭuṭit talivaṉākak koṇṭa tirukkōviyiṉ akattuṟikaḷ orucila paṅkil caṅka ilakkiyattōṭu uṭalukiṉṟaṉa. maṉam iṟakkak kaṟka vēṇṭum eṉṟu uḷintu pāṭum tāyumāṉa aṭikaḷiyum cērntārik kolli eṉṉum ciṉam viṭṭu vikkavilliyē! iviyellām camayac cintaṉik kaḷaṅkaḷ.
cilappatikāra maṇimēkalikaḷiṉ moḻikkūṟukaḷi ārāyuṅkāli, caṅka kālattik ki.mu.vukku muṉṉē iṉṉum koṇṭu cella vēṇṭumēyaṉṟi ivviru kāppiyaṅkaḷiṉ kālattik ki.pi. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭukkup piṉṉē taḷḷalāmā? paṇṭit tamiḻiṉattiṉ tol piṟappiṭatti uṟutippaṭuttuvataṟkut tamiḻ nūṟ cāṉṟukaḷ takkaṉa ākāvā? tamiḻilakkiya varalāṟṟil iṉṉum tuṇiya muṭiyātu taṅkik kiṭakkum pulavōr tam kālaṅkaḷ yāvi? tuṇiyāmik kāraṇaṅkaḷ eṉṉō? iṉṉavi varalāṟṟuc cintaṉik kaḷaṅkaḷ.
arilāṉa oru poruṭ kaḷatti avvappōtu tērnteṭuttu maṟittōṭic cintittu oru tuṇivupaṭak karuttaraṅkukaḷukkum malarkaḷukkum vaḻaṅkiya ik kaṭṭurikaḷ eṇṇat takkavi eṉṟa poruḷil eṇcuviyuṭiyaṉa.  ivi paltuṟi paṟṟiyaṉa.  ātaliṉ ellārkkum potu viruntākuka eṉṟu paṭikkiṉṟēṉ.
āyvu nūlkaḷi mutaliṭṭu veḷiyiṭa añcum niliyaṅkaḷ malintu varum ittamiḻakattil ciṟanta maṇivācaka nūlaka uṟutuṇiyāḷar tiru, meyyappaṉ, tamiḻ nōkku uṭiya aṉparātaliṉ, eṉ cintaṉik kaḷaṅkaḷi vaḻakkam pōl virumpi ēṟṟu veḷiyiṭukiṉṟār; kaṟpār maṉaṅkavarum vaṉappil veḷiyiṭukiṉṟār. ippatippiṉāl maṇivācakar nūlakam paṉmaṭaṅku naṉmatippup peṟṟu vaḷarka eṉṟu tamiḻaruḷi vēṇṭukiṉṟēṉ.
  va.cupa. māṇikkam
katirakam, kārikkuṭi
1-5-1975


iraṭṭik kāppiyaṅkaḷ
mutaṟ patippiṉ mukavuri
aimperuṅ kāppiyaṅkaḷuḷ aḻivupaṭātu nām peṟṟuḷḷavi mūṉṟē. ammūṉṟaṉuḷḷum cilappatikāram maṇimēkali eṉṟa iraṇṭum katiyālum karuttālum kālattālum toṭarpuṭiyavi. ivvuṟavuk kāppiyaṅkaḷ ‘iraṭṭik kāppiyaṅkaḷ’ eṉṉum putiya tokip peyarāl orupatippāka vaḻaṅkiṉ, tamiḻp paravalukkum ārāycci ūṟṟukkum iṭamākum eṉṟu eṇṇiṉēṉ. eṉ eṇṇattic ceyalākkiya perumi kārikkuṭi celvi patippakattukku uriyatu. ipputiya tokinūli oru taṉip patippākavum periya malipup patippākavum celvi patippakattār mūṉaṟāṇṭukaṭku muṉ veḷiyiṭṭaṉar. vaḷarntuvarum ivveḷiyīṭṭu niliyattiṉ tamiḻt toṇṭukku naṉṟiyaṉ.
muṟṟum puṇarcci piriyāta valinta patippukkaḷ iṉṟu tamiḻarkkuk kittuṇiyākā. ellāp puṇarccikaḷiyum alakkalakkākap piritta melinta tamiḻar aṟivu peṟavum nāḷit tamiḻ ākkam peṟavum. ūṭalum kūṭalum pōla, cevvi nōkkip piritalum puṇartalum peṟṟa aḷavup patippukkaḷ-naṭuttarap patippukkaḷ-tamiḻakattiṟku vēṇṭuvaṉa. innōkkattāl eḻunta patippu iraṭṭik kāppiyaṅkaḷ ākum.
canti pirittaṟivataṟku ilakkaṇa nūṟ payiṟci vēṇṭum eṉṟu nam makkaḷ tavaṟāka eṇṇi malikkiṉṟaṉar. viḷiyāṭuṅ kuḻantikkeṉa vīti camippāruṇṭō? tolkāppiya mutalāṉa ilakkaṇap paṉuvalkaḷ tōṉṟiya nōkkam vēṟu. coṟkaṭṭi viḷiyāṭalpōlat tirukkuṟaḷil oru pattu atikāraṅkaḷip puṇarttum pirittum colli vāyppayiṟci ceyyuṅkaḷ, pōtum. tamiḻp puṇarcciyiṉ iyalpu, eḷimi, iṉimi iṉṟiyamiyāmi ellām yārkkum viḷaṅkik kiṭakkum. tamiḻarkaḷ tāymoḻiyilakkiyaṅkaḷik kaṟkum periya eḷiya nērmuṟi itutāṉ eṉpatu muṭinta tuṇipu. ippatippil uḷḷa tamiḻp puṇarcci eṉṉum kaṭṭuri immuṟiyil payiṟcikkut tuṇiceyyum.
kātic curukkam eṉṟa pakutiyil iraṭṭik kāppiyaṅkaḷik kaṟka viḻiyum toṭakkattārkkup payaṉpaṭum. cilappatikārattiṟaṉ maṇi mēkalittiṟaṉ eṉṟa pakutikaḷ ikkāppiyaṅkaḷil muṉṉaṟivuṭiyārkku mēlum tiṟaṅkāṇa vāyil kāṭṭum. oppumic curukkam eṉṟa pakuti iraṭṭik kāppiyaṅkaḷ eṉṉum putiya tokip peyarkku oruvakiyāl cāṉṟu tarum. cila aṭikaḷukku orumuṟi nūlmuḻuvatum mēlē tantuḷḷa karutturikaḷ vēṇṭum iṭaṅkaḷit tēṭa vēṇṭātē eḷitiṟ kāṇac ceyyum. aruñcoṟporuḷ eṉṟa pakuti, curukkam pōlat tōṉṟiṉum, iru kāppiyaṅ kaḷilum varum potuvāṉa coṟkaḷ perumpālum iṭam peṟṟuḷa eṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakum.
nūl muḻuvataṟkum aṭikaḷ variciyākat toṭarntu eṇṇappaṭṭuḷa. cilappatikāram maṇimēkalikku ittoṭa reṇ muṟi ippatippiṟkē uriyatu. iṭaṅ kuṟittuk koḷḷavum mēṟkōḷ kāṭṭavum, ivvaki eṇṇup patippu kaṟṟārkkum kaṟpārkkum peruntuṇiyākum. kāti muṭivil valappakkattuḷḷa tamiḻeṇkaḷ akkātikku uriya aṭiyeṇṇikkiyik kāṭṭum.
iraṇṭām patippākiya ivveḷiyīṭṭil cilappatikāra māntar, maṇimēkali māntar, cilappatikāra ilakkiyak kuṟippukkaḷ, maṇimēkali ilakkiyak kuṟippukkaḷ eṉṟa putiya pakutikaḷ cērntuḷḷaṉa. ilakkiyak kuṟippukkaḷ nirampiyavi yalla; eṉiṉum ilakkiya nilattukku uriya uraṅkaḷiyum ilakkiya uḻavukku vēṇṭum muṟikaḷiyum cevvaṉam pulappaṭuttum.
iraṭṭik kāppiyam patippuvaṉappellām poliya acciṭṭut tanta kārikkuṭi teṉṉintiya (cut intiyā) accakattārkku mikavum naṉṟiyuṭiyēṉ.
va.cupa. māṇikkam
kārikkuṭi, katirakam, 15.12.1960


maṉiviyiṉ urimi
mukavuri
nāṭakaṅkaḷ aintaṉuḷ mutalaṉa mūṉṟum puṟapporuḷic cārntaṉa; nāṉkāvatu akamum puṟamum taḻuviyatu; iṟutiyatu akapporuḷ paṟṟiyatu. ceṉṟakālat tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ tūymi, varuṅkālat tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ cemmi, nikaḻkālat tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ moḻiyaṟivu eṉṟa mūṉṟiṉiyum āciriyaṉ karuttiṟkoṇṭu, eḻutiyatoru ilakkiya naṭiyākum. innāṭakaṅkaḷiṉ naṭi eṉpatum, appaṭiyē naṭakkattakum araṅku naṭiyaṉṟu eṉpatum aṟivārkkup pulaṉām.
oru nūliṉ tavap perumiyum naṉi ciṟumiyum kāṇa viḻivār, ataṉip paṉmuṟi kaṟṟal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam kaṟkum muṟiyiṉiyē, ‘navil toṟum nūl nayam’ eṉṟār āciriyar tiruvaḷḷuvar. paṉmuṟi kaṟṟalōṭu, nuṇukkat tiṉik kāṇavalla aṭippaṭi aṟivum, naṉṟu tītu tuṇiya valla nērmiyum kaṟpārkku iṉṟiyamiyātaṉa. ātaliṉ, eṉ uḻippiṉ mutal uruvaṅkaḷākiya innāṭakaṅkaḷiṉ iḻivu ciṟappu uyarvu ciṟappukkaḷic cīrtūkkik kāṇum poṟuppu kaṟpār pālatākum eṉpatu eṉ paṇivuṭik koḷki.
eṉ nāṭakaṅkaḷi mutaṟkaṇ veḷiyīṭu ceyya aṉpoṭu muṉ vantaruḷiya naṇpar, putukkōṭṭit tamiḻ niliyat talivar avarkaḷukku eṉ uḷḷaṉpu urimiyākum.
	va. cupa. māṇikkam
kārikkuṭi, 1-6-1957


nellikkaṉi 
mukavuri
makkaḷiṉ tēvip poruḷkaḷukkum aṭippaṭi uṇarccikaḷukkum iṭiyē iṉṟu pūcalkaḷ perukiyiruk kiṉṟaṉa. vāḻviṉ puṟanalaṅkaḷip perukkikkoḷvataṟkāka niliyāṉa maṉita uṇarvukaḷik kuṟikkavum tirikkavum aḻikkavum muyalkiṉṟōm.
tēviyiṉ vēṭkikkum iyalpāṉa uṇarccikkum puṟappōrāṭṭam naṭappatu pōlavē, māṉiṭa uṇarcci kaḷukkuḷḷum akappōrāṭṭam nikaḻkiṉṟatu. piṟa uṇarccikaḷi mikiyāka vaḷarttu, taliyāṉa iyaṟki uṇarccikaḷi nacukka muyalkiṉṟōm.
piṟavikku uriya uṇarccikaḷi, maḻiyi maṇ ēṟpatupōla, ēṟṟu nalvaḻippaṭuttik koḷḷa vēṇṭum. atutāṉ vāḻvu neṟi; atuvē uṭampiyum uḷḷattiyum camutāyattiyum ulakiyum vāḻvikkum neṟi. inta uṇmiyi nāṭṭa eḻuntatu innāṭakam.
	ṭākṭar va.cupa.māṇikkam
katirakam, kārikkuṭi-2
1-12-1962


uppaṅkaḻi
mukavuri
pōratircci, pakiyatircci, tērtalatircci, pataviyatircci, pukaḻatircci, poruḷatircci, uṭalatircci eṉṟavāṟu ulakil pala atirccikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ kuṭumpa atircciyē maṉitaṉukku neruṅkiyatu, neñcatti uṟuttuvatu, ēṉiyatirccikaḷukkum uṭkāraṇamāvatu.
ellārkkum ellāp pūcalkaḷum varuvatilli. kuṭumpap pūcalō yāriyum viṭuvatilli. ēṉ? kuṭivaḻiyiṉṟi yār piṟantavar. pala pūcalkaḷit tāṅkum pērāṇmiyaṉ kūṭak kuṭiyatircciyit tāṅkamāṭṭāṉ.
uppaṅkaḻi eṉṟa ikkuṭumpa nāṭakam kuṭiccikkalkaḷ eṉṉa, avaṟṟi yār yār ettuṇiyaḷavu tāṅkik koḷ kiṉṟaṉar, emmuṟiyil mella ēṟṟu nallavaḻi kāṇkiṉṟaṉar, oru cilarāl tāṅka muṭiyāmi ēṉ? eṉṟa vāḻviyalkaḷik kāṭṭukiṉṟatu.
kātalukku niṟam illi, mutalil tōṉṟum nalleṇṇattukku matippaḷikka vēṇṭum, iyaṟkiyoṭu paḻakuvatu uṇarcciyi oḻuṅkupaṭuttum eṉṟa nallōṭṭaṅkaḷ vaḷiyiṭi vīcum vāṉoli pōla innāṭakattil kalantu varukiṉṟaṉa.
camutāyap potuc cīrtiruttamiṉṟic cila kuṭumpac cūḻal kaḷit taṉittaṉi ovvoruvarum māṟṟikkoḷḷa iyalātu eṉṟa puraṭcik kuṟiyum innāṭakattil aṭaṅki yuḷḷatu.
putu ārāyccikaḷiyum paṭippukkaḷiyum tamiḻp paṇiyāka ēṟṟup patippittu varum paṇpuṭiya citamparam maṇivācaka nūlakattārkku vāḻttuk kalanta naṉṟi.
	va.cupa. māṇikkam
kārikkuṭi, 1-11-72
oru noṭiyil
mukavuri 
uyiriṉ cintaṉikku
vāḻvil oru muṟiyāvatu taṟkoliyi eṇṇāta vaḷ illi; eṇṇātavaṉ illi. inta naliveṇṇam enta vayatilum muḷikkiṉṟatu. ēṉ? tuṉpattiliruntu virivil viṭutalipeṟat taṉ kiyil irukkum eḷiyavaḻi taṟkoli eṉṟa karuttiṉip pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷākac camayaṅkaḷum ilakkiyaṅkaḷum aṟaṅkaḷum naṭimuṟi vaḻakkaṅkaḷum ūṭṭip pārāṭṭi vantirukkiṉṟaṉa.
ulakil makkaḷ matiyāta poruḷ oṉṟu uṇṭeṉiṉ atu uyir eṉpatu nāṉ kaṇṭatu. matippatākac colva tellām naṭippē. uṇmiyāṉa uyirppaṟṟu uṭiyavaṉ uṭal nalattik kāppāṉ, kuṇanalattik kāppāṉ, piṟa nalattiyum kāppāṉ. kuṭaliyum uṭaliyum paṇattiyum kuṇattiyum puṇarcciyiyum uṇarcciyiyum eṭuttataṟ kellām keṭuttuk koṇṭu vēkatākappaṭṭuk konta ḷikkum ivvulakam uyirip pōṟṟukiṟatu eṉpataṉi evvāṟu ottukkoḷvatu?
uyir eṉpatu tāṉē vanta taṉakkuvamiyillāta vittuc celvam. koḷki vaṟumi māṉam maṟṟavaṟṟukkāka uyirik kiḷḷuk kīri pōla nīkkalām pōkkalām eṉṟu pāṭi eḻutip paṟicāṟṟip parappivarum paḻampōkkit taṭippaṭutta vēṇṭum eṉṟa etirppu nōkkattālum, uyiriṭattut taṟpaṟṟu ūṭṭi vāḻvil nampikki koḷḷac ceyya vēṇṭum eṉṟa kaṇṇōṭṭattālum ippattu nāṭakaṅ kaḷiyum paṭittuḷḷēṉ. ivi oru poruḷmēl eḻunta taṉimoḻi nāṭakaṅkaḷ. taṉiyāṉa taṉmoḻi uḷḷatti orumippaṭuttum, uṇarcciyit tūṇṭi oḻuṅkupaṭuttum, tuṇivōṭu vāḻum vaḻikāṭṭum eṉpataṉāl innāṭaka vaṭivi mēṟkoṇṭēṉ.
etaṉi nām piṟantapiṉ peṟṟōmō anta uṭimiyi viṭum urimi namakku uṇṭu. uyir eṉpatu iyaṟki varavu. aḵtu iyaṟkiyākap pōka vēṇṭum. inta iyalpāṉa vitiyi maṉpatik kaḷattil putiya uramiṭṭu uḻavuceyya muyalkiṉṟēṉ. iṭiyiṭṭu vantupōm patavikaḷi aḻutta mākap piṭittukkoḷḷap pāṭupaṭum ivvulakam, meyyāṉa piṟavip pataviyip paluvaṉ kāṟṟuppōla eḷitāka viṭalām eṉṟu iṉṉum eḻutip parappik koṇṭiruppatu poruntumā?
etaṉiyum ceyyalām; oru nāḷ taṟkoli ceytu tappittuk koḷḷalām eṉṟa tavaṟāṉa eṇṇatti uyirkkuṇamāka ākki vantirukkiṉṟōm. ataṉāl iḷiñarkaḷukkum uṇarccip piṇṭarkaḷukkum nērvaḻi tōṉṟavilli; kuṟukku vaḻiyē caṭṭeṉap paṭukiṉṟatu. kaṭavuṭ paṟṟum taṟkoliyum muraṇ eṉṟu camayap periyārkaḷ iṭitturikka vēṇṭum. taṟkoliyāka nikaḻccikaḷi muṭittuk kāṭṭātapaṭi katiñarkaḷ kaviñarkaḷ tiriñarkaḷ aṟiñarkaḷ ellōrum tampaṭippukkaḷi nalamāka neṟippaṭutta vēṇṭum. uyirōṭum muṉṉaṟivōṭum tuṇivōṭum paṇpōṭum enniliyilum tiruntiyum tiruttiyum vāḻalām, vāḻka eṉṟa putu maṉappāṉmiyi iḷiñarkaḷukkup pāycca vēṇṭum. ivi eṉ vēṇṭuri.
taṟkoli yeṇṇam eḻuvatum oru noṭiyil; taṟkoli ceytu toṅkuvatum oru noṭiyil; antap pāḻeṇṇam māṟuvatum oru noṭiyil. oru noṭip poṟumi illāmiyāl eytaṟkariya arumiyi, vāḻum urimiyi, celvavuyiri iḻakkalāmā?
taṟkoli yeṇṇam vantavuṭaṉ oṉṟu ceyyuṅkaḷ. uṅkaḷ eṇṇatti oru naṇpaṉiṭamō, periyavariṭamō, āciriyariṭamō vāyviṭṭut terivittuviṭuṅkaḷ. uyirōṭu vāḻvīrkaḷ! uyarvāka vāḻvīrkaḷ! uṇmiyāka vāḻvīrkaḷ!
	va. cupa. māṇikkam
katirakam, kārikkuṭi -2
   	1-12-1972
koṭiviḷakku
pāyiram
uḷḷam uvappa uṭimi yellām
paḷḷikaḷ niṟuvip paṇpiyal vaḷarkka
aḷḷi vaḻaṅkiya aḻakaṉ koṭiyiṉi
nīṭum pukaḻeṉa niṉittē ṉāyiṉum
pāṭuṅ kuṟippup paṇṭeṉak killi.

āvi taṉitta aṇṇal mēṉiyik
kūvik koṭuntī kuṟikkuṅ kāli
vaṇṇat tamiḻaṉi vaḷḷal aḻakaṉit
taṇṇan tamiḻil talivaṉāyp pāṭum
eṇṇam mukiḻppa eḻuntatip pāṭṭu.

paḻutaṟu nampiyiṉ pārpukaḻ vāḻkkiyi
muḻutuṟa virikkum muḻupperuṅ kāppiyam
eḻutum pulami eṉṉaḷa vaṉṟukāṇ;
aṉṉit tamiḻiṉ aṇiviral mōtiram
eṉṉap puṉinta iḷañciṟu kāppiyam
toṭikkup poruḷāyt tōṉṟiya aḻakaṉik
koṭikku viḷakkeṉak kūṟap peṟumāl

muṉṉi vaḷḷiyōr mūvāt tamiḻaṇintu
eṉṟum vāḻvar ivaṉum vāḻvaṉ
ariya poruḷāl aṟivu parappiya
periya koṭiyāṉ piṟappiṉi
uriya neṟiyāl uḷḷuvam nāmē.
		va.cupa. māṇikkam


māmalarkaḷ
muṉṉuri
eṉ mutaṟ kavitinūl koṭi viḷakku eṉpatu oru poruḷ nutaliyatu, veṇpāvāl amintatu. māmalarkaḷ eṉṟa ikkavitip paṉuval eṉ iraṇṭāvatu paṭippākum. itu palvakic ceyyuḷoṭu palvakip poruḷ cārntatu. iḷami mutalē avvappōtu pāṭiya ceyyuṭkaḷ it tokutiyiṉ muṟpakutiyil iṭaṅkoṇṭaṉa eṉṟālum aviyellām iṉṟiya eṉ uṇarvukkum moḻi naṭikkum ēṟpac cevviya tiruttaṅkaḷ peṟṟuḷa eṉṟu karuta vēṇṭum.
eṇṇam irupatu, ūrti muppatu, tamiḻāṭṭu, tamiḻcūṭi, puraṭci maṇṭōtari, kollāc cilampu, aṉṉi kotikkiṟāḷ, ulakappāyiram eṉṟa eṇ pakutikaḷ ittokuppiṉ piṟ pakutiyil uḷa. ivi aṇmip putup paṭippukkaḷākum.
puraṭci maṇṭōtari, kollāccilampu eṉṟa iru pakutikaḷ irāmāyaṇam, cilappatikāram cārntavi; kati muṭivukaḷ muṟṟum māṟāṉavi; peṇmikkum puraṭcip paṇpukkum matippaḷippavi; kāppiyaṅkaḷ tiṭīreṉa maṅkalamāka muṭiyak kūṭāvā eṉṟu cintikka vippavi; vaḻivanta katikku māṟāyiṉum putiya uttikku vaḻi kāṭṭupavi. ittaku putu māṟṟaṅkaḷ nallaṟiñarkaḷiṉ mutaletirppukku uriyavi eṉiṉum aṭippaṭip paṇpukaḷi vaḷarkkum nōkkil ilakkiyak kaṉavulakil ivi etir pārkka vēṇṭiyaviyē.  
‘aṉṉi kotikkiṟāḷ’ eṉṉum aṅkatak kaviti ōr iyakkap pāṭṭākum. ivviyakkam tamiḻi vāḻvu moḻiyākavum uyarnta intiyap potu moḻiyākavum ulaka moḻiyākavum. ākkum, tamiḻiṉatti uyvikkum, tamiḻaka araci vaḻippaṭuttic ceyaṟpaṭuttum. tamiḻ vaḷarcciyil namakku vēṟeṉṉa vēṇṭum?
	va. cupa. māṇikkam
katirakam, kārikkuṭi
1.12.1978

urinaṭiyil tirukkuṟaḷ
mukavuri
tiruvaḷḷuvariṉ ellāk karuttukkaḷiyum ellārum eḷitil aṟintu koḷḷutaṟkut teḷivāṉa inta urinaṭit tirukkuṟaḷ peritum tuṇi ceyyum eṉṟu nampukiṟēṉ.
	va.cupa. māṇikkam
katirakam, kārikkuṭi
kampar nāṟpatu
muṉṉuri
‘pukaḻk kampaṉ piṟanta tamiḻ nāṭu’ eṉṟu pārāṭṭip pāṭiṉār tēciyakkavi pāratiyār. tamiḻnāṭṭil kampaṉ piṟantavūr cōḻanāṭṭut tēraḻuntūr eṉavum, ulakap peruṅkāppiyamāṉa irāmāyaṇattic cevinukar kaṉikaḷākap pāṭip pukaḻpeṟṟu maṟinta ūr pāṇṭināṭṭiṉ nāṭṭaracaṉkōṭṭi eṉavum varalāṟu colkiṉṟatu. ātaliṉ, nāṭṭaracaṉ kōṭṭi vaḷākattil vāḻum nam ciṟuvarkaḷum ciṟumikaḷum kampariṉ ceyyuṭkaḷik kaṟpatu kaṭamiyum perumiyum ākum.
iḷiya uḷḷaṅkaḷil kampariṉ pāṭal vittukkaḷip patiyavikkum nōkkōṭu, irāmayaṇattiliruntu eḷiya iṉiya nāṟpatu pāṭalkaḷip poṟukkik karutturiyum eḻutik kampar nāṟpatu eṉṟa peyarāl icciṟunūli ākkittantār aṟiñar va.cupa. māṇikkam avarkaḷ. vaṉappum nayamum ciṟanta ic ciṟunūli civacatti aṟaniliyattiṉ cārpāka naṉku paravum nōkkōṭu veḷiyiṭukiṉṟōm. kampariṉ kāppiyap pāṭalkaḷ cilavēṉum palarkkut terintirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu karutiya nallaṉpar nāṭṭaracaṉ kōṭṭi tiru. ē. cattiyamūrtti avarkaḷ innūl veḷiyiṭṭiṟku vēṇṭum tuṇi ceytārkaḷ.
it tokuppiṉik kampariṭattil īṭupāṭu koṇṭavar evarum kaṟkalām. ciṟappākap paḷḷi māṇava māṇaviyar kaṟpatu nalla tamiḻaṟivit tarum.
va.cupa. māṇikkam
katirakam, kārikkuṭi


tolkāppiyam eḻuttatikāramum nūṉmarapum
urippāyiram - māṇikkavuri - tamiḻurimi
centamiḻ tāyākat teṉpiṟappuk koṇṭanām
vantamoḻik kellām vayiṟṟaṭimi yākāmal
conta moḻikkē tollurimi nāṭāmō;
toltamiḻ tāyākac coṟpiṟappuk koṇṭanām
palkali yellām paḻakutamiḻ kallāmal
almoḻiyiṟ kaṟpikkum āṇavattic cāṭāmō;
pintamiḻ tāyākap paṇpiṟappuk koṇṭanām
conta nilattiṉum tūyatamiḻ vāḻāmal
nontu maṭikkum noṭinilikku nāṇāmō
nāṇiyum cāṭiyum nāṭṭiyum caṅkanūl
pēṇiyum niṟkum peruntamiḻ makkaḷē
ūṇum uṭiyum uṇarvum uṭiyar;
ulaka moḻiyāṉa untu tamiḻi
alaku niṟiya aṟiviya lākkum
tilaka aracukkē ceynnaṉṟi pāṭutum.
tolkāppiyam eṉṟa ulakak kaḷañciyam moḻiyilum akavāḻvilum puṟaccūḻalilum kaṭṭuppāṭu, vaḷarcci, tūymi vēṇṭum varampunūl; tolkāppiyam eṉṟa tamiḻ mutaṉūl vaḻivanta paḻamit taṭaṅkāṭṭi, nikaḻkālac cevvi cērttu, vaḷarum etirkālap putumip puraṭcikku iṭam vakukkum iyakkanūl; tolkāppiyam eṉṟa maṟinūl piṟappu matam pāl kuḻu iṭam poruḷnili vēṟṟumikaḷip paṟṟātu iyaṟki, aṟam, maṟam, veṟṟi, amiti, kātal, iṉpam eṉṉum ivvulakiyaṅkaḷi māṉiṭaviṉattiṟku eṭuttuk kāṭṭum ulaka vāḻvunūl.
iyakka vāḻvup perumaṟiyāṉa tolkāppiyatti īṉṟavaḷ tamiḻttāy. tamiḻarkaḷākiya nām mūvāyiram āṇṭut toṉmiyuṭiya innūli nam tāymoḻiyilēyē iṉṟum paṭikkum eḷiya iṉiya nērvāyppiṉip peṟṟirukkiṉṟōm. ituvē tamiḻppiṟappiṉ oru ciṟappeṉa uṇarka. mēlum tolkāppiyattukku iṉṟukāṟum piṉtōṉṟiya periya ciṟiya ilakkiya ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷil ellām tolkāppiya nīrōṭṭam uṇṭu. ātaliṉ tolkāppiya niṉivum tolkāppiyak karuttaṟivum peṟutal tamiḻaṉeṉpāṉ ovvoruvaṉiṉ piṟappuk kaṭamiyākum iṉṟu tamiḻiṉam āyiram pirivu piḷavu paṭṭiruppiṉum ellārkkum potu muṉṉōṉ tolkāppiyaṉē eṉṟu uṇarvōmāka. orumiyiṉavuṇarvu peṟuvōmāka.
mēṟcuṭṭiya nalluṇarvukaḷōṭu eṉ tolkāppiyavuri tōṉṟukiṉṟatu marapuppaṭi ivvuri māṇikkam eṉap peyar peṟum. kiṭitta varalāṟṟiṉpaṭi iḷampūraṇar mutaluri yāciriyar āvār. ivarkku muṉṉum urikaḷ iruntaṉa eṉṟa kuṟippu uṇṭu. iḷampūraṇarkkuppiṉ tolkāppiya urik kulam iṭikkāla mutal iṉṟukāṟum vaḷarntukoṇṭē varukiṉṟatu. ivvaḷarcci piṉṉaṭiyātu. innūṟṟāṇṭil paṉṉāṭṭu aṟiñarkaḷum paltuṟiñarkaḷum vevvēṟu muṉiyil olkāp perumpukaḻt tolkāppiyattiṉip parantu virintu nuṇuki āyntu varuvataṉālum, tamiḻakattilum nalval iḷiñarkaḷ tolkāppiya āyvuk kātalarkaḷāka vaḷarntu ciṟantu payilvatālum, yār eḻuttilum pēccilum evvakit talippilum kuyilnuḻi potumpar pōlat tolkāppiyat teṉṟal tūcoḻiya vīcuvatālum, tamiḻiṉ poṟkālam tolkāppiyattiṉ varuṅkālamāka viḷaṅkum eṉṟu naṉavu kāṇkiṉṟēṉ.
perunūṟ paṭippāḷarkaḷ nīṇṭa varuṅkāla nōkkoṭu tam nūlākkaṅkaḷic ceyya. makkaḷ iyalpāṉa vāḻkkiyiṟ kūṭa vīṭu kaṭṭumpōtum teṉṉan tōṭṭam vikkumpōtum nūṟāṇṭu etirkālam koḷvatilliyā? māmallac ciṟpam, tañcip peruvuṭiyār kōyil pōlum kalic celvaṅ kaḷiyum tamiḻp palkalikkaḻakam pōṉṟa uyarkalvi niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷiyum tōṟṟunar ivi taṅkālat toṭu niṟka eṉṟā karutukiṉṟaṉar? evvaḷavu etirkālak kuṟippōṭṭam avarkaḷ cintaṉikkuḷ iruntirukkum? ātaliṉ tol kāppiyam tirukkuṟaḷ cilappatikāram tiruvācakam pāratīyam eṉṟiṉiya paṉuvalkaḷ aimpūtaṅkaḷ pōlum eṉṟum aṭippaṭic cintaṉikku uriyavi: uḷḷaṅkaḷ uḷḷaḷavum uḷḷattakkavi: aṟivukkūrmikkum aṟivuppāyvukkum kaṭṭaḷik kallāṉavi: tamiḻiyattukkum tamiḻiṉattukkum tamiḻmoḻikkum aṭiyā ūṟṟukkaṇ kaḷāṉavi. mali kaṭal vāṉam marañceṭi koṭikaḷ paḻami eṉṟu otukka muṭiyumā? uṇmiyum iyaṟkiyum uyirppum uṭiyavi yellām kālavaṭṭaṅkaṭantu maṉṉiyavi eṉṟu uṇarvōmāka.
tolkāppiya muḻumikkum uriceynōkkamum urikkuṟippukkaḷum uḷaveṉiṉum kālappatampārttu mutaṟkaṇ eḻuttatikārattukku māṇikkavuri eḻutiyiruk kiṉṟēṉ. ivvuriyaṅkaḷ varumāṟu:
a) iyalmuṉṉuri
iḵtu iyalukku oru ciṟiya muṉṉuriyākum. iyal tōṟum varum ilakkaṇakkaruttukkaḷiṉ ciṟappukkaḷip pulappaṭuttum. iṉṟum makkaḷ naṭimuṟiyil irukkum vaḻakkukkaḷōṭu poruntikkāṭṭum. itaṉāl kaṟpavarkkut tamiḻilakkaṇa naṉmarapukaḷ teriyavarum. ilakkaṇakkāppil avarkaṭkup paṟṟum payiṟciyum uṇṭākum.
ā) iyaṟkaruttu
nūṟpākkaḷiṉ karuttukkaḷ mutaṟkaṇ curukkamākat tokuttuk kūṟappaṭum.
i) akalavuri
nūṟpāviṟkup perumpālum collukkuccol kiṭantāṅku colluri eḻutavilli. kaṟpavar uṭaṉē viḷaṅkik koḷḷumāṟu vēṇṭum viḷakkañ¨cērntu virivuriyāka amiyum. muṉṉi nūṟpāviṉ toṭarpōṭu iyipu teriya varum. karuttōṭip paṭikkum eḷimiyuṇṭu. vēṇṭu miṭattuc collukku uriya poruḷ kūṟappaṭum.


ī) vaḻakku
irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil eḻutappaṭum uriyātaliṉ, iṉṟiya pēccu vaḻakkiliruntum eḻuttunūl vaḻakkiliruntum ilakkaṇa vitikaṭku ollumaḷavu eṭuttuk kāṭṭukkaḷ tarappaṭum. ilakkaṇavitikaḷ ceyyuṭku mikutiyum uriyavi eṉpatu mikat tavaṟāṉa karuttu. vēṟoru pulamip payaṉkarutic ceyyuṭkāṭṭukkaḷ tarum marapu ēṟpaṭṭatu. avvaḷavē. uṇmiyac cīrtūkkiṉ toṇṉūṟu viḻukkāṭu ilakkaṇappāṅku naṭimuṟi makkaḷiṉ urinaṭip pēccil amintukiṭappatu. tolkāppiya ilakkaṇak kūṟukaḷ aviya nūl pōlum paḻamicārntavi eṉpatum maṟṟoru tavaṟāṉa karuttu, ātaliṉ aṇmikkāla ilakkiyaṅ kaḷiliruntum cāṉṟukaḷ kāṭṭappaṭum. tolkāppiyam eṉṟum vaḻakkiṟavātatu. nili niṉṟavaḻakkiṉatu eṉṟa meyyyuṇarvit tamiḻmaṉpatikku ūṭṭum cālpil eṭuttukkāṭṭukkaḷ palakālattaṉavāka irukkum. itu valinta nōkkaṉṟu; marapum vaḷarcciyum kalanta nōkku.
u) tiṟaṉuri
nūṟpāviṉ poruḷ, amippu, naṭi, collāṭci, kāṭṭukkaḷ eṉṟāṅku nūṟpāviṉ peruviḷakkamāka ippakuti amiyum. nūṟpāvukku varinta uripporuttam teḷivu ceyyappaṭum. otta piṟa karuttukkaḷum ovvāppiṟa karuttukkaḷum kuṟukiyavaḷavu iṭampeṟum. itaṉāl aiyaṅkaḷ nīṅkit tiripukaḷ akaṉṟu nūṟpāviṉ meymmikaḷ mēlum teḷivākum poruḷ potinta pāṭapētaṅkaḷ uḷavēl avaṟṟiyum ikkūṟu cuṭṭit tiṟaṉ ceyyum. etirkālat toṭarcci kuṟikkappaṭum.
ū ) iyalmuṭivuri
tolkāppiyattukkup pallurikaḷum pala tiṟaṉāyvu nūlkaḷum āyvukkaṭṭurikaḷum piṟaṅki vantuḷa. taṉitta oru nūṟpāvi nuṇuki āyntoḻiya kaṭṭurikaḷum uḷa. taṉ urikkaruttē takum eṉṟa nampikki vēṇṭiyatē eṉiṉum piṟar karuttukkaḷum nallavaṉavāka irukkum eṉṟa cālpuppārvi uriyāḷaṉ paṇpākum. nallaṉavaṟṟic cuṭṭik kāṭṭuvatu taṉ urimatippik kūṭṭum. allaṉavaṟṟi iṉcollāl iṉṉaṭiyāl maṟuppatum iṉṟiyamiyāta urineṟi ākum. aṉittiyum vāri vaḷittukkāṭṭap pukuvatu karuttumaṇṭiya āyvukkāṭāka muṭiyumātaliṉ kaṟpārkkut teriya vēṇṭumaḷavu kaṟpārkkuc cumiyeṉat tōṉṟāta koḷḷaḷavu iyal muṭivuri amiyum. ovvōr iyalilum varum cila pala nūṟpākkaṭkup puṟavuripōl iyaliṉ iṟutiyil im muṭivuri eḻutappaṭum. itaṉāl nuṇmiyum āḻamum aṟivuc cuviyum māṇavarkkup piṟakkum.
pāratap perunāṭṭil moḻikkeṉat tōṉṟiya mutataṟkaḻakam tamiḻp palkalikkaḻakamākum. tamiḻkkuṟikkōḷum tamiḻniṉivum cāṉṟa pēraṟiñar aṇṇāviṉ vaḻivanta tamiḻka mutalvar puraṭcit talivar ma.kō.irāmaccantiraṉār ippalkalik kaḻakattit tōṟṟiya perumāṉ āvār. ippalkalikkaḻakattiṉ vallunar kuḻuttalivarāka iruntu palkalikkaḻakavamippiṉi uruvākkik koṭukkum orupēṟu eṉakkuk kiṭittatu. itaṉ mutal tuṇivēntar mutumuṉivar va.ay.cuppiramaṇiyaṉārum āṭcik kuḻuviṉarum tamiḻ mutaṉūlāṉa tolkāppiyattiṉ taṉipperumi karutit tolkāppiyap pulattakimi eṉṟa ōr āyvuk kaṭṭil uṇṭākki, ataṉ tolkāppiyat takiñarāka eṉṉip paṇi koṇṭaṉar. ivvaṉivōrkkum vaḻivaḻi neṭituvāḻum tamiḻp palkalikkaḻakattukkum mikka naṉṟiyaṉ.
iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ tam peruṅkāppiyattuḷ taṉ vāḻkki kuṟittu oru cila cuṭṭippōnta marapuṇṭu. avvaḻi yāṉum mikac cila kūṟikkoḷvēṉ. ikkuṟippiṉāl kaṟpōrkku nampikkiyum perumitamum uṇṭākum. eṉ patiṉeṭṭām akaviyil parumāviṉtalinakarāṉa iraṅkūṉil kaṭip paṇiyāḷarāka iruntapōtu vāymik kuṟikkōḷ koṇṭēṉ. ataṉ piṉ tamiḻakam vantu aṇṇāmalippalkalikkaḻakam cērntu pulavar tamiḻ payiṉṟēṉ. paṇṭitamaṇi katirēcaṉār, nāvalar vēṅkaṭacāmi makāvittuvāṉ irākavaṉār, tuṟavi kantacāmiyār eṉṟiṉṉa perumpulamiyāḷar eṉ āciriyap perumakkaḷ āvar. aḻakappā kallūriyil tamiḻppērāciriya ṉākavum mutalvaṉākavum, eṉ kalvit tāyakamāṉa aṇṇāmalip palkalik kaḻakattut tamiḻttuṟip pērāciriyaṉākavum intiyap pulattuṟi mutaṉmiyaṉākavum, maturi kāmarācar palkalikkaḻakat tuṇi vēntaṉākavum paṇiceytavaṉ. vaḷḷuvam, tamiḻkātal, kampar eṉṟa oru poruḷ āyvunūlkaḷum; cintaṉikkaḷaṅkaḷ, oppiyalnōkku, tolkāppiyapputumi, tolkāppiyattiṟaṉ, entac cilampu, ilakkiya viḷakkam eṉṟa palporuḷ āyvunūlkaḷum; maṉiviyiṉ urimi, nellikkaṉi, uppaṅkaḻi, oru noṭiyil eṉṟa nāṭaka nūlkaḷum, koṭiviḷakku, māmalarkaḷ eṉṟa kavitinūlkaḷum; iraṭṭik kāppiyam eṉṟa patippunūlum iṉṉum cilavum āṅkilanūl cilavum eṉ eḻuttup piṟappukkaḷ.
paṉṉūṟāṇṭukaḷāka ayalmoḻikaḷiṉ araciyal ātikkattāl uṇarvu maṭaṅkit tamiḻkkalvi tamiḻ makkaḷiṭi kuṉṟiya varalāṟṟiyē kāṇkiṟōm. piṉṉum nāṭu taḻuviya āṅkilap pērātikkattāl uḷḷa niliyum vaṟṟik kāla vaḷarcci peṟamāṭṭāta tamiḻiṉ avalanili varalāṟṟi uṇarkiṉṟōm. pālkuṭimaṟavā variyiḷañ¨ ceṅkāṟ kuḻavikaḷit tamiḻppāl uṇṇaviṭātu āṅkila mutalāṉa ayal maṭippālkaḷi valintu mayaṅki ūṭṭum kaṭumkoṭum pōkkiṉik kaṇṭu kiyāṟu eytiyirukkiṉṟōm. ataṉ mēlum varampaṟṟut taṭiyaṟṟu vēṟṟumoḻic coṟkaḷiyum olikaḷiyum eḻuttukkaḷiyum tammuṭimi pōṟṟātu kalakkaviṭṭa orē kāraṇattāl tamiḻaka nilavellikaḷ aṇmikkālattup paṟipōy maṇṇakam curuṅkiya iḻappukkaḷiyum tamiḻneñcaṅkaḷ maṟakkamuṭiyavilali
paḷḷimutal palkalikkaḻakamvari, kikkuḻavi mutal kaṟṟār vari ayalvaḻi ōṭumpōtu, etaṉi evvaḷavu eḷimi yāka eḻutiṉālum, tamiḻil kuṟuntaramum illātārkku eṉṉa ceyvatu tam taramiṉmi nōvātu tamiḻi nōvatu naṉṟā? tamiḻar tarattāl uyarntu tamiḻi vaṇaṅka vēṇṭumē yaṉṟit tām tāḻum kalappukkuḻikkut tamiḻi vaṇakkalāmā? eṉiṉum tamiḻiṉ varuṅkālam naṟuṅkālamāka irukkum eṉṟa nampikkiyuṇṭu. tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ taratti uyarttuvatē nam kalvikkōḷ. eḻuttuk kōḷ iḻanta varalāṟṟukkuk kavalātu cōrvuṟuttātu etirkāla nalval varalāṟṟik kāṇpatē aṟivuṭimi. ātaliṉ ellāt tamiḻarum aṟiya vēṇṭiya tamiḻmaṟi mutaþṉūlāṉa tolkāppiyatti `eṇporuḷa vākac celaccolli' eṉṟa tirukkuṟaṭpaṭi ollum vaḻiyellām eḷimippaṭuttuvōm; kālattuṟkēṟṟa eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷ tantum ilakkiyam kāṭṭiyum eṇmip paṭuttuvōm. tamiḻ `kallātu niṟpār piṟariṉmiyiṉ kalvi vallārumilli. avi vallarallārum illi' eṉṟa kampar perumāṉ kaviti naṉavākak kāṇpōm. makkaḷiṉ tamiḻppiṭiyum tamiḻttaramum tolkāppiya vuṇarvumē eṉ uri nōkkam.
		va.cupa.māṇikkam
kārikkuṭi,
6-8-1986


nakarattār aṟappaṭṭayaṅkaḷ
patippuri
nakarattār aṟappaṭṭayaṅkaḷ eṉṉum innūlil eṭṭuvaki ēṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ kālam ki.pi.1600 mutal 1800 vari eṉṟu matippiṭalām. nāṭṭuvaralāṟu eṉpatu pōrc ceyalkaḷiyum aracarkaḷiṉ ceytikaḷiyum maṭṭum kuṟippataṉṟu; kuṭimakkaḷiṉ ceyalkaḷiyum camutāya naṭappuk kaḷiyum kūṟavēṇṭum. avvakkālattu maṉappāṉmikaḷik kāṭṭa vēṇṭum. ivvēṭukaḷ paḻaṉittalattu munnūṟṟimpatu āṇṭukaḷāka nikaḻntuvarum camaya naṭappukkaḷiyum naṭattiyavar kaḷiyum avarkaḷiṉ aṟputacceyalkaḷiyum acceyalkaḷi viyanta aracarkaḷiyum avvaracarkaḷiṉ camaya matippiṉiyum virivākak kūṟukiṉṟatu. ivvaṟaṅkaḷic ceytaruḷiyavarkaḷ nakarattārkaḷ; ātaliṉ nakarattār varalāṟṟiṉiyum perumiyiṉiyum ivvēṭukaḷ cuṭaṭikkāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.
nūṟṟukku mēṟpaṭṭa pakkam koṇṭa ivvēṭukaḷ pāṇṭiyar, cōḻar, maturi nāyakkar, civakaṅkiccīmi marutu, putukkōṭṭit toṇṭimāṉ mutaliyōrip paṟṟiya kuṟippukkaḷik koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ātaliṉ varalāṟṟāciriyar kaḷukkut tuṇi ceyyum eṉa nampukiṉṟēṉ. munnūṟṟimpatu āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ pēccut tamiḻppaṭi eḻutappaṭṭa ivvēṭukaḷ tamiḻmoḻi yārāycciyāḷarkkum nalla tuṇi ceyyum. camayam paṟṟiya pala marapukaḷ iruttaliṉ, civa camaya varalāṟṟiṟkum īṇṭuccāṉṟukaḷ uṇṭu. ivvaccu nūlil aṭaṅkiya ēṭukaḷiṉ vaki:-
paṭṭayattiṉ amippu muṟikaḷ
cāli, kali, tamiḻ eṉṟa mūṉṟuvaki āṇṭukaḷum tiṅkaḷ nāḷ kiḻami ōrikaḷum pala paṭṭayaṅkaḷil kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṉṉārukku iṉṉār muṉpāka iṉṉār koṭutta tarmacācaṉappaṭṭayam eṉṟu eḻutumuṟiyik kāṇkiṉṟōm. viḷakkamākavum muṭivil tokuttum cācaṉa nōkkam tarappaṭṭuḷḷatu. camayattuṟiyil yār yārkku eṉṉeṉṉa mariyātikaḷ, mariyāti varicikaḷ avvaricikkuriya kāraṇaṅkaḷ, aṟputa nikaḻccikaḷ, pūci naṭappukkaḷ, annaṭappukku vēṇṭum poruḷ taraṅkaḷ ellām aiyattiṟkum vaḻuviṟkum iṭamiṉṟik kuṟikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟikkuṟikkum nōkkamē ippaṭṭayaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiya nōkkamākum. oru paḻaṅ kiyeḻuttup puttakappaṭiyiliruntu innūl accākkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. eṉiṉum mūlappaṭṭayaṅkaḷ ēṭukaḷāka iruntaṉa eṉṟu aṟiyavēṇṭum.
ovvoru paṭṭayattiṟkum ettaṉi ēṭukaḷ eṉpataṉi mēl aṭṭavaṇiyiṟ kāṇalām. ivvaṟap paṇiyi ulakam uḷḷavari naṭattuvōm eṉavum, etu nili tirintālum ivvaṟanili tiriyōm eṉavum, ivvaṟattiṟku iṭiyūṟu ceytavarkaḷ iṉṉapaḻikku āḷāvārkaḷ eṉavum, utavi ceytavarkaḷ iṅṅaṉam vāḻvārkaḷ eṉavum iṟutiyil viṭātu kuṟippiṭuvatu ippaṭṭayaṅkaḷiṉ vaḻakkāṟākum. iccācaṉam eḻutiṉatu iṉṉār coṟpaṭikku iṉṉāṉ eṉṟu kāṭṭi muṭikkappaṭum.  yār eḻutiṉāl cācaṉa nōkkam valuppaṭumō avarikkoṇṭu eḻutaccollum aṟivuṭi miyip pārkkiṉṟōm. paṭṭayattiṉ toṭakkattilum kaṭiciyilum murukaṉ pāṭalkaḷ iruttaluṇṭu. iṉi ovvoru paṭṭayattiṉ karuttiṉiyum curukkamākak kāṇpōm.
1. paḻaṉikkōyil naṭappu aṭṭavaṇi (pakkam 1- 27)
nēmaṅkōyil iḷanala vakuppu kuppāpiccañ ceṭṭi makaṉ kumarappaṉ paḻaṉittalañ ceṉṟu uppu vaṇikañ ceytatu, paḻaṉikkōyil teyvanāyaka paṇṭārattu maṉiyil taṅkip paṇṭārattiṉ utaviyiyum avarmaṉivi, pārvatiyammāḷ utaviyiyum peṟṟatu. vēlāyuta cuvāmikku ilāpattil makamip paṇam cērttatu, piṟakōyil nakarac ceṭṭiyārkaḷum kumarappaṉōṭu uppu vāṇikattiṟkuc cērntu koṇṭatu. uppuc ceṭṭikaḷ eṉṟa peyar peṟṟatu. (kalāmaṭam)īcāṉiya civācāriyār kaṭṭaḷippaṭi kumarappaṉ paḻaṉikkuk kāvaṭi kaṭṭiyatu, iṉṉiṉṉa iṭattil taṅkic ceṉṟu,aintu nāḷ makēcuvara pūci ceyvatu, ēḻu kōvil nakarattārum kāvaṭi eṭuttatu. īcāṉiya kurutēcikar kumarappaṉ mutalāṉavar paciyip pōkkiyatu, pūcikkup piramputtaṭi aruḷiyatu, kumarappaṉ campaṭṭiyil nattaliṅka nāyakkar maṉiviyiṉ vayiṟṟuvaliyip paḻaṉiyappar aruḷāṟ pōkkiyatu. nāyakkar maṉivi kumarappaṉ vīṭu tēṭivantu naṉṟi celuttiyatu, nāyakkar iraṇṭu vēlāyutam paḻaṉiyapparukkuk koṭuttatu, vēlāyuta cuvāmi pārvatiyammāḷukkum kumarappaṉ peṇcāti ciṭṭāḷukkum īcāṉiyacivācāri yārukkum āyikkuṭi camīṉtārukkum kaṉavil tōṉṟic colliyatu, camīṉtāriṉ curanōyip pōkkiyatu, maṇṭakappaṭi naṭattuvatu, paṇṭārattu maṉiyi maṭamāka vittuk koḷvatu, paṇṭārattukkuk kuṭumpattārkkum vaḻi vaḻi naṉṟi maṟavātiruppatu eṉṟa karuttukkaḷum iṉṉārukku iṉṉa koṭuppatu eṉṟa kaṭṭaḷi varaṉmuṟikaḷum ivvaṭṭavaṇiyil collappaṭṭuḷa.
maṇṭakappaṭiyil paṇṭārattukku aritip parivaṭṭamum, kumarappaṉukku alaṅkārap parivaṭṭamum, nakarattārkaḷil mutalāvatu tirunīṟum kāḷāñciyum koṭuppatu eṉavum, nakarappotuviliruntu (kalāmaṭam) īcāṉiya kurutēcikarukkum (pātarakkuṭi) tiruppuṉal tēcikarukkum, teyvanāyaka paṇṭārattukkum, kumarappaṉukkum koṭuppatu eṉavum, iṉam oṉṟukkut tirunīṟṟuppi oṉṟu koṭuppatu eṉavum mariyātikaḷ variyaṟukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. (pak.24)
2. ēḻunakarattār tarumacācaṉam (pakkam 28 -42)
itu teyvacikāmaṇi paṇṭārattu makaṉ teyvanāyaka paṇṭārattukku ēḻu nakarattārum eḻutikkoṭutta tarumacācaṉam. cāli āṇṭu 1780 eṉpatu piḻiyeṉat tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. kaliyāṇṭōṭum piṟacācaṉaṅkaḷōṭum ottiṭṭup pārkkumpōtu, cāli 1520 eṉa iruttal vēṇṭum pōlum. yukaṅkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ, nakarattār perumikaḷ, ēḻu nakarattāriṉ irupattiraṇṭu vakuppukaḷ, teyvanāyaka paṇṭārattukkum aṉṉatāṉa maṭattut tiruviḻāviṟkum koṭukkavēṇṭum arici mutalāṉa poruḷaḷavukaḷ, tīrttamum tirunīṟum vaḻaṅkum mariyāti varicikaḷ, pattāntirunāḷ maṇṭakappaṭiyil koṭukkum poruḷaḷavukaḷ, taṅki varavēṇṭiya iṭaṅkaḷ ellām iccācaṉattu eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
cōḻavaracaṉ nakarattār peṇṇimaruvip poṟukka muṭiyāta tontaravu ceytapaṭiyāl, iḷiyāttaṅkuṭi vanta ceytiyiyum, araca taṇṭaṉikku okkūruṭiyāṉ maṟutali koṭuttapaṭiyāl nakarakkōyilkaḷil okkūruṭiyāṉukku mutal mariyāti naṭakkiṉṟa ceytiyiyum aṟikiṉṟōm; eṉiṉum, nēmam kumarappaṉ paḻaṉittalattukku uppu viṟkavantu paṭṭapirayāciyi matittu, paḻaṉittalattil mutal vipūtiyum kāḷāñciyum parivaṭṭamum kumarappaṉukku naṭakkaṭṭum eṉṟu (pak.38) eḻu nakarattār aṉivōrum orēmaṉatāyppēci muṭittaṉar eṉpatu iccācaṉattiṉ uyirāṉa karuttākum. iccācaṉam nakarattār coṟpaṭikku okkūruṭiyāṉ uṭiyappa ceṭṭi aṭikkappaṉ eḻutiyatu. okkūruṭiyāṉ taṉakku uriya mutal mariyātiyip paḻaṉittalattil nēmam kumarappaṉukku viṭṭukkoṭukka vēṇṭiyiruppatāl, iccācaṉatti okkūruṭiyāṉik koṇṭu eḻutac colliṉar nakarattār eṉa aṟiyavēṇṭum. ēḻu nakarattārum taṉittaṉi āṟu kāl kūṭattil kumarappaṉik kumpiṭṭu viḻuntu tirunīṟu vāṅkikkoḷvatu eṉṟa mariyātic ceytiyum, kumarappaṉ cantatiyil mutalvaruṭam paṭṭam kaṭṭukiṉṟa vivaramum aṟiyalām.
3. ēḻu nakarattār tarumacācaṉam (pakkam 42 - 65.)
itu cāli 1688 - kali 4868 (ki.pi.1766) ām āṇṭil kuḻanti paṇṭārattu makaṉ kumāracāmi paṇṭārattukku ēḻu nakarattārum aḷitta cācaṉappaṭṭayam. mēliccācaṉam pōla yukaṅkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷum nakarattār perumikaḷum pirivukaḷum paṇṭārattukkum kuṭumpa vaḻiyiṉarkkum koṭukkum kaṭṭaḷikaḷum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iraṇikkōyil pe. kuṭṭayaṉ vikāvūr nāṭṭu vicayakiri vēlāyutat turiyar kaḷukku āṇ kuḻanti piṟakkum eṉṟu colliyatu, turiyiṉ paṭṭattammāḷ kuṭṭayaṉiṭam makappēṟu kēṭṭatu, kuṉṉak kuṭiyil periyamarutu pāṇṭiyar mūṉṟu kēḷvi kēṭṭut takka viṭipeṟṟatu, kuṭṭayaṉukkuk kaṇiyāḻiyum yāṉik kompum koṭuttatu eṉṟa aṟputaṅkaḷi aṟiyalām.
vicayakirit turiyum marutupāṇṭiyarum uttara viṭṭapaṭi, iraṇikkōyil pe. kuṭṭayaṉukku aruḷāṭum kāraṇattāl, iraṇṭāvatu kāḷāñciyum mariyātik kāḷāñciyum koṭuppatu eṉṟa muṭivu iccācaṉattiṉ uyirāṉa ceytiyākum. mariyāti varicikaḷum kāraṇaṅkaḷum muṟikaḷum 59 - 62ām pakkam vari virivāka iṭam peṟṟuḷa. ippaṭṭayattil vicayanakarattu aracarkaḷiyum avarkaḷ vaḻiyāṇṭa nāyakkar kaḷiyum makāmaṇṭalēcuvaraṉ eḻupattēḻu pāḷiyam mutaliyavaṟṟiyum paṟṟic ciṟanta kuṟippukkaḷ uḷa (pak. 47 - 49)
4. ēḻu nakarattār tarumacācaṉam (pakkam 66 -78)
itu cāli 1710 kali 4889 (ki.pi. 1788) ām āṇṭil kumāracāmi paṇṭārattu makaṉ teyvanāyaka paṇṭārattukku ēḻu nakarattārum koṭutta taruma cācaṉappaṭṭayam. nakarattārkaḷiṉ perumikaḷ pirivukaḷ aintu nāḷ pūcikkum aṉṉatāṉa maṭattut tiruviḻāvukkum koṭukkum arici poṉ paḻam mutaliyavi, paḷḷayattukkuk koṭukkum poruḷ aḷavukaḷ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷa. veṟṟili pākku paḻam kāykaṟi kaḷōṭu pōyiliyum curuṭṭum koṭuppatuṇṭu eṉṟa oru putucceytiyi ippaṭṭayattil kaṟkiṉṟōm. nēmaṅkōvil kumarappaṉukkum iraṇikkōyil kuṭṭayaṉukkum piṟarukkum uriya mariyāti varicikaḷum kāraṇaṅkaḷum 73 -76 ām pakkaṅkaḷil iṭampeṟṟuḷa. teyvanāyaka paṇṭārattu maṉiyil muṉpirunta āṟukāl kūrik kūṭattik kaltiruppaṇi ceytu āṟukāl cavukkiyākkiyatum, 78 kāl tiruppaṇi maṇṭapam ceytatum kuṟikkattakka muṉṉēṟṟaṅ kaḷām. iṅṅaṉam nakarattār celavu ceytu paṇi perukki ṉālum aṉupavikkum urimi paṇṭārattu vaḻiyiṉarkku eṉṟum uṇṭu. vayiravaṉ kōyil vēlappa ceṭṭi vayiravaṉ tiruppaṇi vēliyil mikkamuyaṟci eṭuttuk koṇṭataṉālum, cuvāmi vayiravaṉiṭam nēril tōṉṟip pēciyataṉālum (pak.76) nakarattārkaḷil mūṉṟāvatu mariyāti vayiravaṉukku uriyatu eṉpatu iccācaṉattiṉ uyirāṉa ceytiyākum.
5. piṟāmali ātīṉam naṭappu aṭṭavaṇi (pakkam 79 -85)
paḻaṉikkōyil iṭumpaṉmali mutal ūr varum variyil īcāṉiya civācāriyār, paṇṭāram, kumarappaṉ nakarattār ceyyavēṇṭiya muṟikaḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷa. iṉṉārukku iṉṉa kaṭami eṉṟu caṉṉitāṉam colliyapōtu ātīṉa mariyāti taṉakkum vēṇṭum eṉṟu iraṇikkōyil tiruvēṭpū ruṭiyāṉ kuṭṭayaṉ kēṭṭāṉ. maṟṟavarkaḷik kalantālōcittu, tiruvāviṉaṉkuṭiyil naṭakkiṟa paṭṭuparivaṭṭamum antac celavum kuṭṭayaṉ taṉatāka irukkaṭṭum eṉṟu cuvāmi colliyatu. caṉṉitāṉam ūrukku varumpōtu kuṭṭayaṉukku mūṉṟāvatu paṭṭup pītāmparam koṭukkiṉṟatu.
6. ātīṉam paṭṭāpiṭēka aṭṭavaṇi (pakkam 86 -90)
kumarappaṉ paḻaṉiyāṇṭavar aṉukkirakam peṟṟa paṭiyāṉum, ātīṉattukku vēṇṭum kāriyaṅkaḷum uḻippum ceytapaṭiyālum ātīṉappaṭṭāpiṭēkattu. iraṇṭāvatākak kumarappaṉ paṭṭu cāttukiṉṟatu eṉṟu īcāṉakurutēcikar moḻintār. itaṉi irupattoru kōttirattu nakarattār kaḷum cariyeṉa ottukkoṇṭaṉar. vaḻakkampōl naṭakka vēṇumēyoḻiyap putuvaḻakkam ceyyappaṭātu eṉṟu iḷiyāttāṅkuṭi paṭṭaṇacuvāmi vakuppu aruṇācalañ ceṭṭi aṇṇāmali maṭṭum caṉṉitāṉattiṉ tiruvaṭiyil viḻuntu maṟuppuk kūṟiṉār. kumarappaṉ perumiyik kurutēcikar eṭutturittapiṉ aṇṇāmaliyum ottuk koṇṭār. ottukkoṇṭataṟku uṟutiyāka aṇṇā maliyiyē aṭṭavaṇi eḻutumpaṭi īcāṉiya civācāriyār kaṇittār.
7. maṭavālayat tarumacācaṉam (pakkam 91 - 100)
itu cāli 1727 kali 4906 (ki.pi.1805) āṇṭil nākappa paṇṭārattu makaṉ kuḻantivēlup paṇṭārattukku ēḻunakarattār aṉivōrum koṭutta cācaṉappaṭṭaya mākum. muṉṉiccācaṉaṅkaḷ pōlap paṇṭārattukkuk koṭukkum naṭappukkaḷum piṟavum kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷa. iṭam pōtāmiyiṉāl aṭuttamaṉi mōḷiyattēvaṉ vakiyaṟā iṭatti vilikku vāṅki ākkupuriyum muppattoṉpatu kāl tiruppaṇi maṇṭapamum kaṭṭiya vaḷarcci kuṟippiṭattakum. maṭattiṟkāka vāṅkiya immaṉikaḷum paṇṭārattiṉ anupōkattiṟku uriyaṉavē piḷḷiyārkōyil vakuppu muttappaṉukkuc civappaṭṭiyil paḻaṉiyāṇṭavaruṭiya aruḷvanta ceytiyum, toṇṭimāṉ aracar muttappaṉ vīṭṭiṟku vantu niṉitta kāriyattik kēṭṭu makiḻntu cālvi mutaliyaṉa koṭutta ceytiyum, muttappaṉ kēṭṭuk koṇṭapaṭi kōvil vīṭṭukkut tāmpūravēl koṭutta ceytiyum, nēmaṅkōvil paḻaṉiyappaṉ paḻaṉikkōvilukkuk kāvaṭi eṭuttu varumpōtu inta vēlāyutattiyum vittuppūci ceytuvarumpaṭi colliya ceytiyum, itaṉic colvataṟkākap paḻaṉiyappaṉi araṇmaṉikku aḻittuvantu mariyāti ceyta ceytiyum ippaṭṭayattukku mukkiya ceytikaḷākum. (pak. 96 -98) tēvakōṭṭi rattiṉavēlāyutamum kuṉṟakkuṭi vēlāyutamum cērttukkoḷḷappaṭṭaṉa.
8. nirampavaḻakiya tēcikar paṭṭayam (pakkam 100 -116)
itu cāli 1549 kali 4730 (ki.pi.1627) ām āṇṭil ēḻu nakarattār muṉpākak kuṉṟakkuṭi ātīṉam nirampa vaḻaṅkiya tēcikar (tuḷāvūr maṭam) koṭutta paṭṭayam. yukaṅkaḷiṉ peyar kaḷum nāyakkar peyarkaḷum muṉṉark kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tēcikar perumi, nakarattār perumi, kōttiram, kulacēkarapura cavuntarapāṇṭiyaṉ nakarattārkaḷi naṟkuṭiyākat taṉ cīmikku aḻittatu, nakarattāriṉ veḷḷāṭṭip peṇkaḷ cōḻaṉ uttira viṭṭum (kalāmaṭam) īcāṉiya kurutēcikari toḻa maṟuttatu eṅkaḷ nirampavaḻakiya kurutēcikar (tuḷāmaṭam) pāṇṭināṭṭiṟku vantāl nāṅkaḷum pōvōm eṉṟu urittatu, veḷḷāṭṭip peṇkaḷ taṅkaḷ kuruviṭam muṟiyiṭṭatu, cōḻaṉ kurutēcikari varavaḻittup pāṇṭiyaṉ pala kirāmaṅkaḷ taruvāṉ eṉṟu moḻintu irupattiraṇṭu kōttirattu nakarattār āṇkaḷum veḷḷāṭṭip peṇkaḷum pāṇṭi nāṭṭiṟku vantatu, pāṇṭiyaṉ ellōriyum etir koṇṭaḻittu mākāṇaṅkaḷ carvamāṉiyamāka aḷittatu. kuṉṟakkuṭi ātīṉam amintatu mutalāṉa nakarattār varalāṟṟuk kuṟippukkaḷi ippaṭṭayattut teḷivāka aṟiyalām.
nēmaṅkōyil kumarappaṉum avaṉ maṉivi veḷḷāṭṭi ciṭṭāḷum uppumāṟi eṭutta makamiyāl inta ātīṉamum piṟa tarumaṅkaḷum naṭipeṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉavum, paḻaṉittalattilpōla intattalattilum nakarattārkaḷil mutalāvatu tīrttam tirunīṟu kumarappaṉukkuk koṭuppatu eṉavum nirampavaḻakiya tēcikar uttaravuppaṭi nakarattār aṉivōrum uṭaṉpaṭṭa ceytiyi ippaṭṭayam mukkiyamākac collukiṉṟatu. mariyāti varicikaḷ 114 -15 ām pakkaṅkaḷil uḷḷaṉa.
paṭṭayaṅkaḷum naṭimuṟikaḷum
paṭṭayaṅkaḷil kuṟittuḷ naṭappukkaḷ iṉṟum perumpālum mēṟkoḷḷappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. kālappōkkil cila māṟṟaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭirukkakkūṭum. ellāp paṭṭayaṅkaḷilum mutal mariyātikkuriyavarāṉa nēmam kumarappaṉ vaḻi vantōrē iṉṟu neṟkuppi ku.paḻa. vīṭṭiṉarāvar. iraṇṭām mariyātikku uriya aruḷāṭi iraṇikkōyil pe.kuṭṭayaṉ vaḻivantōrē iṉṟu kaṇṭaṉūr kupa.cu. vīṭṭiṉar nakarattārkaḷil mūṉṟām mariyātikku uriya vayiravaṉkōyil vayiravaṉ vaḻivantōrē iṉṟu kārikkuṭi kuma.peri.cu.paḻa. vīṭṭiṉar. tāmpūravēl peṟṟa piḷḷiyōr kōvil muttappaṉ vaḻivantavarē iṉṟu mēliccivapuri āṟu.muru. kuṭumpattiṉar iṅṅaṉam teyva aruḷpeṟṟa nakarattār nālvar vaḻivantōr ālpōl taḻittu vāḻntu varutaliṉ, muṉṉōr paṇikaḷ iṭiyaṟātu kākkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟa uṇartal vēṇṭum. ikkuṭumpattārkaḷ tam kuḻantikaḷukku murukaṉ peyarkaḷiyē vippar eṉpatum niṉiyattakum.
paṭṭayaṅkaḷum tamiḻum
ēṟakkuṟiya munnūṟu āṇṭukaṭku muṉtōṉṟiya iccācaṉat tamiḻ palavakiyālum ūṉṟi ārāyvataṟku uriyatu eṉṟu maṭṭum īṇṭuc cuṭṭa virumpukiṉṟēṉ. tamiḻ urinaṭi varalāṟṟil iccācaṉa naṭi iṭampeṟat takuvatu. oru perum puttaka aḷaviṟku oppāṉa iccācaṉap paṭṭayaṅkaḷ pēcuvatu pōṉṟa tamiḻ naṭiyil eḻutappaṭṭuḷa. oṇṇu, reṇṭu, mūṇu, paṉireṇṭu, aruci, kuṭuttu, rācā, pōyi, tuṇṇuttuppi, matiyāri, ciṟicākkiṉi, vīṭṭukkup pōratu, pokkucam, naccēttiram eṉappala marūuccoṟkaḷi nirampakkāṇalām. akkālattut tamiḻccoṟkaḷi eṅṅaṉam olittārkaḷ eṉṟum aṟintu koḷḷalām. māṟi (viṟṟu) atumērikku (atupōla) paraṅkaḷ (paratēcikaḷ) vaḷami (vaḻakkam) peṇṇāṭṭi (peṇṭāṭṭi) neṭṭeḻuttu (kiyeḻuttu) eṉṟa coṟkaḷiyum, aṭakeṭuttal, aritipparivaṭṭam, alaṅkārap parivaṭṭam, vēṟpaṭi, aruḷāṭi mutalāṉa coṟkaḷiyum aṟikiṉṟōm. uṇṭumā, meḻukivittuvi. nāṉum varuka, varuvōmākavum eṉṟa toṭar muṭipukaḷiyum aṟikiṉṟōm.
nakarattārkaḷ oḷaviyiṉ tamiḻppāṭṭukkup poṟpāṭakam koṭuttārkaḷ eṉavum, varappuyara eṉṟu oḷavi uḻavarkaḷi vāḻttiyatu pōla maṭiccīli peruka eṉṟu vaṇikañceyyum innakarattārkaḷi vāḻtti varaṅkoṭuttār eṉavum ippaṭṭayaṅkaḷ viḷampukiṉṟaṉa. nāyakka maṉṉaṉ muttamiḻ aṇaṅkaṉ eṉavum, vaḷḷuvamaṟiyuṅ kaṟṟu muppāl moḻiyumpaṭiyē aṟivi aṟintu allavi kaṭintu nallavi nāṭṭik kuṭitaḻikka āṇṭāṉ eṉavum moḻikiṉṟaṉa. nāṅkaḷ ēḻu nakarattārkaḷ naṭṭakalliyum iṭṭacūlamum aḻikka māṭṭōm; makāmērukiri cāyntapōtilum, kaṭal ēḻum vaṟṟiṉa pōtilum, kāvēri vaṟaṇṭa pōtilum, cantirāḷ cūriyāḷ teṟku vaṭakkāṉapōtilum nāṅkaḷ nakaram poycolla māṭṭōm eṉavarum uṟutippāṭukaḷ nakarattāriṉ nāṇayattip paṟicāṟṟukiṉṟaṉa.
ūḻi peyariṉum tām peyarār cāṉṟāṇmikku
āḻi yeṉappaṭu vār
eṉṟa vaḷḷuvamaṟippaṭi vāḻntavarkaḷ nakarattārkaḷ eṉpataṉi inta aṟappaṭṭayaṅkaḷ kāṭṭikkoṇṭu niṟkiṉṟaṉa. paṇṭiyōr tam poyyāp paṇpiṉiyum kaṭṭuppāṭṭiṉiyum pukaḻiṉiyum oḻuṅkiṉiyum aṟitaṟku ippaṭṭayanūl ārāycciyāḷar kippaṭṭupparavum eṉṟu etirpārkkiṉṟēṉ. nakarattār camutāyam tamiḻpperuñ camutāyattiṉ oru pakutiyātaliṉ, ippaṭṭayaṅkaḷ tamiḻiṉattiṉ nākarika vaḷarcciyik kāṇa muyalvārkkut tuṇiceyyum eṉṟu cuṭṭikkāṭṭa virumpukiṉṟēṉ.
peṟutaṟkariya tammuṉṉōriṉ tarumap paṭṭayaṅkaḷi yāvarum aṟiyum vaṇṇam accu nūlvaṭivākki utaviya tiru.ku.paḻa.cu.paḻa.cuppiramaṇiya ceṭṭiyār avarkaḷukkut tamiḻulakam mikka kaṭappāṭu uṭiyatu. ellā nakarattār kaḷuṭiya poruḷutaviyiṉālum ivveḷiyīṭu varukiṉṟatu. nakarattārkaḷukku ellāt tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ naṉṟiyuriyatu.
