nītinūlkaḷ
 māṇikkaṉār vakutta eḷiya uri
mūtaṟiñar va.cupa. māṇikkaṉār uḷḷam, col, ceyalkaḷāl tūyar. nāṭu nalam peṟa naṟkaruttukkaḷi nāḷum eṇṇi eḻutiyavar. ciṟuvar neñcakaḷil naṟkaruttukkaḷ nāḷum vitikkap peṟal vēṇṭum eṉṟa nalla nōkkiṉar. ātti cūṭi amippil ‘tamiḻccūṭi’ iyaṟṟic ciṟuvar uḷḷamum vāḻvum ceḻikka vaḻivaki kaṇṭavar. nalla karuttukkaḷip parappum nōkkil ‘māṇikkak kuṟaḷ’ iyaṟṟiya māṇpiṉar. tirukkuṟaḷ cintaṉikaḷit teḷivāka uṇartta cevviya uri kaṇṭa cemmal.
kārikkuṭiyil tamiḻccaṅkam amittu ataṉ talivarāy viḷaṅkiya māṇikkaṉār tamiḻp pāṭalkaḷiyum nīti nūlkaḷiyum ciṟuvarkaḷ neñcakattil eṉṟum nili niṟutta virumpiṉār. ātticūṭi, koṉṟi vēntaṉ, naṟuntoki, mūturi, nalvaḻi, naṉṉeṟi, ulaka nīti ākiya nūlkaḷukku eḷiya teḷivuri vakuttut tamiḻc caṅka veḷiyīṭākap pala patippukkaḷ varac ceytār.  tamiḻccaṅkam mūlam nīti nūl oppuvittal pōṭṭi naṭatti āṇṭutōṟum ciṟuvarkaḷukkut toṭarntu paricukaḷ nalkiṉār. vaḷarum payirukku uramum nīrum oḷiyum aḷittuk kāppatil vallavar. ceṟivu naṭiyum pulami naṭiyum valla māṇikkaṉār ciṟupar poruṭṭu mika mika ḷayi tamiḻil paḻaku naṭiyil uri vakuttuḷḷār.
nīti nūl karuttukkaḷ parava vēṇṭum eṉṟa eṇṇattiṟkuc ceyal vaṭivam koṭuttār. kārikkuṭi tamiḻc caṅkattip payaṉpaṭuttik koṇṭār. payiṟṟu vittār. paricukaḷ nalkiṉār. nīti nūlkaḷukku eḷiya uri vakuttār.
ceyal ceyal eṉa vāḻnta cemmal. vāyccol vīrarāka vāḻāmal colliyavaṇṇam ceytu muṭitta perumakaṉārākavum tikaḻntār.
ivar, kaṟṟal, kaṟpittal mutaliya kalvi neṟikaḷil cīriya kōṭpāṭukaḷuṭaṉ vāḻnta pērāciriyar. ivaratu eḻuttum pēccum ivartam kalvik koḷkikaḷip paṟicāṟṟu kiṉṟaṉa. tūya vāḻviḷarākiya mūtaṟiñar naṉmuṟiyil nīti nūl iyaṟṟattiṭṭamiṭṭiruntār. ivaratu vāḻkkik kuṟippu itaṉi uṇarttukiṟatu. ciṟuvarkaḷukkuriya nīti nūlkaḷip paṟṟi pērāciriyar eḻutiya āyvuk kaṭṭuri peruñ ciṟappuṭiyatu.
iḷiñarkaḷukku uṭal, uḷḷam, uyir paṟṟiya nalvaḻik karuttukkaḷ, nalvāḻvuk kōṭpāṭukaḷ tamiḻil evvāṟu collappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa eṉpati nīḷa niṉintavar. naṉku ārāyntavar. tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkōṭu tamiḻ aṟakkōṭ pāṭukaḷum vaḷarum kuḻantikaḷ neñcil patiya vippatil ceyal ārvam kāṭṭiyavar.

nītinūlkaḷ paṟṟi māṇikkaṉār uḷḷam uvantu iyaṟṟiya ippāṭali eṉṟum neñcil niṟuttuvōm.
tamiḻar vāḻvunalak kōṭpāṭukaḷi ēḻu nītinūlkaḷum ilakkiya iṉpamuṟum vakiyil eṭuttiyampum tiṟattiṉi ivartam ārāycci muṉṉuriyil aṟintu koḷḷalām. pērāciriyariṉ kaṉavu naṉavākat tamiḻnāṭṭut toṭakkap paḷḷitōṟum mīṇṭum nītinūl kalvi kaṭṭāyappaṭuttappaṭa vēṇṭum. inta ariya nalla paṇiyic ceyta pērāciriyarkkut tamiḻ ulakam kaṭamippaṭṭuḷḷatu. nītinūlkaḷip pala patippukkaḷ veḷiyiṭṭup parappiya kārikkuṭi tamiḻccaṅkac ceyalāḷar mā.a. ñāṉa cēkarapāṇṭiyaṉ avarkaḷukkum va.cupa.mā. eḻuttukkaḷ aṉittum accil kāṇa avāvi niṟkum aṇṇal māṇikkaṉāriṉ iḷaval va.cupa.cokkaliṅkam avarkaḷukkum māṇikkaṉāriṉ aṉittu nūlkaḷiṉ patippuc cemmikku uṟutuṇiyāy irukkum vāḻum va.cupa.māvākiya ṭākṭar irā.cāraṅkapāṇi avarkaḷukkum eṅkaḷ neñcārnta naṉṟi.
māṇikkaṉār eḻuttukkaḷ aṉittiyum ‘māṇikka nūl varici’yil veḷiyiṭṭu makiḻntōm. inta āṇṭu tirukkuṟaḷukku māṇikka uriyiṉip patippittup pēruvaki koṇṭōm. toṭarntu māṇikkak kuṟaḷi veḷiyiṭṭu maṭṭilā makiḻcciyuṟṟōm. māṇikkaṉār iḷiñar kaḷukkākavē eḷiya muṟiyil eḻutiya nītinūlkaḷiṉ urikaḷip patippittup pēruvaki koḷkiṟōm.


The Tamil Concept of Love
 Preface
I deem it my sincere duty to present before the scholars of the world the noble principles of Aham Literature whose origin is as old as Tamil language itself and whose influence on all kinds of Tamil literature of all periods is incalculable. An elementary knowledge of Aham is indeed essential even for a beginner in Tamil. Without its study, Tamil culture and civilisation will be a sealed book.
Love is no doubt the common theme of any literature in any language. The selective nature of the love-aspects, the impersonal and algebric form of the characters and the universal and practical treatment of the subject are the differentiating points of Ahattini.
What is the impulse behind the creation of Aham literature? The unity of the family is the bed-rock of the unity of the world. The achievement of that conjugal unity depends upon the satisfaction of the sexual congress between the rightful lovers in youthhood. Dissatisfaction unconsciously disintegrates the family. There will be few problems in society, religion and politics, if family life is a  contented one and the husband and wife pay high regard to each other’s sexual hunger. Therefore sex education is imperative to every young man and woman before and after marriage.
How to educate them? The ancient Tamils saw in literature an effective and innocent means for instructing boys and girls in sexual principles and sexual experiences and with that noble motive created a well-defined literature called Ahattini with inviolable rules. I hope the study of this book will be highly useful for promoting good relations between young lovers, whatever their race and nationality may be.
I am greatful to the authorities of the University of Madras for their kind permission to publish my thesis (“Love in Sangam Poetry”) submitted by me for the Ph.D. Degree in 1956.
My thanks are due to my colleagues Lieut. U. Bala Subramaniam M.A., and Thiru R. Sarangapani M.A., M. Litt. for their assistance in publishing this book.
I shall always cherish the selfless service of the Saiva Siddhanta Works Publishing Society for coming forward to publish my work.
‘KATHIRAKAM’			     						V. Sp. MANICKAM,
Karikudi-2
15-5-1962
Collected Papers
Preface
The research papers presented and key-note addresses delivered in seminars and conferences and articles contributed to journals and souvenirs by me on different occassions are now being collected and published in the form of a book entitled collected papers. It is natural for any author to enjoy a delightful feeling when the stray flowers of his papers are strung together into a garland of book.
These seventeen papers are related to the varied aspects of Tamil Language. litereature, history and culture. It is gratifying to note that Tolkappiyam, the earliest Tamil work finds a place in many papers.
The author will feel rewarded if the discerning readers find that his views regarding the orderly and continued growth of Tamil are worthy of consideration and discussion.
My thanks are due to Thiru. C. Kathirvelu, Research Scholar, Annamali University for going through the proof and M/s. Sivakami Printers for the neat printing and execution.
			     V. Sp. MANICKAM
	T.D.A.R. Centre, Karikudi-2
15-6-1970

A  study Of Tamil Verbs
 Preface
It really gives me immense pleasure to publish after a long spell of twenty four years, my research thesis “Tamil Verbs” approved by the University of Madras for the award of M.O.L. Degree in 1948. I  am aware that most of the philological terms used in this thesis are not now in vogue and that new linguistic terms have replaced them. Notwithstanding the change of terminology, I do hope that the collection of material bearing on the several aspects of verbs in Tmil and the conclusions based on them are still valid in the field of linguistics. This prompted me to make this thesis avilable to researchers in print.
I feel honoured by the favour shown to me by the Vice-Chancellor and members of the Syndicate of the Annamali University for sanctioning publication of this book in the University series. I am very grateful to them for their kindness.
It is my duty to record my sincere obligation to the late Dr. A. Chidambaranathan Chettiar, former Professor of Tamil in this University for his fruitful guidance in this research work of mine.
I wish to express my thanks to Dr. S. Agesthialingom, Director of the Centre of Advanced Study in Linguistics, Annamali University for his valuable Foreword. My thanks are due to Dr. S.V. Shanmugam, Reader in Linguistics for going through the thesis before publication and for his useful suggestions and to Thiru K. Narayanan, M.A., Lecturer in Tamil for preparing Index.
V.Sp. Manickam
Annamalinagar, 12-10-72



tirumuṟi tilliyampalam ēṟiyatu!
tēvāra tiruvācaka mutalāṉa teyvat tirumuṟikaḷit tilli naṭarācar kōyilil kīḻē niṉṟutāṉ pāṭum vaḻakkam ituvari iruntu vantatu. tirumuṟikaḷi ampalattiṉ mēl pāṭa kurukkaḷ aṉumatippatilli. itu tirumuṟikku matippuk kuṟivāka iruntatu. maturip palkalik kaḻaka muṉṉāḷ tuṇivēntar. va.cupa.māṇikkaṉār talimiyil tirumuṟi iyakkakkuḻu pōrāṭat toṭaṅkiyatu. tirumuṟikaḷ kuṭamuḻukku viḻāviṟkuḷ tilliyampalam ēṟāviṭṭāl uṇṇā nōṉpu toṭaṅkavum muṭivu ceytatu. makkaḷum, māṇavarkaḷum tiraḷāka itaṟku ātaravu tantaṉar. tamiḻaka aracum ikkōrikkikku ottuḻittatu.
kōṭṭāṭci uyar aluvalarkaḷ potuk kuḻu kurukkaḷiṭam tamiḻukkum tirumuṟikkum uriya matippuk koṭukka vēṇṭum eṉavum 11.2.87 ām nāḷ naṭipeṟum kuṭamuḻukku iṭiyūṟiṉṟi naṭipeṟa vēṇṭum eṉavum eṭuttuc coṉṉārkaḷ. uṇmi niliyi uṇarnta kurukkaḷ perumakkaḷ icintaṉar. 1.2.87 ām nāḷ mutal tirumuṟikaḷ tilliyampalattiṉ mēl kurukkaḷāl pāṭap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. itu varalāṟṟuc ciṟappu mikka māṟṟamākum. itu pallāyirak kaṇakkāṉa makkaṭku makiḻcciyiyum uṇarcciyiyum tantatu.
teyvat tamiḻt tirumuṟi talimiyāṉa tillik kōyilil ampalam ēṟi muḻaṅkat toṭaṅki viṭṭatu. iṉi ellāk kōyilkaḷum tamiḻ vaḻipāṭṭit tāṉē virivil mēṟkoḷḷum eṉavum itaṟku ōr iyakkam tēvippaṭātu eṉavum nampukiṉṟōm. makkaḷiṉ tamiḻp pakkuvattiyum araciṉ tamiḻuṟutiyiyum kōyilāḷarkaḷ purintu ceyaṟpaṭuvārkaḷāka

talivar, tirumuṟi iyakkakkuḻu.
tamiḻiṉ poṟkālam
irupattōrām nūṟṟāṇṭukku muntiyē, irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutikkālattē, tamiḻukkup poṟkālam toṭaṅkiviṭṭatu eṉṟuteḷikiṉṟēṉ. āṉanta cutantiram aṭintu viṭṭōm eṉṟu āṭuvōmē paḷḷup pāṭuvōmē’ eṉap pāṭṭukkoru pulavaṉ pārati teḷintu,iṟanta kāla naṭiyiṟ pāṭiṉāṉ; atu poykkavilli. nāṭuviṭutali peṟṟa varalāṟu aṟivōm tolkāppiya marapiṉpaṭi etirkālattut teḷivum nampikkiyum koṇṭa meymmikaḷi iṟanta kālattuk kuṟippoṭu kiḷattalē tamiḻ naṭiyākum. innaṭippaṭi ñālam aḷanta tol putut  tamiḻ moḻi maḻalip paḷḷi mutal palkalik kaḻakam vari orē payiṟṟumoḻiyāka ākiviṭṭatu eṉavum kalikkalvi, aṟiviyal kalvi, toḻiliyaṟ kalvi ellāttuṟiyilum tamiḻ oṉṟē tamiḻakattil payiṟṟu moḻiyāyiṟṟu eṉavum tamiḻvaḻik kaṟṟavarkkē uyarkalvic cērkki uṇṭu eṉavum tamiḻvaḻip payiṉṟavarkkē tamiḻakattil ellā vēli vāyppukaḷum uṇṭu eṉavum naṉavu kāṇpōmāka!
miyap pārāḷ maṉṟaṅkaḷil tamiḻum āṭci moḻiyākum urimi vāyppup peṟum eṉavum miyavaracōṭu tamiḻilum eḻuttuṟavu naṭipeṟum eṉavum ayalakat tūtaraṅkaḷilum tamiḻ nalliṭampeṟum eṉavum irupattōrām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattilēyē cīriḷamit tiṟam vāynta centamiḻiṉa kural aikkiya nāṭṭaviyiṉ araṅkil muḻaṅkum eṉavum nampuvōmāka. innampikki vēṇavāvāl collappaṭuvataṉṟu; tamiḻ itaṟkellām itaṟku mēlākavum takutip pēṟuṭiya celva moḻi eṉpataṉi ulakam aṟiyum. ulaka moḻikaḷōṭu tamiḻiṉ varalāṟṟi oppiṭuvār tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ uḷḷāṟṟali naṉkaṟivar. toṉmiyil kuṟivā? amippil kuṟivā? ilakkaṇa varampil kuṟivā? nūṟcelvattil kuṟivā? olit tūymiyil kuṟivā? nam tāymoḻiyiṉ mēṉmiyi aṟiyakillāta tamiḻp pāvikaḷiṉ kuṟivoṉṟit tavira tamiḻukku eṉṉa kuṟivuṇṭu? niṟivik kuṟiveṉṟu tūṟṟum maṭavōrkaḷiṉ aṭimiyit tavirat tamiḻiṉ cemmikku eṉṉa kuṟipāṭu uṇṭu? ikkuṟi kūṟuvār ellām irupattōrām nūṟṟāṇṭil uṇmiyi uṇarntu aṟivu cemmippaṭṭu urimi pūṇṭu tamiḻk kaṭami ceyvar eṉṟu nampuvōmāka! yāmaṟinta moḻikaḷilē tamiḻ moḻi pōl iṉitāvatu eṅkum kāṇōm eṉṟu kavippaṟiyaṟinta pārati vākku kuṟaḷ vākkaṉṟō? eṅkum kāṇōm eṉpateṉṉa eṉṟum kāṇōm eṉṟa nilikkut tamiḻ iṉṉum cila āṇṭukaḷilē uyarvaḷarcci peṟum. takutiyuṭiya vallāḷaṉi yārum aṭakki vikka muṭiyātatupōlat takuti mikka tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ pūtavāṟṟali āṅkila mōkaṅkoṇṭa ettamiḻk kūṭṭamum taṭuttuviṭa muṭiyātu. muṭiyavē muṭiyātu. `tamiḻ eṅkaḷ uyirukku nēr’ eṉṟa moḻi vīrarkaḷ āṅkilap pācamum vācamum paṟṟiya evvaḷavu tāy tantiyar taṭuttālum, tamiḻmoḻiyit tamiḻakattil ellāt tuṟiyilum muṭimoḻiyākki veṟṟi kāṇpar; poruḷātāra vāḻvellām tamiḻvaḻikkē; piḻippu vāypellām tamiḻvaḻikkē; koḷviṉi koṭuppiṉiyellām tamiḻ vaḻikkē eṉṟu tamiḻmoḻi maṟavarkaḷ iṉṉum cilavāṇṭil moḻi vāki cūṭuvar. aṉṉit tamiḻē vēli peṟu; āṅkilamē veḷiyēṟu eṉṟa iṭikkural muḻaṅkat toṭaṅkiviṭṭatu. ittamiḻk kuṟikkōḷil nām ellām oṉṟātal kaṇṭu, eṅkum tamiḻ etilum tamiḻellā vēliyum tamiḻukkē eṉṟa putukkural kēṭṭu āṅkila mōka māyikoṇṭa pakivar iṉṉum cilavāṇṭiliruppiṭam teriyātu eṅkō maṟintu viṭuvar; palar `tirunti viṭuvar. tamiḻ vāḻum vaḷarum kalvi uṟuppeṟum; ellāruma kalviccelvam peṟuvar; pārata nāṭutamiḻ vaḷarcciyāl uyarvu peṟum. itaṉālaṉṟō tēciyam pāṭiya pāratiyār vāḻiya centamiḻ vāḻka naṟṟamiḻar; vāḻiya pārata maṇit tirunāṭu’ eṉṟu kāraṇa kāriya muṟippaṭi pāṭiṉār. ippāṭṭiṉ uṇmi eṉṉa teriyumā? pāratat tirunāṭṭiṉ vāḻvu tāṉum centamiḻiṉ vāḻvic cārntirukkiṉṟatu; tamiḻ vāḻvu kuṉṟiṉāl pāratattiṉ tiruvumkuṉṟiviṭum eṉṟa kuṟippup pulappaṭavilliyā? pāratiyi uṇarātavar pāratattiyē uṇarātavar āvar.
eṅkum tamiḻ entuṟiyum tamiḻ evvēliyum tamiḻukkē eṉṟu nām collumpōtu, iru karuttukkaḷit teḷiya vēṇṭum intiyāvil uḷḷa teluṅku, pañcāpi, kucarattu, inti mutaliya ēṉimoḻikaḷ tattam iṭaṅkaḷil muḻuvurimi peṟṟu vaḷara vēṇṭuma eṉpatuvē namkoḷki, iṉṟu āṅkila ātikkam intiyāviṉ enta moḻikaḷiyum kālattukkēṟṟa uyarmoḻiyākavum vēli vāyppu moḻiyākavum vaḷaraviṭavilli intiyarkaḷi vaḷarkkavum intiya moḻikaḷi vaḷarkkavumtāṉ intiyā urimi peṟṟatu. intiyarkaḷ āṅkilatti vaḷarkkavum āṅkilattāl oru cila intiyarkaḷ vaḷaravum nām viṭutali peṟavilli. viṭutalik kaṭami eṉṉa eṉpati nām uṇarntāka vēṇṭum. perumakkaḷ evvaḷavutāṉ poruḷātārattil vaḷarvatu pōlat tōṉṟiṉālum, makkaḷ moḻi urimiyum vaḷarcciyum vēlivāyppum, peṟātavari, cintaṉiyaṭimiyākattāṉ irukkum urimipeṟṟu nāṟpatu āṇṭukaḷākiyum nām iṉṉum aṭimic cintaṉiyāḷarkaḷākat tāṉ uyir vittuk koṇṭirukkiṉṟōm. namvaḻiyiṉariyum āṅkila aṭimit taṉattiṟ paḻakkik koṇṭirukkiṟōm. namnāṭṭil uyarkalvi kaṟṟavarkaḷ ellām maṉattālum naṭimuṟiyālum ilaṇṭaṉiyum amerikkāviyumtāṉ tāy nāṭākak karutukiṉṟārkaḷ. aṅkuk kuṭiyurimi peṟuvatittāṉ virumpukiṉṟārkaḷ. āṅkila mōkam āṅkila nāṭukaḷiṉ mōkamāki intiyat tēciyap pakiyākiviṭṭatu. veḷippaṭṭa puṟappakiyi viṭa āṅkila mōkaṅkoṇṭa uṭpakiyē intiyat tēciyattiṟku aṇukkuṇṭākum. āṅkila mōkam aḻiyum nāḷ ennāḷō annāḷ intiyattēciyam vāḻum nāḷākum. ātaliṉ irupattōrām nūṟṟāṇṭil intiya moḻikaḷ ellām maḻali mutal palkali vari uriya iṭaṅkaḷil payiṟṟu moḻikaḷ ākum eṉavum ellāvēli vāyppum intiya moḻi vaḻiyiṉarkkē kiṭikkum eṉavum naṉavu koḷvōmāka!
iṉṟiya ulakac cūḻalil piṟa moḻiyum ayal moḻiyum kaṟpatu iṉṟiyamiyātatu eṉṟa karuttil eṉakku vēṟṟumiyilli. tāy moḻiyi ellāttuṟiyilum mutaṉmiyākak kaṟpatōṭu. cilavāṇṭukaṭkuppiṉ ayal moḻikaḷiyum kaṟkum vāyppu irukka vēṇṭum. ayal moḻikaḷilum āṅkilam oṉṟukkē taṉi mutaṉmi koṭukkāmal uruciyam pireñcu, cerumaṉi, pāṉicu, cappāṉ mutalāṉa moḻikaḷiyum intiyap piṟamoḻikaḷiyum naṉku kaṟṟuk koḷḷum vāyppiṉi eṅkum vaḻaṅka vēṇṭum. eṉavē irupattōrām nūṟṟāṇṭil nam kalvik koḷkikaḷ piṉvarumāṟu amiyum eṉṟu etirpārkkiṉṟēṉ.
1. tamiḻakattil tamiḻ oṉṟē maḻali mutal palkali vari ettuṟiyilum payiṟṟu moḻi.
2. tamiḻvaḻi māṇavarkkē ellā vēli vāyppum uyar kalviyum.
3. intiyap piṟa moḻikaḷiyum ayal moḻikaḷiyum mēl vakuppukkaḷil naṉku kaṟṟu koḷvataṟku niṟinta vāyppum tiṭṭaṅkaḷum amittal.
- mūtaṟiñar va.cupa. māṇikkam


eḻuttuc cīrtiruttam eṅkē pōy muṭiyum?
tamiḻeḻuttuc cīrtiruttam paṟṟi ari nūṟṟāṇṭuk kālamāka eḻuntu varum karuttup pūcalkaḷ namakkut teriyum. tamiḻeḻuttu varivaṭivam kālantōṟum māṟi vantuḷḷa nīṇṭa varalāṟum namakkut teriyātataṉṟu. periyāriṉ `eḻuttuc cīrtiruttam’, tuṇivēntar vāce. kuḻanticāmiyiṉ `tamiḻ eḻuttuc cīramippu’, koṭumuṭi caṇmukaṉāriṉ putiya tamiḻ vaṭivam, koṇṭal makātēvaṉāriṉ `eḻuttum karuttum’, ce.vi. caṇmukaṉāriṉ `eḻuttuc cīrtiruttam’, vīrā. irācakōpālaṉāriṉ tamiḻ eḻuttuc cīrtiruttap puraṭci’, tamiḻ moḻiyiyaṟ kaḻakattiṉ `eḻuttuc cīrtirutta malar’, veḷḷiyāmpaṭṭucuntaraṉār tokutta `eḻuttuc cīrmi’ maṇaviyāriṉ `kālam tēṭumtamiḻ’, cu. irattiṉacāmiyiṉ `tamiḻ eḻuttu varivaṭiva varalāṟu’ mutāṉa nūlkaḷum pala itaḻkaḷil eḻutiya aṟiñarkaḷiṉ kaṭṭurikaḷuma nam urimip pārvikkum pakuttaṟivuc cintaṉikkum uriyavi.
ikkāla vaḷarccikkeṉat tamiḻ eḻuttut tiruttam tēviyē illi eṉpavarip paḻamivātikaḷ eṉṟō, camutāya vaḷarcciyiṉ etirikaḷ eṉṟō, puriyāta piṟpōkkāḷarkaḷ eṉṟō, 21ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ vaḷarcci vēkatti aṟiyātavarkaḷ eṉṟō, tamiḻk kuḻantikaḷiṉ eḻuttuc cumiyik kaṇṭum iraṅkātavarkaḷ eṉṟō, paḻippaṭṭam cūṭṭa vēṇṭiyatilli. atu pōla eḻuttut tiruttam virumpuvōrit tamiḻukku etirikaḷ eṉṟō, tamiḻp pakivarkaḷiṉ kiyāṭkaḷ eṉṟō, tamiḻ marapu aṟiyātavarkaḷ eṉṟō, aṟintum kaṟpaṉi vātikaḷ eṉṟō, tamiḻiṉ taṉittaṉmiyik keṭuppavarkaḷ eṉṟō iḷivaravu colla vēṇṭiyatumilli. iviyellām koṇṭa koḷkiyi valiyuṟuttum aṟiñarkaḷ karuttu.
eḻuttuc cīrtiruttattukku yār yār icintuḷḷaṉar, ātaravu nalkiṉar eṉṟa peyarivartam paṭṭiyalit tuṇivēntar vā.ce.ku. avarkaḷ kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ. ittiruttattukku iciyātōrum, vēṇṭām eṉpōrum palaruḷar. tamiḻāḻvār eṉap pōṟṟappeṟṟa moḻiyaṟiñar tēvanēyap pāvāṇar vaṭamoḻiyeḻuttuk kalappu tamiḻiṉ tūymiyik keṭukkum eṉavum tamiḻeḻuttu varivaṭiva māṟṟamum tamiḻukkut tēviyilli eṉavum aḻuttamākak kūṟiyavar. periyāriṉ vaḻittōṉṟalāṉa aṟiñar aṇṇā āṭcikku vanta piṉṉum periyāriṉ eḻuttu māṟṟattip piṉpaṟṟavilli; kaliñariṉ āṭcik kālattum itutāṉ nili. muttamiḻk kāvalar ki.ā.pe. vicuvanātam, cilampuc celvar ma.po.ci., tīpam pārttacārati, peruñcittiraṉār, putuvit tuṇivēntar vēṅkaṭacuppiramaṇiyaṉ eṉṟiṉṉa palar muṟiyeḻuttu vaṭiviṉiyē vēṇṭiṉar.
iṉṟum ilaṅki malēciyā mutalāṉa veḷināṭṭut tamiḻiṉattār muṟivaṭiviyē kaṭippiṭikkiṉṟaṉar. aracāṇiyiṭṭa tirutta vaṭivaṅkaḷi ēṟṟuk koḷḷāta pala tamiḻitaḻkaḷum iṉṉum namatu nāṭṭil uḷa. kaṇicamāṉa potup perumakkaḷum itaṉip piṉpaṟṟavilli.
āṇiyeḻuttu
pāṭanūlkaḷē viṭāppiṭiyāka āṇiyeḻuttip payaṉ paṭuttukiṉṟaṉa. āṇiyeḻuttil acciṭṭa nūlkaḷittāṉ potu nūlakattil vāṅka vēṇṭum eṉṟa vitiyiṉāl, patippāḷikaḷ viṟpaṉi karuti ivveḻuttil acciṭukiṉṟaṉar. inta āṇiyeḻuttāl pakkaṅkaḷ accuc celavukaḷ kūṭukiṉṟaṉa. eṉpataṉi aṟintum niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷ kaṭṭāyam piṉpaṟṟa vēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu.
tanti periyār 1935il kāṭṭiya eḻuttut tiruttaṅkaḷi ari nūṟṟāṇṭuk kālam ēṟṟuk koḷḷavilli eṉṟāl, avarmēl aṉpilli eṉpatu poruḷaṉṟu. avartam camutāyap pōrāṭṭak koḷkikaḷi ēṟṟu ottuḻitta toṇṭarkaḷkūṭa moḻi paṟṟiya inta oṉṟiṉi mēṟkoḷḷa muyalavilli. ataṟkuk kāraṇaṅkaḷ: pala māṟṟaṅkaḷ ceyvataṟku inta eḻuttu māṟṟam tēviyilli eṉa oru cārār karutiṉar. oru taṭavi eḻuttu varivaṭivil kivikka iṭaṅkoṭuttu viṭṭāl, yār yārō avaravarm ātikkattiṟ kēṟpa, kivittuk koṇṭēyiruppar: toṭarcciyiṉṟi moḻiccitivum, moḻikkulivum, nūlaḻivūm ēṟpaṭum eṉa orucārār añciṉar. iḵtu iṉṟikkum poruntum.
periyār nūṟṟāṇṭu viḻāviṉ niṉivāka 19.10.1978il 15 eḻuttukaṭku māṟṟāṉa cīrtirutta eḻuttukkaḷit kāṭṭi aṉṟirunta aracu aracāṇi piṟappittatu. āṇiyiṭṭa cila tiṅkaṭkuḷḷēyē, aikāramum oḷakāramum māṟiṉṟip paḻiya vaṭivaṅkaḷilēyē irukkum eṉa aracu veḷiyiṭṭatu. itaṉi ōr eccarikki eṉṟu koḷḷavēṇṭum.
tiruttak kāraṇaṅkaḷ
iṉṉa eḻuttukku iṉṉa eḻuttuk kuṟiyīṭu amiya vēṇṭum eṉṟa muyaṟciyi mēṟkoḷvataṟku muṉ, tiruttam vēṇṭikaḷ ataṟkuc collivarum kāraṇappāṭukaḷi mutaṟkaṇ nām ottuka koḷkiṉṟōmā? ottukkoḷḷa muṭiyumā? ottuk koṇṭāl, ivi tamiḻ moḻiyi eṅkuppōy eppaṭic cāykkum eṉṟu uṉṉippākap pārkkavēṇṭum. ik kāraṇat taṉmikaḷi nōkkum pōtutāṉ ulakamalikaḷi oṉṟu cērttālaṉṉa malippu nammip piṇikkiṉṟatu.
muṭinta nōkkam vēṟu: tamiḻaṉṟu
`oru moḻikku olitāṉ aṭippaṭi: eḻuttukkaḷ olikaṭku nām koṭukkum kuṟiyīṭukaḷ; oliyaṉkaḷi nām kuṟikkavilli; varivaṭivu moḻikku oru karuviyē. vaṭivatti māṟṟuvatālō kuṟippatālō moḻi pātikkappaṭuva tilli’ eṉpatu eḻuttut tiruttikaḷiṉ oru vātam. itu añcattakka vātam ākum. ivvātam kaṟkālattic cārntatu: pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷāka oru moḻikkum ataṉ eḻuttu vaṭivukkum uyirum uṭalum pōṉṟa piṇippu marapāka, varalāṟāka vantirukkiṟatu. ōr eḻuttu vaṭivip pārttavuṭaṉ itu iṉṉa moḻi eṉṟu nām aṟintukoḷvatilliyā? oru moḻiyiṉ olikkum ataṉ eḻuttukkum maraput toṭar pilli eṉṟāl, tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ olikaḷi enta eḻuttilum eḻutalām eṉṟa accamāṉa ōr uṭkaruttu itaṉuḷ aṭaṅkik kiṭakkiṟatu eṉpataṉi uṇartta virumpukiṟēṉ. tamiḻicik kiḷarcciyil icikku moḻi tēviyilli; enta moḻiyilum pāṭalām eṉṟa vātattiṟkum itaṟkum vēṟupāṭu uṇṭā? `uyirkaḷ kollap paṭuvatilli; uṭalkaḷi yātum ceyyalām’ eṉṟa kītiyiṉ pōkkē iṅku veḷippaṭukiṉṟatu.
inta muṭivu nōkkatti mēlum nām purintukoḷḷa vēṇṭum. tamiḻ eḻuttuc cīrtiruttam vēṇṭunariṉ karuttukkaḷiyum viḷakkaṅkaḷiyum tokuttuk kaṇittup pārttāl, intiya nāṭṭiṉ orumippāṭu nōkki, tēvanākari eḻuttukkaḷit tamiḻum mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉṟu aṟivārum uḷar. ulakavorumippāṭu karuti urōmaṉ eḻuttukkaḷiyē tamiḻ ēṟṟukkoḷḷalām eṉavum itaṉāl āṅkilam kaṟkum ellāt tamiḻk kuḻantikaṭkum oru moḻic cumi kuṟiyum eṉavum valiyuṟuttum aṟiñarkaḷum uḷar. mēṟkuṟitta iruvakiyiṉaruḷ urōmaṉ eḻuttuvātikaḷē kuṟippiṭattakkavarkaḷ. `mummoḻik koḷki ēṟkappaṭṭu atu vaṭa mānilaṅkaḷil ceyalpaṭuttappaṭumāyiṉ varivaṭiva eḷimi kāraṇamāka maṟṟa moḻikaḷi viṭat tamiḻ atika māṇavarkaḷāl kaṟkappaṭuvatu cāttiyamākum’ eṉpatu ōr aṟiñar karuttu. eṉavē ivar kaṭkellām tamiḻ eḻuttuc cīrtiruttam ōr iṭikaḻi nōkkē eṉpatu maṟukkavō maṟikkavō iyalāta uṇmi. iṉṟiya aṟiviyal yukattil evvakiyālum tamiḻiṉ tūymiyum, taṉittaṉmiyum iṉṉōrkkuk kacappākum. mutalvar kaliñar karuṇānitiyār piṉvarumāṟu eḻutukiṉṟār. `tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷic cīrtiruttam ceyyum pērāl tamiḻiyē cīrtiruttam ceyyappōvatākap paṟicāṟṟit tiripavarkaḷuma, intap pōrviyil tamiḻiṉ oli-ōciyikkūṭa māṟṟiyamikkum ceyalkaḷil tuṇintiṟaṅkum tōḻarkaḷum keṭuttu viṭṭirukkiṟārkaḷ. oliyi maṭṭum aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu, tamiḻi rōmaṉ lipiyil māṟṟiṉālkūṭap paravāyilli eṉṟu collum āpattāṉa vātamum cilarāl tuṇintu eḻutappaṭukiṉṟatu. eṉavē kaliñariṉ karuttu  eḻuttut tiruttikaḷiṉ uḷnōkkatti veḷippaṭuttak kāṇpīrkaḷ.
tuṇi vēntar vā.ce. kuḻanticāmi tamiḻ eḻuttuca cīramippukkuk kūṟum attaṉi kāraṇakkūṟukaḷum urōmaṉ eḻuttiṉ aṭittaḷattik koṇṭavi. cerumāṉiya, intōnēciya, malēciya moḻikaḷ ellām urōmaṉ vaṭivattip peṟṟu vaḷarkiṉṟaṉa eṉa avar eṭuttuk kāṭṭuvar. `eppaṭiyō uyirmey vaṭivaṅkaḷi uruvākkuvatil eṇṇaṟṟa cikkalkaḷi nām uruvākkik koṇṭōm eṉavum, `urōmaṉ varivaṭivattōṭu oppiṭumpoḻutu namatu varivaṭivattiṉ cikkalkaḷ puriyum’ eṉavum avar colvati liruntē tamiḻukku avar koṇṭa aḷavukōl urōmaṉ eṉpatu teḷivākum. uyirum meyyum eṉṟa iraṇṭōṭu uyirmey eṉṟa mūṉṟāvatu kūṭṭuṟavik kaṇṭatum, ataṟkeṉat taṉikkuṟiyīṭukaḷ koṇṭatum tamiḻ moḻikkē yuriya tirāviṭaviyalākum. it taṉmiyi vaṭamoḻiyum vaṭa vintiya moḻikaḷum ēṟṟukkoṇṭapiṉ, atu intiya moḻikaḷiṉ taṉmiyāka virintatu. `ittirāviṭaviyal cikkalāṉatu. aṟiviyal vaḷarccikku utavātatu’ eṉṟu vā.ce. kuvum avar pōṉṟa eḻuttu māṟṟam vēṇṭunarum colvataṟku aṭittaḷam, urōmaṉi aḷavukōlākak koṇṭatākum. uyirmey eṉṟa kūṭṭuniliyi nām uṭaṉpaṭāviṭṭāl, tamiḻ moḻikkēyaṉṟi intiya moḻikaṭkum uṭaṉpaṭā viṭṭāl ilakkaṇattil eṉṉa citivukaḷ ēṟpaṭum eṉak kaṟpaṉi ceyyavum iyalātu. eḻuttut tiruttikaṭku itu paṟṟiya kavali ili.
urōmaṉ aḷavi
karuvikaṭkēṟpat tamiḻeḻuttu vaṭivaṅkaḷic cīramippuc ceyyavēṇṭum eṉṟu collum aṟiñar vā.ce. kuḻanticāmi kuṟiyīṭu eṉpatu uyir, meyeḻuttukkaḷiṉ pātiyaḷavu akalamuḷḷatākavum iru iṇikōṭukaṭkuḷ aṭaṅkuvatākavum irukkavēṇṭum eṉavum neṟi kūṟuvar. inta aḷaviyal ulaka moḻikaḷiṉ potumiyā? enta moḻiyiṉ aṭippaṭiyil inta aḷavu muṟi tamiḻukku ilakkaṇamākac collappaṭukiṉṟatu? mēlum iruiṇik kōṭṭiṟkuḷ tamiḻ varivaṭivukaḷi aṭakkiviṭṭāl poṟivaḻi acciṟkum taṭṭaccu, tolipēci, miṉṉiyal tolipēci ākiya vaṟṟiṟkum peritum tuṇi ceyyum eṉpatu iṉṉōrtam karuttu.
- mūtaṟiñar cemmal va.cupa. māṇikkam
akattiṇi pākupāṭu
akapporuḷ puṟapporuḷ eṉṉum tamiḻ nūlvaki iraṇṭaṉuḷ ciṟanta akattiṇiyāyvu iṉip paṉmuṟiyāṉ mēṟ koḷḷappaṭum. āyvukkiṭi ettaṉiyō karuttu māṟāṭṭaṅkaḷ vallavaṉ iṭṭa muṭiccuppōlat tōṉṟi nammi malivikkiṉṟaṉa; kuḻantikkup pōṭum putirkḷ pōla nammaṟivic cōtikkiṉṟaṉa. kayiṟu ceyvōṉ muṭiccōṭu ceyvatilli. ataṉip payaṉ koḷvār tam aṟiyāmi yālum tuṇiccalālum muṭiccukkaḷ paṭṭuviṭukiṉṟaṉa. “putirkaḷ pōṭuvārkkēṉum viḷaṅkavēṇṭumallavā?” cilarkkup putirkaḷ iṭat teriyumēyaṉṟi avaṟṟi viṭatteriyā. iṅṅaṉamāka akattiṇikkuk kālantōṟum ēṟiya muṭiccukkaḷum palavāyiṉa. ippaṭi muṭiccukkaḷi - kaṭum putirkaḷik kaṇṭu maliyātum, iṭiyil tōṉṟiya muṭiccu eṭuttup putirviṭuttup pōvatutāṉ āymuṟi eṉṟu mayaṅkātum, āyvāḷaṉ akattiṇi mūla ilakkiyattōṭu nēraṭi aṟivut toṭarpukoḷḷum aṭiyavaṉ pōlac cemporuḷ kāṇpāṉ. marapiyal, nūliyal, aṟiviyal, uḷaviyal paṟṟic colluṅkālit tam muyaṟcikku ēṟpa akattiṇi aiyaṅkaḷ akaṉṟoḻiyak kāṇpāṉ. tolpaḻam paṭippāṉa akavilakkiyattiṉ uḷḷaṅkāṇa muyalum namakku, iṟṟit tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ, nākarikattiṉ vēṟupāṭṭāl aiyappāṭu pala tōṉṟutal iyalpiṉum iyalpē. aiyattōṟṟam uṇmit tōṟṟattukku vaḻi kāṭṭum ātaliṉ, akattiṇik kalviyāḷarkku uṇṭām aiyakkūṟukaḷi mutaṟkaṇ moḻikuval.
(a) akattiṉ ilakkaṇam yātu? akappāṭṭu eṉpatu kātaṟ pāṭṭāyiṉ, kātaṟṟiṇi eṉṟu peyar vittirukkalāmē? akattiṇi eṉap peyariyatēṉ? akam kātal eṉpaṉa oruporuṭ paṉmoḻiyā? iṭikkāla uriyāciriyarkaḷum ikkālap pulavar perumakkaḷum akattiṟkuk kūṟivarum ilakkaṇam takavuṭiyatā?
(ā) akattiṇi eḻuvakippaṭum eṉṟa pākupāṭu iciyumā? ivveṇṇikkitāṉum piḻiyeṉat tōṟṟavilliyā? caṅkavilakkiyattu 1862 akappākkaḷuḷ kikkiḷikku nāṉkē, peruntiṇikkup pattē uriyavākak kaṟkiṉṟōmē! kikkiḷi peruntiṇikaḷ akamāyiruppavum, caṅkac cāṉṟōr ivaṟṟip pōṟṟip pāṭal pala ceyyāmikku ētuveṉṉiyō? tamiḻ ilakkiyamellām akam puṟam eṉṉum vakiyiraṇṭāy aṭaṅkum eṉṟa karutturi ēṟpuṭittā?
(i) tamiḻ moḻikkaṇ akattiṇi nūl piṟattaṟku utaviya cūḻnilikaḷ yāvi? akattiṇikku vitikaḷ vakuttār yāvar? oppaṟṟa aṟivu muṉivaṉ oruvaṉ itu viti, itu marapu eṉṟu kaṟpittaruḷiṉaṉō? aṉṟip palar kūṭi āynta aṟivuk kāṭciyiṉ viḷivō? akavilakkiyam ilakkiya vulakiṟku oru taṉittamiḻ ñāyiṟu eṉavum, eṇṇam nuṇṇiya tamiḻaṟi viṉōr akalakkaruti āḻaniṉintu paṭittaḷitta ilakkiyap putukkōḷ eṉavum colli makiḻvōmēl, aṉṉa putupapaṭippiṉic ceytaṟku uriya nōkkam eṉṉa? annōkkam aṟavaḻippaṭṭatā? talivaṉ taṉ kaṟpuṭi nallāḷi aḻavittup paratti vēṭṭam nāṭukiṉṟāṉ; inta illaṟa vaḻukkali oru tiṇipporuḷākak koṇṭu akattiṇi pāṭukiṉṟatē! ituvā nōkkam? ituvā aṟam?
(ī) pāṭuvārkku ērāḷamāṉa vitikaḷi immiyum vaḻuvalākā marapeṉa vitikkum akavilakkaṇam kaṟpaṉi yūṟṟukkuk kallākātō? aṟivuk kūrmiyi maḻukkum aram ākātō? aviyakkāla akappulavarkaḷ tolkāppiyam kūṟum akavitikaḷi muḻutum piṉpaṟṟiṉarkol? muraṇiyaṉavum perukkiyaṉavum uḷavō?
ivvāṟu akattiṇi kuṟittu aiyaviṉākkaḷi aṭukkic collutalō eḷitu; aṟivokkum viṭit teḷivu kāṇpatu attuṇi eḷitaṉṟēṉum, kāṇa muyalvatum muyalvippatum ivvārāycciyiṉ pāṭākum. caṅka vilakkiyamē nām peṟṟirukkum. tamiḻ nūlkaḷuḷa toṉmiyāṉa akavilakkiyam, ilakkiya ilakkaṇa ārāycci, kātalarkaḷ pōla tammuḷ pirintu nillātavi; pirikka muṭiyātavi; valintu pirittāl vaḷappaṭātavi. ātaliṉ, akavilakkiyamum akavilakkaṇamum caṅkanūṟ celvaṅkaḷiṉ peruntuṇi koṇṭu oruṅku ārāytaṟkuriyaṉa. āyuntōṟum mēleṇṇiya aiyac cikkalkaḷ oruvāṟu aṟupaṭak kāṇpīr.
akattiṇip pirivukaḷiṉ aṭippaṭi
1. kikkiḷi 2. kuṟiñci 3. mulli 4. marutam 5. neytal 6. pāli, 7. peruntiṇi eṉappaṭṭa ēḻum akattiṇip pākupāṭām. ivaṟṟuḷ kuṟiñci mutal pāli iṟutiyākiya iṭiniṉṟa aintum aintiṇiyeṉa oru kūṭṭāka vaḻaṅkappaṭum. ippirivukaḷukku aṭinilik kāraṇam yātu? kikkiḷiyāvatu orutalikkāmam eṉṟum, aintiṇiyāvatu otta kāmam eṉṟum, peruntiṇiyāvatu ovvākkāmam eṉṟum aṟintōr colluvar. iṅṅaṉam kāmat taṉmikaḷ aṭippaṭi ākumāyiṉ, aintiṇiyi orē pirivākak koṇṭu, akattiṇip pirivukaḷ mūṉṟeṉal poruntu mēyaṉṟi  ēḻeṉal poruntātukāṇ. mēlum, kikkiḷi peruntiṇi eṉṉum peyarkkuṟikaḷ tam kāmattaṉmi cuṭṭum ki, peru, aṭikaḷ peṟṟuḷa. atu voppa aṉputtiṇi eṉṟa peyaraṉṟō aintiṇikku varutal vēṇṭum? aintiṇi eṉa eṇṇaṭiyiruttal okkumā? ātaliṉ akappirivukaṭkuk kāmattaṉmi aṭippaṭiyākātu.
tamiḻ akattiṇi kūṟum palvēṟu kātaṟ kūṟukaḷ tamiḻakattiṉ palvēṟu nilakkūṟukaḷic cārntu eḻuntavi eṉa oru kōṭpāṭu uḷatu.1 nam tāyakattup pāli eṉṟu cuṭṭik kāṭṭattakka nilappāl illi (tāl, 947) kuṟiñci, mulli, marutam, neytal eṉṟa nāṟṟiṇikaḷukkē iyalpāṉa nilappāṅkukaḷ uḷa. itaṉāl nāṉilam eṉpatu ulakiṟku oru peyarāyiṟṟu. pālikku iyalnilam iṉṟēṉum, tirinilam uṇṭu eṉṟum, kāṭum maliyum kōṭit tī veppattāl tattam pacumi iḻanta tiriniliyē pāli eṉṟum cilappatikāram naṉku teḷivupaṭuttum.
vēṉalaṅ kiḻavaṉoṭu veṅkatir vēntaṉ
tāṉalan tirukat taṉmiyiṟ kuṉṟi
mulliyum kuṟiñciyum muṟimiyiṟ ṟirintu
nalliyal piḻantu naṭuṅkutuya ruṟuttup
pāli yeṉpatōr paṭivaṅ koḷḷum (cilap. 15, 95-9)
oru kālavēṟṟumiyāl iraṇṭu tiṅkaḷ iyalpumāṟit tōṉṟum nilacceyaṟkiyē pālittiṇikku iṭamām. eṉiṉum, niliyā nīrillā nilattiliruntu nīṭittu nilikkum paṉṉūṟu pālip pāṭalkaḷi viḷivittukakoṇṭa caṅka kālac collēruḻavarkaḷiṉ ilakkiya uḻavu aṟiviṉ koḻumuṉikku ōr eṭuttukkāṭṭu. iṅṅaṉ pālit tiṇikku oruvakiyāṉa nilam uṇṭeṉṟu koḷḷiṉum kikkiḷi peruntiṇikaṭku evvāṟṟāṉum nilam illi eṉpatu nūṉmuṭivu. ātaliṉ eḻutiṇi akappirivu nilavaṭip paṭi koṇṭatu eṉṟālum poruntāmi kāṇka.
tiṇi ēḻāyiṉamikkuk kāraṇam mukkāmattaṉmiyum aṉṟu; nāṉilap pāṅkum aṉṟu eḻuvakik kātaloḻukkaṅ kaḷē uripporuḷkaḷē - ām, eṉṟu aṟivōmāka. ivvoḻukkaṅkaḷ taṉi nilikoṇṭavi; tammuḷ toṭarpu vēṇṭi nillātavi eṉpatu nīḷa niṉiyattakum oru ciṟappiyalpu. ātalālaṉṟō kuṟiñci mulli, marutam, neytal, pāli eṉpavi otta kāmattaṉmiyāl oṉṟāyiṉum, aintiṇi eṉat taṉinili kuṟikkum eṇṇup peyar peṟṟaṉa. kuṟiñci mutalāya kuṟiyīṭukaḷ avvannilattukku uriya malarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷē eṉṟum, ippeyarkaḷ piṉṉar uripporuḷkaḷip pulappaṭuttum kuṟiyīṭukaḷāyiṉa eṉṟum karutuvar oru cārār2. aṟṟaṉṟu eṉa maṟuttuk kuṟiñci mutalāyavi puṇartal mutalāya uripporuḷkaḷi mutaṟkaṇ taruvaṉa eṉavum piṉṉarē malarkaḷiyum nilaṅkaḷiyum kuṟikkappōntaṉa eṉavum karutuvar maṟṟoru cārār. mutaṟporuḷ etu? vaḻipporuḷ etu? eṉat tuṇivatil iṉṉōr māṟupaṭuvar. iruvakip poruḷum kuṟiñci mutalām coṟkaḷukku muṉṉō piṉṉō uṇṭu eṉpatilum, akattiṇiyilakkaṇam vakutta nāḷil iruporuḷum vaḻaṅkiṉa eṉpatilum iṉṉōr vēṟupaṭṭilar. eṉiṉum kikkiḷi mutalāp peruntiṇi iṟuvāy eḻutiṇi eṉa eṇṇuṅkāl, kuṟiñci mutalām coṟkaḷ tarum, taravēṇṭum poruḷkaḷ eṉṉa? nilamillāk kikkiḷi peruntiṇi kaḷōṭu eṇṇappaṭutaliṉ, ivaṟṟukku nilapporuḷ ceytal ākātu; uripporuḷ kōṭalē muṟi eṉpatu teḷivu. aintiṇiyuḷ aṭaṅkiya ovvoṉṟum taṉit tiṇiyākak karutappaṭṭamiyiṉ, aviyap pulavarkaḷ aintiṇi mēlum pāṭātu āḷukku oru tiṇiyāka ilakkiyam kaṇṭaṉar.
marutamō rampōki neytalam mūvaṉ
karutuṅ kuṟiñci kapilaṉ - karutiya 
pāliyōta lānti paṉimulli pēyaṉē
nūliyō tiṅkuṟu nūṟu.
eṭṭuttokiyuḷ aiṅkuṟunūṟum kalittokiyum pāṭiṉōr eṇṇikki kuṟinta nūlkaḷ. āṇṭum tiṇikkoru āciriyarāṉa ceytiyi - aintiṇiyiyum ōrāciriyaṉ pāṭā muṟiyi mēli veṇpākkaḷ aṟivikkuma.
peruṅkaṭuṅkōṉ pāli kapilaṉ kuṟiñci
marutaṉiḷa nākaṉ marutam aruñcōḻaṉ
nallut tiraṉmulli nallan tuvaṉeytal
kalvivalārta kaṇṭa kali.
peruṅkaṭukkō pālittiṇi (pirivu) oṉṟaṉiyē pāṭip `pāli pāṭiya’ eṉṟa ciṟappup peṟṟavar. oru tiṇikkuriya uripporuḷip pāṭuṅkālum, ellāt tuṟi mēlum pāṭātu oru tuṟiyaḷavil pāṭiniṉṟa akap pulavar palar. veṟi pāṭiya kāmakkaṇṇiyār oru tuṟi pāṭiya ciṟappup pulavar. mēlum mutal karupporuḷkaḷic ciṟitum kūṟātē uripporuḷ maṭṭum kūṟum akappāṭalkaḷum palavuḷa.
nōmeṉ ṉeñcē nōmeṉ ṉeñcē
imitīyp paṉṉa kaṇṇir tāṅki
amitaṟ kamintanaṅ kātalar
amivila rākutal nōmeṉ ṉeñcē		(kuṟun 4)
uripporuḷ oṉṟē nuvalum ivvaṉiya pākkaḷ pālittiṇi, kuṟiñcittiṇiyeṉac cevvitiṉ peyar peṟukiṉṟaṉa. kāraṇam tiṇimiyāvatu uripporuḷē paṟṟiyatu. mutalum karuvumō apporuṭkut tuṇimiyām attuṇiyē. ātaliṉ akattiṇi eḻumip pakuppu kātalarkaḷiṉ kikkiḷi puṇartal pirital iruttal iraṅkal ūṭalperuntiṇi eṉṉum kāmavoḻukkaṅkaḷi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭatu eṉṟu teḷiyattakum akattiṉ ilakkaṇam eṉṉi? akam eṉṟa kiḷavikkup poruḷ eṉṉi? ivi iṉit toṭarntu āyavēṇṭupavi eṉiṉum, ippōtu āyvu peṟumāṟilli. iṭiyē cilapala akacceytikaḷ viḷakkam peṟṟa piṉpu. ivvāyvit toṭaṅkutu teḷivukkum muṭipukkum eḷimi ceyyum.
akattiṇiyum aintiṇiyum
akattiṇip paṭippil kikkiḷi peruntiṇikaḷi viṭa aintiṇi pallāṟṟāṉum mēlāyatu eṉpataṟku uṟaḻkūṟṟu vēṇṭā. akattiṇit tollāciriyar tolkāppiyar kikkiḷi peruntiṇikaḷiṉ poruḷkaḷi iruvēṟu taṉicciṟu cūttiraṅkaḷ aḷavil (995-996) akattiṇiyiyal iṟutik kaṇṇē colli amikuvar. `kikkiḷi mutalāp peruntiṇi iṟuvāy’ (949) eṉa eṇṇuṅkāl mutaliṭam peṟṟa kikkiḷikūṭap poruḷ kūṟuṅkāl avviṭam peṟāmi karutattakum. `kikkiḷik kuṟippē’, `peruntiṇik kuṟippē eṉṟu ivviru tiṇikaḷiṉ ciṟunili tōṉṟa avaṟṟiṉ poruṭ cūttiraṅkaḷi muṭittuk kāṭṭuvar. `kuṟippu’ eṉṟa coṟpeyvāl ivviru tiṇikaḷ virittuppāṭum peṟṟiyavalla eṉṟu cuṭṭuvar. itaṉāl tolkāppiyam aintiṇiyē akattiṇi eṉpatupōla, aintiṇiyiṉ ilakkaṇak kūṟukaḷip palapaṭa viritturippak kāṇalām. akattiṇiyiyal 55 nūṟpākkaḷik koṇṭatu. akattiṇi eṉap potup peyar pūṇṭiruntum, itaṉ 50 nūṟpākkaḷ aintiṇiyiṉ neṟikaḷiyē virittu moḻital kāṇka. kaḷavil kaṟpiyal poruḷiyal meyppāṭṭiyal eṉappaṭum piṟa nāṉku iyalkaḷuṅkūṭa aintiṇi nuvalum amippiṉavākavē uḷa. aintiṇik kātal aṟanalattatu. ulakam oppuvatu, makkaṭku iyalvatu, ilakkiyattukku icintatu. iṉṉa naṉṉayaṅkaḷi nōkkic caṅkap pulamiyiṉōr aintiṇit tuṟikaḷiyē peritum pāṭiṉar. tolkāppiyar ceytatu pōlak kikkiḷi peruntiṇikaṭku uriya otukkiṭam nalkiṉar. ippāṅkiṉpaṭi, aintiṇi peritum, ēṉi irutiṇi ciṟitumāka innūlilum ārāyccippaṭum.
aintiṇi ivvaḷavu mēlān tarattateṉiṉum, akattiṇi aintiṇi eṉṟa coṟkaḷ oruporuṭ paṉmoḻiyākā eṉpatu aṟika. ivaṟṟi oṉṟeṉak karutiyum, kikkiḷi perun tiṇikaḷi akattiṉ vēṟeṉak karutiyum vaḷarnta eḻuttellām perum piḻi. “akattiṇiyiṉkaṇ kikkiḷi varutal tiṇi mayakkām piṟaveṉiṉ, kikkiḷi mutaṟ peruntiṇi yiṟuvāya eḻutiṇiyiṉuḷḷum kikkiḷiyum peruntiṇiyum akattic cārnta puṟamāyiṉum” (pā. 4) eṉṉum tirukkōvi yuriyāciriyar karutturi ippiḻiyiṭaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu. orukuṭip piṟanta pallōruḷ cilar ciṟappuṟṟum maṟṟum cilar ciṟappiṉṟiyum iruntataṟkāka, aṉṉōr oru kuṭippiṟappukku iḻukkuṇṭō? aintiṇi nōkkak kikkiḷi peruntiṇikaḷ ciṟappilavākalām; eṉiṉum, avviraṇṭum eḻutiṇi eṉappaṭṭa akattiṇi yātaṟku iḻukkilli.
kikkiḷi mutalāp peruntiṇi yiṟuvāy
muṟpaṭak kiḷanta eḻutiṇi yeṉpa	 (tol, 946)
eṉṟa toṉṉeṟippaṭi, akattiṉ mutalākavum iṟutiyākavum eṇṇuppeṟṟa kikkiḷi peruntiṇikaḷi `akattic cārnta puṟam’ eṉṟu kōviyuriyāciriyar kuṟippatu aṭi muraṇilliyā? ivviraṇṭiyum puṟamākkiya avar `akatti” eṉṟa collāl muḻutum aintiṇiyittāṉē karutiviṭṭār eṉpatu pulappaṭavilliyā? kikkiḷi peruntiṇikaḷiṉ perumippāṭu evvāṟāyiṉum, avaṟṟi akattiṇip pakuppiṉiṉṟu taḷḷa muṭiyāmikkum, aintiṇiyōṭu uṭaṉeṇṇu taṟkum kuruti yoppaṉṉa paṇpoppiṉi ivaṟṟiṭit tamiḻ mūtāḷar kaṇṭiruttal vēṇṭum. avvoppiṉi piṉṉiyalkaḷil virippōm. ivviraṇṭiyum cērttu koḷḷāviṭiṉ, akattiṇi nakamaṟṟa viralpōlavum paṭiyaṟṟa maṉipōlavum muḻuvaṉappil oru kuṟivupaṭiyatākat tōṉṟum eṉa īṇṭuk kuṟippiṭal pōtum, ēḻu tiṇikaḷiyum oruṅku cuṭṭa vēṇṭuṅkāl, akattiṇi eṉṉum potuk kūṟiyīṭṭi āḷutal tolkāppiya vaḻakku.
puṟattiṇi maruṅkiṟ porunti ṉallatu
akattiṇi maruṅkiṉ aḷavuta vilavē.			(tol. 1000)

akattiṇi maruṅkiṉ ariltapa vuṇarntōr.
puṟattiṇi yilakkaṇam tiṟappaṭak kiḷappiṉ		(tol. 1001)
akattiṇip potuppeyari aintiṉip piriviṉ maṟu peyarākat tolkāppiyam āṇṭatilli. “makkaḷ nutaliya ataṉ aintiṇi” (999) “aṉpoṭu puṇarnta aintiṇi (1037) eṉṟāṅku uriya pirivup peyarāl vitantu collum naṭiniṉiyattakum. `akaṉ aintiṇi’ eṉṟa potuvaṭiyāl, aintiṇi akat tiṇiyuḷ oruvaki eṉpatum, akakkikkiḷi akapperun tiṇi eṉap piṟavakikaḷum uḷa eṉpatum koḷḷakkiṭattal kāṇka. “aṉpiṉ akattiṇi kaḷaveṇappaṭuvatu eṉaṉātu” eṉṉātu, “aṉpiṉ aintiṇi kaḷaveṉappaṭuvatu” eṉṟa iṟiyaṉārum “akat tuṟikaḷ tāṅki aintiṇi neṟiyaḷāvi” eṉṟu viḻipapōṭu coṟ peytali oppu nōkkavēṇṭum. ātaliṉ kikkiḷi peruntiṇikaḷ kuṟinta pāṭalkaḷ uṭiyaṉavē yeṉiṉum akat tiṇi ārāycciyil iṭampeṟa vēṇṭiyavi eṉpatu teḷivu.
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1.	The Ancient Tamils, Part 1: p 46. S.K. Pillli.
2.	tol-poruḷ. ru. iḷampūraṇam; tolkāppiyap poruḷatikāra ārāycci pa. 24; paḻantamiḻar nākarikam; pa. 18. ḷuvaraṉaniḷa in kūyaaṭi ṭuvinacayavaracana yaṉē ḻaḷivaṭicala ña. 273.
3.	iṟiyaṉār akapporuḷ. pavāṉantar kaḻakap patippu, pa. 25 tol. poruḷ. 5. nacciṉārkkiṉiyam; ṭākṭar pāratiyār. tol, akam, pāyiram iḷavaḻakaṉār akattiṇiyiyal viḷakkam, pa. 87-89. aṭikkuṟippu.
- mūtaṟiñar cemmal va.cupa. māṇikkam 
putiya pārvi juṉ 2016


akattiṇi pākupāṭu
1. aintiṇi - kaḷaviyal- iyaṟkip puṇarcci
akattiṇiyiṉ peruṅkūṟāṉa aintiṇik kātalkaḷi iṉi ārāyvōm. caṅka akappāṭalkaḷic cāṉṟākak koṇṭu aintiṇit tuṟikaḷiṉ tiṟaṉ kāṇpōm. kaḷavoḻukkam kaṟpoḻukkam eṉa aintiṇi irupāṟpaṭum eṉṟu potuvākak kūṟiṉālum, kuṟiñcikkaḷavu kuṟiñcik kaṟpu, pālikkaḷavu pālikkaṟpu, mullikkaḷavu mullikkaṟpu, marutakkaḷavu marutakkaṟpu, neytaṟkaḷavu neytaṟkaṟpu eṉat tiṇitōṟum iraṇṭum koḷḷattakum. iṅṅaṉam koḷvatāl kaḷavukkātaluma kaṟpukkātalum ellā nilattilum nikaḻvaṉavē eṉṉum ulaka vaḻakkam peṟappaṭum.
kātalukkuk kāraṇam
kāmappuruvam eytiya naṅki, tāy ēvaṟpaṭi tiṉippuṉam celkiṉṟāḷ. tōḻiyarum uṭaṉcelkiṉṟaṉar. muṟṟiya katirkaḷik kavarum kiḷikkūṭṭattit taṭṭik karuvikoṇṭu veruṭṭukiṉṟaṉar. aruviyāṭiyum taḻi koytum malar toṭuttum vantu acōkantaṇṇiḻalil amarkiṉṟaṉar (kuṟiñcip, 39, 102) kumarapparuvam eytiya nampi taṉ vēṭṭināyoṭu maliyelām cuṟṟit tirikiṟāṉ. pal maṇam kamaḻum talimāli aṇintu ivvēṭṭuvak kumaraṉ naṅki irukkumiṭattukkut tāṉāka vantu cērukiṟāṉ (akam. 28). taṉ ampāl puṇpaṭṭa matayāṉi ippakkam pērntatuṇṭō? pōka nīvir kaṇṭatuṇṭō? eṉṟu malarūtum vaṇṭiṉ icikkuc cevi koṭuttirunta iḷam peṇkaḷi ucāvukiṟāṉ (akam. 388) appōtu avaṉukkuk kātaṟ kuṟippu illi. mārpil ceṅkaḻunīrum taliyil veṭcippūvum akattuc cantaṉap pūccum kiyil villampum koṇṭu muṉṉiṟkum iḷiñaṉukku muṉṉē iḷamātarkaḷ niṟkak kūciṉar. viṉāvukku maṟumoḻi aṉṉōr nāveḻavilli. itaṟkūṭē nampiyiṉ kaṇṇum naṅkiyiṉ kaṇṇum naṭpukkoṇṭaṉa. kātalāṭiṉa; kāmattīppaṟṟiṉa. (akam. 48).
kaṇṭatum kātal eṉpatu ituvē. “kaṇ taravanta kāma oḷḷeri” (kuṟuta 305) eṉṟu kuppik kōḻiyār kāmattiṉ piṟappiṭamākak kaṇṇi kūṟuvar. kalloṭu kal cērntu tī uṇṭāvatupōlat kaṇṇoṭu kaṇṇum cērntu tī uṇṭām; atu kāmattīyām eṉṟu ippulavar kuṟippippar. kal piṟappitta tī pañcu mutalāṉa paṟṟu poruḷ illiyāyiṉ vaḷarātu. kaṇ piṟappitta kāmattī tāṉēyum vaḷaravallatu eṉpati `oḷḷeri’ eṉṉum aṭiyāl aṟiyalām. kaṇkaḷ kalantapiṉ kātalartam uḷḷaṅkaḷ kalakkiṉṟaṉavā? muṉṉarē uḷḷaṅ kalantamiyik kāṭṭum aṟikuṟiyā kaṇkalappu?
nāṭṭam iraṇṭum aṟivuṭam paṭuttaṟkuk
kūṭṭi yurikkum kuṟippuri yākum 	(tol. 1041)
oṉṟupaṭṭa uḷḷakkuṟippit talivaṉ pārvi talivikkum, avaḷ pārvi avaṉukkum urikkiṉṟaṉa. oru karutti vāyāṟ coṉṉāl veḷippaṭiyāka naṉku viḷaṅkum. vāyāṟ collik koḷḷutal kātalulakiṟkup poruntumā? kātal nōyi vaḷarkkumā? ātaliṉ kātalarkaḷ uḷḷakkaruttik kaṇṇāṟ collikkoḷpa aḵtu avarkaṭku vāyāṟ collik koḷvatupōlap puriyum. vāyiṉ ceyalik kaṇ ceytalāl, “nāṭṭam iraṇṭum uriyākum” eṉpar tolkāppiyar. kātaṟ kaḷattil kaṇṇukkuriya matippu ciṟitum vāykkilli. “vāyccoṟkaḷ eṉṉa payaṉum ila” eṉpar tiruvaḷḷuvar. kaṇ tanta kāmam eṉpataṟkākak kāmattukkuka kaṇ kāraṇamāyviṭumā? vīraṉippeṟṟa vayiṟu (“īṉṟa vayiṟō ituvē” puṟam 89) eṉṟāl, vīraṉ piṟappukku vayiṟu kāraṇam eṉṟu colla māṭṭōm. ātaliṉ uḷḷak kāmam kaṇṇil veḷippaṭṭatu eṉpatallatu kāmattukkum kaṇṇukkum piṟapput toṭarpiṉṟu. pāl vēṟupaṭṭa āṇmiyum peṇmiyum pārttatum uḷḷam paṟikoṭuttuk kāmap pittēṟutaṟkuk kāraṇam eṉkol? ivviṉā iṉṉum tīrā viṉāvō. kātal kāraṇam eṉṟu eḷitākac colli viṭukiṉṟōm. kātal eṉpatutāṉ yātu? itaṟku aṟiviyalum viṭi kūṟa oṇṇāmiyik keṉṉattu vākkar `pāluṭaliyal’ nūlil oppukiṟār: “pāluṇarvupaṟṟi oru poruttamāṉa koḷkiyi nām kūṟuvataṟkiyalavilli eṉpati ottukkoḷḷavēṇṭum. peṇṇoruttiyik kāṇkiṉṟāṉ; avaḷ moḻiyik kēṭkiṉṟāṉ; avaḷ maṇatti mōkkiṉṟāṉ. avaḷ meyyit tīṇṭukiṉṟāṉ. kaṇṭu kēṭṭu uṇṭu uyirttu uṟṟu aṟiyum pōtu uḷḷam kāmattiṉ koḷkalam ākiṉṟatu. uṭampil cila māṟṟaṅkaḷ nikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. iviyellām puṇarcciyiṟpōy muṭīvaṭiyum kātal puṟanilikaḷik kaṇṭu kūṟumaḷavē aṟiviyal ceyyakkūṭiyatu. ataṉ tōṟṟatti itu teḷivākkumā? illavē illi.” ērāḷamāṉa iḷiya āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum nāḷtōṟum cāliyilum cōliyilum oruvariyoruvar kāṇkiṉṟaṉarē, kaṇṭum, oruvaṉ yārō oruttimēl kātal koḷkiṉṟāṉ; orutti evaṉō oruttaṉpāl kaṇcāykkiṉṟāḷ. palari vilakki oruvarit tērntu virumpum icciṟappup pārvikkuk kāraṇam ituveṉa uyiriyal nūlum colla muṭivatilli. iḵtu.
āyvukku uriya ariya cikkal eṉṟu nam muṉṉōrum karutiṉar. vāḷāvirātu kāraṇaṅkāṇa muyaṉṟaṉar. āṇ peṇ eṉpaṉa paṭipput toṭakkattu orē oru poruḷām eṉṟum, teyva cāpattāl piḷavupaṭṭu iraṇṭāyiṉa eṉṟum, aṉṟu mutal oṉṟāy iṇiyap pāṭupaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum kātalukku viḷakkam kūṟuvar piḷēṭṭō. itu umiyoru pākam aruttanārīcuvaraṉ. eṉac civaṉukku āṇpāti peṇpāti uruvam kaṟpikkum intup purāṇattip pōṉṟatu.
oṉṟē vēṟē eṉṟiru pālvayiṉa
oṉṟi uyarnta pāla tāṇiyiṉ
otta kiḻavaṉum kiḻattiyum kāṇpa.	(tol. 1038)
uyarnta pāliṉ āṇiyāl. nallūḻiṉ ēvalāl kātaṟ kāṭci nikaḻum eṉpar tolkāppiyar. kiḻavaṉ kiḻattiyik kāṇpāṉ eṉṟō, kiḻatti kiḻavaṉik kāṇpāḷ eṉṟō
eḻuvāyum ceyappaṭu poruḷumākat tolkāppiyam naṭi toṭukkavilli. muntik kātalittavar yār? eṉṟa kātaliyalpu pulappaṭa, kiḻavaṉum kiḻattiyum kāṇpa eṉa iruvariyumē eḻuvāyāka, vittuk kāṭṭum naṭippāṭu aṟiyattakum. ip pālāṭciyic caṅkap pulavarkaḷām pēricāttaṉārum ammūvaṉārum oppupa.
pālvarin tamittal allatu avarvayiṉ
cālpaḷantaṟitaṟku yāa yārō			(pēricāttaṉār kuṟun 366)

piṟitoṉ ṟākak kūṟum
āṅkum ākkumō vāḻiya pālē		(ammūvaṉār ain. 10)
orē pāṭaliṉ āciriyar mōtācaṉār kātaṟ pāṅki aḻunta ārāyntār. kātalarāvār vevvēṟiṭattavarkaḷāka irukka vēṇṭum eṉpatilli. muṉpiṉ mukamaṟiyāp putiyavarkaḷātal vēṇṭum eṉpatilla. nāḷtōṟum palkāl paḻakikkoṇṭu varu ōr iḷiyāṉum iḷiyāḷum eṉṟō orunāḷ tiṭīreṉak kātal tōṉṟap pārttal iyalpēyām. ciṟuvaṉ ciṟumiyāy irunta paruvattu ivviruvarum talimuṭi paṟṟiyiḻuttu aṭittuk koṇṭaṉarē, ceviliyar iṭimaṟittu vilakkavum viṭātu caṇṭiyiṭṭaṉarē. iṉṟē iṇinta malarmāli pōla maṇa makkaḷāka viḷaṅkak kāṇkiṉṟōm nallūḻiṉ viḷiyāṭṭaṉṟē itu eṉṟu talivaṉ taliviyic ciṟuparuvam toṭṭu aṟintōr avviruvartam uṭaṉ pōkkik kaṇṭu pālāṟṟali viyakkiṉṟaṉar.
ivaṉivaḷ aimpāl paṟṟavum ivaḷivaṉ
puṉtali ori vāṅkunaḷ pariyavum
kātaṟ ceviliyar tavirppavum tavirātu
ētil ciṟuceru uṟupa maṉṉō
nallimaṉ ṟamma pālē melliyal
tuṇimalarp piṇiyal aṉṉavivar
maṇamakiḻ iyaṟki kāṭṭi yōyē.				(kuṟun. 229)
mōtācaṉāriṉ ippāṭal “pālatāṇiyiṉ” eṉṟa tol kāppiyattukku nalviḷakkamātal kāṇka. iḷampūraṇar tol kāppiyattukku mutaluriyāciriyar. ivar uri uḷḷa urikaḷuḷ ēṟṟamuṭiyatu. oṉṟē vēṟē (1038) eṉṉum nūṟpāviṟku.
“oruvariyoruvar kaṇṭuḻiyellām puṇarccivēṭki tōṟṟāmi yiṉ, pālatāṇiyāṉ oruvariyoruvar puṇartaṟ kuṟippōṭu kāṇpa eṉṟavāṟu”
eṉa ariya viḷakkam taruvar iḷampūraṇar. kaṇṭatum kātalkaṇṭa mutaṟpārviyilē kātalkoṇṭu viṭutal eṉṟa kōṭpāṭṭi orupāl maṟuppar. āṇ peṇ pālarkaḷic cantikkac ceyvatu vitiyiṉ vēliyaṉṟu. avarkaḷ mutalmuṟi cantikkalām; palamuṟi cantittum irukkalām. ic cantip pellām pālatāṇiyilli. mutalnāḷ mutaṟpārvi yilō, piṉṉāl pala pāriviyalō, oru pārviyik kātalōṭu pārkkac ceyvatē vitiyiṉ āṇi eṉṟu teḷivippār uriyāciriyar. ikkātaṟ puṇarcciviti kūṭṭiya mutaluṟavu iyaṟkip puṇarcci eṉṟu potuvākap peyar peṟum. talivaṉum taliviyumām viti muṉṉarē uṇṭeṉiṉum tōṉṟātu, kālam pārttu aṭaṅkiyirukkum. kālamāvatu iruvarum kāmam nukarvataṟkuriya kumarap paruvam. apparuvam vāyātārkkuk kaṇṇil kātalnōkkam eḻātu; piṟar kaṇṇil eḻunta kātalnōkkattip purintu koḷḷum aṟivum irātu. ātaliṉ aṭaṅkiyirunta viti, puṇartaṟkuriya iruvarum āḷāṉa piṉṉarttāṉ veḷippaṭuvatu muṟiyum payaṉumām. itukarutiyē, tolkāppiyamum iṟiyaṉār akapporuḷum, kāmapuṇarcci eṉṟu immutaṟpuṇarccikkup peyariṭṭuḷa. ikkuṟiyīṭu iyaṟkip puṇarcci eṉṟa kuṟiyīṭṭiṉum virumpattakkatu. ituvari ārāynta kāmat toṭarpupaṟṟi iṟiyaṉārakap poruḷ, tirukkōviyār uriyāciriyarkaḷuma toṭiviṭipaṭa eḻutiyuḷḷaṉar.
uḷḷappuṇarcciyum meyyuṟupuṇarcciyum
āṇpeṇ uṟavukkuk kātal kāraṇam eṉṟōm. kātalukkup pāl kāraṇam eṉṟōm. aṟitōṟum aṟiyāmi kaṇṭatallatu eṉṉa viḷakkam uṇṭōm? pālāvatu yātu? pālatāṇi eṉpatu eṉ? pāl kūṭṭu vittaṟkuk kāraṇam eṉṉa? otta talivaṉum taliviyum pālatāṇiyāl kātaṟ kuṟippoṭu kāṇpar eṉiṉ, avviruvarum ottavarkaḷāka iruppataṟkuk kāraṇam eṉṉa? otta talivaṉum taliviyum pālatāṇiyāl kātaṟ kuṟippoṭu kāṇpar eṉiṉ, avviruvarum ottavarkaḷāka iruppataṟkuk kāraṇam piṟituṇṭō? oppu eṉpatu etu? aḵtu orupiṟaviyoppā? palpiṟavi yoppā?
immi māṟi maṟumi yāyiṉum
nīyā kireṉ kaṇavaṉi
yāṉā kiyarniṉ ṉeñnēr pavaḷē.				(kuṟun.49)

niṉakkivaṉ makaṉāt tōṉṟi yatūum
maṉakkiṉi yāṟkunī makaḷā yatūum
paṇṭuma paṇṭum palpiṟap puḷavāl
kaṇṭa piṟaviyē yalla kāriki					(maṇimē. 24, 569)
eṉṟu ilakkiyam kūṟumāṅku, talivaṉ taliviyar vaḻivaḻik kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyarā? iṉṉa aiyaṅkaḷ nam aṟiviyal kiḷikkiṉṟaṉa. tirukkōvi urip pērāciriyar kātal vāḻkkiyil vitikku uyariya matippu nalkavilli. pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam tōḻiyaṟ kūṭṭaṅkaḷukkup pāṅkaṉum tōḻiyum tuṇiyāvār attaṇi yallatu talivaṉ talivi kātalukkuk kāraṇamākār aṉṟē; atupōla iyaṟkippuṇarcciyil viti kūṭṭuvitta tuṇiyallatu avartam aṉpukku kāraṇamākātu eṉpatu avvuriyāciriyar tuṇipu. niṟka,
kātalartam maṉavoṟṟumiyē akat tiṇiyiṉ uyirp paṇpu. iv voṟṟumiyi uḷḷap puṇarcci’ eṉṟu akavilakkaṇam kūṟum. evvaki akattiṇip pāṭalukkum akattuṟip pāṭalukkum uḷḷap puṇarcci iṉṟiyamiyātatu; puṟa naṭiyillātatu; iṟimi cāṉṟatu. akappulavar ivvaṭippaṭikku ūṟupaṭayātum kūṟār. ūṟupaṭā niliyil yātum kūṟuvar. ip pēruṇmi ivvārāycci muḻuvatum nīḷa niṉiya vēṇṭuvatu eṉṟu kuṟikkoṇmiṉ.
āḷāṉa talivaṉ talivi uḷḷam puṇarntapiṉ uṭaṟpuṇarccikku avāvutal muṟiyē. maṉam oṉṟiya kātalōr uṭal oṉṟutaṟkut takka taṉiyiṭam tērvar. iṭam vāyppiṉ iṉpappāyal koḷḷap piṉ vāṅkār. peṟṟōrik kalantu mēlceyvaṉa ceyvōm  eṉṉum aṟivu oṭār. maṇ cuḻaliṉum kaṭal poṅkiṉum mali peyariṉum viṇ vīḻiṉum aracu vitippiṉum makkaḷ etirppiṉum, varuvatu varuka eṉṟu, accam aṟiyār. kātal kātal kātal, kātal nīṅkiṟ cātal cātal cātal eṉṉum pērāṇmi koṇṭa iḷiya uḷḷaṅkaḷ peṟṟōrkku añciyō piṟa accaṅkoṇṭō puṇarcciyit taḷḷippōṭa eṇṇā. iṭam vāyāmiyiṉ puṇarcci nikaḻātirukkalām. iṭam vāyttum tāytuñcāmi nāytuñcāmi mutalāṉa iṭiyūṟukaḷāl puṇarcci taṭippaṭṭirukkalām. uḷḷam kalanta ciṉṉāṭkaḷil tirumaṇam naṭipeṟṟirukkalām. kaḷavukkālattu uṭaṟ kūṭṭuṟavē illi eṉṟum, atu kūṭātu eṉṟum kūṟuvār koḷki piḻiyām; iyaṟki yuṇarccikku muraṇām. kaṟpu eṉappaṭuvatu kātalartam meyttoṭarpukkup piṉ varum oḻukkamaṉṟu. oruvari yoruvar uḷḷattāl niṉitta appoḻutē kaṟpeṉṉum tiṇmi vēṇṭappaṭum. meyppuṇarvu paṭṭapiṉ, peṟṟōr iciyārāyiṉ, eṉceyvatu? kaṟpuk keṭumaṉṟē; aṉṉōr icivu teriyumvari uḷḷam puṇarnta aḷavil vittuk koḷvōm eṉṟa pakuttaṟivu uṇmik kaṟputtiṟamāmō? “uḷḷattāṉ vēṭki celliṉum meyyuṟṟārāyiṟṟu” eṉa aṟutiyiṭṭurippar iṟiyaṉārakap poruḷ uriyāciriyar. ātaliṉ kaṟpuk karuti, kaḷavil meyyuṟār eṉpatu aṟamākātu. kaḷavuk kālattu meyyiṉpam tuyttami paṟṟiya akappāṭalkaḷ palavuḷa. meyyuṟum ceyki iṉṟāyiṉa, iravukkuṟi mutalāṉa ettuṇiyō tuṟikaḷ ciṟanta payaṉilavāy oḻiyuṅkāṇ.
ayattuvaḷar piñcāy muruntiṉ aṉṉa
nakippolin tilaṅkum eyiṟukeḻu tuvarvāy
ākat tarumpiya muliyaḷ paṇittōḷ
māttāṭ kuvaḷi malarpiṇit taṉṉa
māyitaḻ maḻikkaṇ māa yōḷoṭu
pēyum aṟiyā maṟiyami puṇarcci	(akam. 62)
talivaṉ mutaṟpuṇarcciyi mīṇṭum niṉiyum pāṭṭu itu. māniṟat taliviyiṉ cevvitaḻ cuvittu, arumpiya mārpiyum akaṉṟa tōḷiyum taḻuvi nukarnta maṟittaṟkariya mutaṟ kūṭṭuṟavi niṉivu koḷkiṉṟāṉ. pēyum naṭamāṭṭam oṭuṅkit tūṅkum naḷḷiravil tuytta kaḷavup puṇarcci eṉṟu perumitaṅ koḷkiṉṟāṉ. uḷḷap puṇarccikku maṟiviṭam vēṇṭiya tilli. pēyum aṟiyā naṭuyāmam vēṇṭiyatilli. ātaliṉ paraṇar ippāṭṭil kaḷavuk kātalariṉ meymmuyakkattik kūṟukiṟār eṉpatu veḷippaṭi muṉpiṉ yātoru uṟavum mukavaṟivum illātār uḷḷam aṉpuṟṟu uṭal iṉpuṟṟa kāṭciyi orukuṟuntokip pāṭṭāl aṟikiṉṟōm. kātalaṉ puṇarcci niṟivil kātaliyi nōkki, ippōtu nam uṭalkaḷā kalantaṉa? “aṉpuṭi neñcan tām kalantaṉavē” (kuṟun 40) eṉṟu nākarikamāka nalam pārāṭṭukiṉṟāṉ.
2. iṭantalippāṭu
taṟceyalāka orunāḷ kaṇṭa iruvar uḷḷam puṇarntaṉar: kātalarāyiṉar. aṭaṅkiya vēṭki perukiṟṟu. mutalnāḷ kaṇṭaviṭattē iṉṟum kāṇalām eṉṟa nampikkiyōṭu talivaṉ tāṉē avviṭam celkiṉṟāṉ. ārāk kātaluṭiya taliviyum avaṉukku muṉṉarē vantu aṅku niṟkiṉṟāḷ. ēṉ? orumuṟi āṇ pulliyapiṉ, peṇṇukkuk kāmam ciṟakkum. muṉpōla tōḻiyarōṭu viḷiyāṭa vantum viḷiyāṭṭil maṉaṅkoḷḷāḷ pūppaṟittu malar toṭukka uḷḷam nāṭāḷ. avaḷ kātaṟ perukki uṇarnta talivaṉ, “āṭalum toṭuttalum ceyyātu taṉittuniṟkum nī yārō: uṉakku eṉ vaṇakkam. kaṇṭavar kaṇṇip paṟikkum aḻakiyē! kaṭal vāḻ teyvamō nī?”
yāri yōniṟ ṟoḻutaṉam viṉavutum
kaṇṭōr taṇṭā nalatti teṇṭirip
peruṅkaṭaṟ parappiṉamarntuṟi aṇaṅkō	(naṟ. 155)
eṉṟu taṉ kātaṟpaṟiyi muḻakkukiṉṟāṉ; aḻakut teyvamākat toḻukiṉṟāṉ. ‘vaḷi olippa naṇṭiṉi āṭṭupavaḷē! naṇṭiṉi āṭṭupavaḷ pōlak kūntalāl mukatti maṟittu niṟpavaḷē māli maṟiyiṉ niṉmuyakkam kiṭikkum” (aiṅ. 197) eṉṟu vāyūṟi moḻikiṉṟāṉ. eṉ collukkellām maṟumoḻi collātu nāṇupavaḷē! niṉ nāṇam eṉ kāmattip perukkiṉ tāṅkuvatu eppaṭi? (“kāmam kimmikiṉ tāṅkutal eḷitō”) eṉṟu tāṉē tāṉē paṉṉukiṟāṉ.
iṭantalippāṭu paṟṟi mūṉṟu pāṭalkaḷē (aiṅ. 197. naṟ. 39; 153) caṅkavilakkiyattil uḷa. puṇarcci vēṭki kāṇappaṭuvataṉṟip puṇarnta kuṟippuc ciṟitu ippāṭalkaḷil illi. iyaṟkip puṇarcci, pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam, tōḻiyiṟkūṭṭam eṉṟa kuṟiyīṭukaḷ kātalariṭip puṇarcciyuṇṭu eṉpati veḷippaṭiyākak kāṭṭuvatu pōla, iṭantalippāṭu (tol. 1443) eṉṉum kuṟiyīṭu kāṭṭavilli. itaṉ uṭkōḷ eṉṉa? kaṇṭāl muyaṅkutal, muyaṅkutaṟkē kāṇutal eṉpatu ācikkāmam ākumaṉṟi aṉpuk kāmam ākātu. kātalarkaḷ puṇarvu viruppiṉṟiyum kaṇṭu koḷvār; kāṇuntōṟum puṇarvu mēṟkoḷḷār eṉpatu (kīḻāṉa paruva eḻuccikku iṭaṅkoṭā) uyarnta kātalākum. iyaṟkip puṇarcciyil kaṇṭu kalanta talivaṉukakum talivikkum avvaḷavil maṉaniṟivu ēṟpaṭumā? pirinta piṉ puṇarnta niṉivum puṇarntār uruvamum neñci arittaṉa. putiya periya uṟavi mīṇṭum orumuṟi kaḷittuk kāṇpatē iṭantalippāṭu. it talippāṭṭāl ārā iḷiya neñcaṅkaḷ vēkantaṇikiṉṟaṉa; amitikku varukiṉṟaṉa; nampikki koḷkiṉṟaṉa.
3. pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam
iṭantalip pāṭṭiṉ piṉṉar, naṇpaṉ talivaṉik kāṉkiṉṟāṉ; mukaccōrvik kaṇṭu iravellām uṟakka milliyō eṉṟupotuppaṭak kēṭkiṉṟāṉ. kāmattippaṟṟi veḷippaṭiyāka uriyāṭal āṇ naṇparkaḷiṉ maṉakkūṟu. uṟaṅkavilliyō eṉṟa viṉāvukku oru kuṟacciṟumiyiṉ taṇṇirpōlum meṉcāyal tīp pōlum eṉ neñcuratti avittuviṭṭatu;
ciṟukuṭik kuṟavaṉ neruntōṭ kuṟumakaḷ
nīrō raṉṉa cāyal
tiyō raṉṉaveṉ uraṉavit taṉṟē.	(kuṟun 95)
eṉṟu talivaṉ oḷivu maṟiviṉṟip perumitam tōṉṟa maṟumoḻi kiṉṟāṉ. ivvoḻukkam niṉ aṟivukkum perumikkum kuṭimikkum takumō eṉṟu iṭikkum naṇpaṉukku, `iviyellām  uṉṉiviṭa eṉakkutteriyum. avaḷ kuvaḷik kaṇṇip pārppataṟkumuṉ pēca vēṇṭiya pēccukkaḷ’ eṉat talivaṉ etirurikkiṉṟāṉ. (naṟ. 190) aṟivuniliyil niṟkum iḷampāṅkaṉ. “talivi ariyavaḷ; avaḷ niṉippi viṭṭoḻi (naṟ. 201) eṉṟu mēlum taṭutturippavē. talivaṉ āṟṟalaṉ āyiṉāṉ. kāmakkiṟukkaṉāyk kātaṟpittaṉāy kikaṭantu pēcal āṉāṉ.
kēḷir vāḻiyō kēḷir nāḷumeṉ
neñcupiṇik koṇṭa añcil ōtip
peruntōṭ kuṟumakaḷ ciṟumel lākam
orunāṭ puṇarap puṇariṉ
arināḷ vāḻkkiyum vēṇṭaleṉ yāṉē	(kuṟun 280)
iṟivaṉip pēcāta nāḷellām piṟavāta nāḷāka aṉpaṉ karutuvāṉ. pōrkkaḷam pukkup puṇpaṭāta nāḷellām puṟaṅkoṭutta nāḷāka maṟavaṉ karutuvāṉ. akam pukuntavaḷiṉ melliya ākatti mēvāta nāḷellām vāḻā nāḷākak kātalaṉ karutuvāṉ.
makkaḷ āṇṭu palavāka āyiram āyiram nāṭkaḷ vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. palar vāḻkkiyi niṉintu pārppiṉ, ellā nāḷum ciṟappiṉṟi, orunāḷ pōlavē kaḻikiṉṟaṉa. iṉṉaniliyi mēṟkāṭṭi kuṟuntokit talivaṉ veṟukkiṟāṉ. ituvari vāḻntati vāḻkakiiyāka avaṉ matikkavili. iṉiyum ivvāṟu celvati avaṉ virumpavilli. niṉiyattakka viṉicciṟappuṭiya oru nāḷāvatu vēṇṭum eṉṟum, annāḷ taṉ uḷḷam kavarnta kaḷviyiṉ ākattit taḻuvum nāḷē eṉṟum, ataṉpiṉ vīṇvāḻkki ciṟupoḻutum vēṇṭām  eṉṟum, ārāk kātalāl taṉ naṇpaṉ atirum kuṟikkōḷic collukiṟāṉ. kēḷir kēḷir eṉṟu tōḻaṉi aṉputōṉṟa aṭuttaṭuttu viḷittal kāṇka. uṇmi naṭpu maṟuppaṟiyātu; kāmattukkuk kaṇṇilli (kuṟun 78); annōy paṟṟiya talivaṉukku eppōtum uṟakkamilli (aiṅ 173); eṉceyvatu eṉṟu iyalpu uṇarnta tōḻaṉ talivaṉ coṉṉa kuṟiyiṭam celkiṉṟāṉ. muṉṉarē vantuniṟkum taliviyiṉ aṉpik kaṇṭu viyakkiṉṟāṉ (aiṅ 174) pāṅkaṉāl talivaṉukkut taliviyōṭu oru kūṭṭam nikaḻkiṉṟatu. itu pāṅkaṟkūṭṭam eṉappaṭum.
pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam paṟṟi 27 akappāṭalkaḷ uḷa. ivaṟṟuḷ pāṅkaṉ kūṟṟāy varuvaṉa iraṇṭē (kuṟun. 78,204) eñciya irupattintum talivaṉ kūṟṟāvaṉa. caṅkappulavarkaḷuḷ miḷip peruṅ kantaṉār eṉpavarē talivaṉ kūṟṟākavum pāṅkaṉ kūṟṟākavum iru pāṭalkaḷ poruḷ toṭarcci paṭap pāṭiyirukkak kāṇkiṉṟōm. caṅkap pāṭal ellām oṉṟōṭoṉṟu karuttuk kōviyillāt taṉittaṉit tuṟippāṭalkaḷē eṉiṉum kantaṉār pōlum ōriru pulavarkaḷ cila tuṟit toṭar amiyap pāṭiya kōvip pāṭalkaḷum uḷa eṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakum.
miḷipperuṅ kantaṉā:
kāmam kāmam eṉpa kāmam
aṇaṅkum piṇiyum aṉṟē nuṇaṅkik
kaṭuttalum taṇitalum iṉṟē yāṉi
kuḷakumeṉ ṟāṇmatam pōlap
pāṇiyum uṭittatu kāṇunarp peṟiṉē	(kuṟun. 136)
itu talimakaṉ pāṅkaṟku urittatu.
kāmam kāmam eṉpa kāmam
aṇaṅkum piṇiyum aṉṟē niṉippiṉ
muticcuvaṟ kalitta muṟṟā iḷampul
mūtā tivan tāṅku
viruntē kāmam peruntōḷōyē	(kuṟun 204)
itu talimakaṟkup pāṅkaṉ urittatu.
irupāṭṭilum mutalaṭikaḷ iraṇṭum ottaṉa. kāmam eṉṉum col paṇṭu iṭattiṟkēṟpa uyarkāmattiyum iḻikāmattiyumkuṟittatu pōlum, uyarnta kāmatti iḻivāṉa kāmamāka ulakōr karutuvar; av vuyarkāmam iḻikāmam pōla varuttamum nōyum taruvatilli, eṉṟu miḷipperuṅ kantaṉār, kātali nukarnta talivaṉ vāyilākavum, avaṉi iṭitturittam aṟiñaṉ vāyilākavum ulakōrkku maṟuppu urippar; kāmattiṉ ciṟappip pulappaṭuttuvar. ivvuyar kāmaḻāvatu yātu; aṉpuṭiya etirpālārik kāṇumpōtu veḷippaṭuvatu eṉkiṉṟāṉ talivaṉ. iḵtu ivaṉ tuyttuk kaṇṭatu. kāmamāvatu oru maṉanili; virumpiṉōri atu, viṭātu veṟuttōri atu toṭātu eṉṟu iṭitturikkiṉṟāṉ pāṅkaṉ. iḵtu ivaṉ tuyppaṉmiyāl vanta aṟivuri; allatu, kaṭami yuṇarcciyāl eḻunta uri eṉṟum koḷḷalām pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭac ceyyuṭkaḷ ellāmē aṟivuc ceṟivum ilakkiyap polivum uṭiyaṉa. kātaliyar tam kaṇ eṉṉum piṉṉā valiyuḷḷum, kūntal eṉṉum piṉṉiya valiyuḷḷum paṭṭa āṭavar tam neñcat tuṭippik kāṭṭuvaṉa. ip pāṭalkaḷāl kātalāvatu oṟṟumi vēṟṟumittaram pārttu ārāycci ceytu aṟivuniliyil nikaḻvataṉṟu; `itaṟkitu māṇṭatu eṉṉātu ataṟpaṭṭu āṇṭoḻintaṉṟē māṇtaki neñcam’ (kuṟun. 184) eṉṟu oru talivaṉ kūṟiyapaṭi, kāraṇam kaṭanta tūya maṉaniliyil amivatu eṉṟa uṇmiyik kaṟkiṉṟōm
aintiṇi ilakkiya māntarkaḷil 75 paṭippu iruvakip payaṉuṭiyatu. cekappiriyar eḻutiya `paṉṉiraṇṭām iravu’ nāṭakattil, ilīriyāk kōmakaṉ ārciṉō celvap perumāṭṭi oluviyāvik kātalikkiṉṟāṉ. ivaṉ kātalukku avaḷ iṇakkakkuṟippu nalkavilli. ātaliṉ taṉ pāṅkaṉ cecāriyōvit tūtu viṭukkavē avaṉ olīviyāvi nēriṟ kaṇṭu avaḷ taṉiyaḻakiyum ārciṉōviṉ peruṅkātaliyum kaviñaṉ pōl eṭuttiyampukiṉṟāṉ. mika nalam peṟṟa nī oru tamiyaḷāy mūppaṭiyalāmā? uṉ elliyaṟṟa aḻakukku ulakil orupaṭi vēṇṭāmā? eṉṟu iṉikkap pēcukiṉṟāṉ. iṅṅṉ.
taliviyāvāḷip pāṅkaṉ nērniṉṟu kāṇalum muṉṉiṉṟu moḻitalum tamiḻ akattiṇikku ovvā; tamiḻakam peṟṟa makaḷir nāṇattukku ēlā īṇṭu pāṅkaṉ taliviyit tāṉkāṇpāṉ, avaḷāl kāṇappaṭāṉ. talivi pāṅkaṉ taṉpakkamākac celvatik kaṇṭāluṅkūṭa, avaṉi oru vaḻippōkkaṉ eṉavē karutiviṭuvāḷ. taṉṉip pārttuvarat talivaṉ viṭutta tōḻaṉ eṉa eṇṇāḷ ēṉ? talivaṉatu tōḻarkaḷ yār eṉpatupaṟṟi avaḷukkut teriyātu. `tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭam pōlappāṅkaṉ uriyāṭi iṭiniṉṟu kūṭṭāmiyiṉ’ (tol. 1047) eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum, “taṉ vayaṟpāṅkaṉ avaḷvayiṟ pāṅku ceyyāṉ” (tol. 1443) eṉap pērāciriyarum pāṅkaṉ karumatti variyaṟuttal kāṇka.
- mūtaṟiñar cemmal va.cupa. māṇikkam 
putiya pārvi cūṉ 2016


akattiṇi pākupāṭu
pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam
kuṟitta iṭattut talivi vantu niṟkiṉṟāḷā eṉpatip pārttuc collum ōr aṟivippāḷaṉē pāṅkaṉāvāṉ. eṉiṉum, ivaṉ toṭarpāl akat talivaṉukku irunalam uṇṭu. iṉpa veḷḷattāl, tuṉpap puyalāl neñcu aliyumpōtu, avaṟṟip piṟariṭam pēciṉāl āṟutal uṇṭākum. kēṭpataṟku uṟṟa naṇparkaḷum naṇpikaḷum vēṇṭum talivaṉ etirpārā niliyil oru naṅki pāl kātal koṇṭu taṉ neñcakattip putiya uṇarccikaḷukkup paṟikoṭuttu viṭṭāṉ. kāma vaṇuvum kāma curamum avaṉ uḷḷattit tiṉṟaṉa.
ñāyiṟu kāyum vevvaṟi maruṅkiṟ
kiyil ūmaṉ kaṇṇiṟ kākkuma
veṇṇey uṇaṅkal pōlap
parantaṉ ṟinnōy nōṉṟukoḷaṟ karitē	(kuṟun 58)
eṉṟapaṭi, kaḷavuttalivaṉ taṉvayam iḻantu, kāmavayattaṉ āṉāṉ.
tirumaṇattukkuppiṉ talivi uṭaṉvāḻvāḷātaliṉ, talivaṉatu kāmavēkattukku iṭaṉilli. kaḷavoḻukkattō avaḷ kāṇaṟkum kalattaṟkum ariyavaḷ. ataṉāl avaṉukku mēlum kāmacuram perukiṉ uyirpōtalum kūṭum: aṟivu tiriyavum ceyyum. inniliyil pāṅkaṉ oru paṟṟukkōṭu. avaṉiṭattut taṉ taliviyit `teyvaccāyal uṭiyavaḷ, melliyaḷ’ arumpiya muliyaḷ, civanta vāyiṉaḷ (aiṅ 255) eṉṟu collun tōṟum ciṟupāmpukkuṭṭi kāṭṭāṉiyi varuttiyatupōla avaḷ melluṟuppukkaḷ eṉṉittākkiṉa. (kuṟun 119) eṉṟu colluntōṟum talivaṉukku maṉavēkam taṇiyavum, aṟivu nilikku varavum kāṇkiṉṟōm. talivaṉ kūṟṟāka 25 pāṭal iruppataṟku ivvuḷaviyalē kāraṇamām.
pāṅkaṉ talivaṉuṭaṉ cērntu avaṉ celluḻi ellām cellum vaḻakkamuṭiyavaṉ. viṭṭup piriyāta tōḻami koṇṭavaṉ. talivaṉ oruttipāṟ paṭṭa piṉṉum pāṅkaṉ muṉpōl uṭaṉvarutul takātaṉṟō. itaṉi avaṉ eppaṭi aṟivāṉ? talivaṉatu putiya uṇarcciyiyum putiya uṟaviyum avaṉtaṉ meyppāṭṭālum collālum terintu koṇṭa pāṅkaṉ kuṇamum kulamum aṟivum cuṭṭi māṟṟa muyalkiṉṟāṉ; payaṉiṉmi kaṇṭapiṉ avaṉōṭu kūṭa varuvati niṟuttik koḷkiṉṟāṉ. itaṉāl talivaṉukkut taliviyik kāṇavēṇṭumpōtu taṉittuc cellum peruvāyppuk kiṭikkiṉṟatu. ivvāṟu pāṅkaṉ talivaṉatu kāmapputumiyik kātu koṭuttuk kēṭkum ceviyaṉāy, mēlum ciṟitu iṭiyūṟum ceyyāta tōḻaṉāyk kaḷaviṉ toṭakkattē vantu pōyviṭak kāṇkiṉṟōm. aintiṇi ilakkiyattu ivaṉvaravu itaṉoṭu muṟṟum.
mēl viḷaṅkiya iyaṟkip puṇarcci, iṭantalippāṭu, pāṅkaṟkūṭṭam eṉṟa mūṉṟu kāṭcikkum kuṟiyiṭam oṉṟē ākavum, talivaṉ iṭantalippāṭupōl mīṇṭum ēṉ tāṉē ceṉṟiruttal kūṭātu? pāṅkaṉi viṭuttataṉ nōkkam eṉṉa? pal iḻanta kiḻappacu iḷampullit taṭavi makiḻnta maṉaviṉpamē kāmaviṉpam; kāmamāvatu taṉṉi uṇarntār uṇarātār eṉap pārātu ellāriyum paṟṟum perum pētimiyuṭiyatu; iṉi uḷḷal kūṭātu eṉṟellām kātalkāyum talivaṉip pāṅkaṉ nakiyāṭi iṭitturikkiṉṟaṉaṉ. taṉ vēṭki takkatē eṉṟum, iṭippuriyāl oḻiyātatu eṉṟum uṇartta virumapiṉāṉ talivaṉ. pāṅkaṉ taṉ colli nampāṉ; taṉṉi mayakkiyavaḷik kaṇṭāl nampuvāṉ; piṉṉar iṭitturiyāṉ eṉa eṇṇiṉāṉ.
amma vāḻi kēḷir muṉṉiṉṟu
kaṇṭaṉi rāyiṟ kaḻaṟalir maṉṉō...
naṟṟēr vaḻuti koṟki muṉṟuṟi
vaṇṭuvāy tiṟanta vāṅkukaḻi neytaṟ
pōtupuṟaṅ koṭutta uṇkaṇ
mātar vāṇmukam matiiya nōkkē.	(akam. 130)
`tōḻa! eṉṉip piṇittavaḷiṉ nōkku matar matarppu uṭiyatu. avaḷ kaṇ pacumikkuk kipaṭāta, vaṇṭuvāy tiṟanta, tuṟiyiṭattu vaḷarnta neytaṟpūvum nikarākātē. in nōkkaḻakiyi muṉṉiṉṟu nī orumuṟi kaṇṭuviṭṭāṟ pōtum; piṉ eṉṉik kaṭinturiyāy eṉṟu ēvavē, pāṅkaṉuma talivaṉ coṉṉa kuṟiyiṭam ceṉṟu, attakiyāḷik kāṇkiṉṟāṉ. `neytaṟpū maṇakkum kūntalaḻakiyiṉ piṭip paṭṭōr iravum uṟaṅkuvārō? pāmpu kaṭittaṉṉa pāṭupaṭārō?’ eṉṟu pāṅkaṉē uṇarak kāṇkiṉṟōm.
iravi ṉāṉum iṉtuyil aṟiyātu
aravuṟu tuyaram eytupa toṇṭit
taṇṇāṟu neytaliṉ nāṟum
piṉṉirum kūntal aṇaṅkuṟ ṟōrē.	(aiṅ. 173)
4. tōḻiyiṟ puṇarcci
caṅka ilakkiyattu 882 kaḷavup pāṭalkaḷ uḷa. ivaṟṟuḷ 40 pāṭalkaḷē iyaṟkip puṇarcci, iṭantalippāṭu. pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam eṉṟa mūṉṟu vakikkum uriyaṉa. eñciya 842 pāṭal - 95 viḻukkāṭu - tōḻiyiṟ puṇarcci eṉṉum oru vakikkē varuvaṉa. itaṉāl tōḻiyiṉṟik kātalarkaḷiṉ kaḷavoḻukkam nīḷātu eṉpatum, aintiṇi ilakkiyap paṭippukkut tōḻi eṉṉum āḷ (pāttiram) iṉṟiyamiyātavaḷ eṉpatum, tōḻiyiṟ puṇarccikkuriya tuṟikaḷē pulavarkaḷiṉ neñcik kavarntaṉa eṉpatum peṟalām. tōḻiyiṉ toṭarpukkup piṉnikaḻum kātaṟ ceytikaḷ aḷavila. avaṟṟiyellām iṅkuk kūṟap pukutal mikiyum kuṟiyumām, vēṇṭumaḷavē viḷampuval.
kātaliyit taṟceyalāka evvaḷavu nāḷ kāṇa muṭiyum. pāṅkaṉ evvaḷavu utavi ceyvāṉ? niṉitta viṭamellām cuṟṟittiriyum pōkkurimi āṭavarkkut tamiḻc camutāyattukku uṇṭu. aṉṉa urimi kulamakaḷirkkum kumariyarkkum ili.
yāyē kaṇṇiṉum kaṭuṅkā talaḷē
entiyum nilaṉuṟap poṟāaṉ cīṟaṭi civappa
evaṉila kuṟumakaḷ iyaṅkuti eṉṉum	(akam. 12)

kāppum pūṭṭiciṉ kaṭiyum pōkali
pēti yalli mētiyaṅ kuṟumakaḷ	(akam. 7)
iravum pakalum tāy tantiyiṉ kaṭuṅkāppukku illak kumariyari uḷḷākkuvatu tamiḻ nākarikam. “vaṇṇac cīṟaṭi maṇmakaḷ aṟintilaḷ” eṉac cilappatikāramum, “kaṉṉik kāvalum kaṭiyiṟ kāvalum vilimakaḷirkku illi” eṉa maṇimēkalik kāppiyamum moḻital kāṇka. orukāl peṟṟōr icivupeṟṟu veḷic celliṉum viṭātu tōḻiyum uṭaṉ celvāḷ.
yāmē piriviṉ ṟiyinta tuvarā naṭpiṉ
irutalip puḷḷiṉ oruyi rammē	(akam. 12)
eṉat talivi tōḻiyiṉ iṇipiriyāk kēṇmiyik kapilar ilporuḷuvami eṉṉum kaṟpaṉiyāl veḷippaṭuttuvar. ivviruvarum orunāḷ nīrviḷiyāṭap pārtta talimakaṉ avhtam kaṭunaṭpit teḷintāṉ. teppattiṉ talip pākattit tōḻi piṭittāl, ivaḷum appākattiyē piṭikkiṉṟāḷ. avaḷ ataṉ kaṭippakutiyit taḻuviṉāl ivaḷum ataṉiyē taḻuva muntukiṉṟāḷ. tōḻi teppappiṭi vaḻuvi nīruḷ mūḻkiṉāl, ivaḷum uṭaṉ mūḻkuvāḷ pōlum (kuṟun 222)” eṉak kaṇ cāṉṟākat talivaṉ kaṇṭu koṇṭaṉaṉ. tōḻi nīṅkā ōr iṭiyūṟu eṉṟum, tōḻiyiṉ tōḻamiyip peṟṟālaṉṟit taliviyiṉ tōḷip peṟutal aritu eṉṟum avaṉukkup pulaṉāyiṟṟu.
kaḷavut toṭarpit tōḻikku uṇarttuvatu eṅṅaṉ? talivi nāṇoṭu piṟantu nāṇilē vaḷarntavaḷ. ātaliṉ tōḻiyiṭattut taṉ putiya uṟavik kūṟavē kūṟāḷ. kūṟum poṟuppō kūcāp piṟappiṉāṉākiya āṭavaṉic cārntatu. tōḻiyum taliviyum oruṅku irukkumpōtu ceṉṟu veḷip paṭiyākavō kuṟippākavō kaḷavi nayamākap pulappaṭuttal talivaṉ muṟiyākum.

oṉṟu, irappāṉpōl eḷivantu collum ulakam
purappāṉ pōlvatōr matukiyum uṭiyaṉ
vallāri vaḻipaṭ ṭoṉṟaṟin tāṉpōl
nallārkaṭ ṭōṉṟum aṭakkamum uṭiyaṉ
illōṉ puṉkaṇ īkiyiṟ ṟaṇikka
vallāṉ pēlvatōr vaṉmiyum uṭiyaṉ
aṉṉāṉ oruvaṉtaṉ āṇṭaki viṭṭeṉṉic
colluñcoṟ kēṭṭī cuṭariḻāy	(kali. 47)
eḷimi, perumitam, aṟivu aṭakkam, aruḷ āṟṟla uṭiya oru nampi evvaṉmiyum illāta eṉpāl vantāṉ; niṉṉi iṉṟi eṉakku vāḻkki illi eṉṟāṉ; oru peṇporuṭṭut taṉ āṇ takutikaḷiyellām ki viṭṭāṉ. ivaṉ col nampattakkatō? taṉi oru peṇṇāl ārāyntu uṇmi kāṇum takutiyuṭiyatō eṉa ivvāṟu talivaṉiṉ tōṟṟattilum paṇpilum tōḻi īṭupaṭṭāḷ. avaṉ kuṟiyi muṭittu vikkavum eṇṇiṉāḷ. eṉiṉum taliviyi aṇukuvatu eppaṭi? kaḷavuc ceyti tōḻikkut teriyum eṉṟu aṟintāṟkūṭa avaḷ nāṇam poṟukkātē? meyyāṉa nikaḻcciyic colliyō (kali. 60), poyyāka nikaḻcciyik kaṟpittō (kali. 37, akam. 32) iru poruḷpaṭumpaṭi toṭaramittu, evvakiyāṉum taliviyiṉ melliya nāṇam ūṟupaṭātavāṟu, avaḷ neñcatti aṟiya muyalka eṉṟu tolkāppiyar vaḻi kūṟuvar.
meyyiṉum poyyiṉum vaḻinili piḻiyātu
palvēṟu kavarporuḷ nāṭṭat tāṉum	(tol. 1059)
makaḷiṟku nāṇuṇarcci mikak kūriyatu. oru peṇṇiṉ kātaluḷḷatti neruṅkip paḻakum maṟṟoru tōḻip peṇ viṉavattuṇiyāḷ. “eṉ tōḻi naṉi nāṇuṭiyaḷ (aiṅ. 205) eṉṟu taṉ taliviyiṉ iyalpi oru tōḻiyē kūṟutal kāṇka. tōḻi eṉṉa, īṉṟu vaḷarttat tāykūṭat taṉ oru makaḷiṉ kātaṟkaravik kēṭka añcuvāḷeṉṟāl tirututal nallavar nāṇiṉ nuṇmi aṟiyattakkatō? `taṉmakaḷ pōkkit tāyē aṟintukoḷḷaṭṭum; itu paṟṟi namakkeṉṉa kavali eṉṟu vāḷā irātu, vāy koḻutta ūrp peṇṭukaḷ orunāḷ irunāḷ alla, palanāḷ vantu vantu eṉṉiṭam eṉmakaḷ paṟṟic collukiṟārkaḷ. yāṉō avaḷiṭam atupaṟṟi oṉṟum vāytiṟappatilli. ētum kēṭkap pukiṉ, nāṇam avaḷik kollum. ātaliṉ ūrār collum ceytiyi mikavum maṟittu vittuk koḷkiṟēṉ”.
uvakkuna ḷāyiṉum uṭaluna ḷāyiṉum
yāyaṟin tuṇara eṉṉār tīvāy
alarviṉi mēval ampaṟ peṇṭir
iṉṉaḷ iṉiyaḷniṉ makaḷeṉap paṉṉāḷ
eṉakkuvan turippavum taṉakkurip paṟiyēṉ
nāṇuvaḷ ivaḷeṉa naṉikaran turiyum yāṉ	(akam. 203)
eṉa oru tāy kūṟṟil vittuk kapilar peṇpāliṉ nāṇac cevviyip pulaṉākkuvar. kumariyar tam kāmakkuṟippic collālum veḷippaṭuttuvatilli. eṇṇattālum aṟiya iṭaṅkoṭuppatilli. ākaliṉ, aṉṉavar akamaṉattik kāṇpataṟku oruvaḻi pōtāteṉṟu tōḻikku eḻuvaḻi kūṟuvar tolkāppiyar. aviyāvaṉa. 1. putiya maṇam 2. putiya kaḷi 3. putiya oḻukkam 4. uṇavil maṉam cellāmi 5. ceyali maṟittal 6. taṉiyē cellal 7. taṉiyē iruttal.
maṭalēṟutal
taliviyiṉ naṟkuṟippu aṟintapiṉ, kātalarkaḷ pakalum iravum kāṇavum kūṭavum tōḻi vaḻiceyvāḷ. iyaṟkip puṇarcci mutalāṉa muṉṉik kūṭṭaṅkaḷ nikaḻnta iṭattē nikaḻum. tōḻi kūṭṭuvikkum kūṭṭaṅkaḷō vēṟuvēṟiṭattu naṭakkum. iṉṉa iṭam eṉṟu kaḷam cuṭṭuvatu, tōḻiyiṉ poṟuppākum. ikkāṭcik kaḷaṅkaḷ pakaṟkuṟi, iravukkuṟi eṉappaṭum. talivaṉōṭu taliviyiṉ uṟavi aṟintukoṇṭa piṉṉum, tōḻi talivaṉukku iṭiyūṟu pōlak kāṭṭik koḷvāḷ. avaṉatu poṅkiya kātalukkuc cevicāyāḷ. avaṉ aritu muyaṉṟu ārvattōṭu koṇṭuvarum kiyuṟiyi ēṟka maṟuppāḷ. vēṇṭumeṉṟē kūṭṭam nikaḻāvāṟu nāṭ kaṭattuvāḷ. taliviyiṉ arumiyit talivaṉ uṇara vēṇṭum eṉpatum, talivaṉiṉ uṟutiyit tāṉ uṇara vēṇṭum eṉpatumē tōḻi iṅṅaṉam ceyvataṉ nōkkam.
akattiṇikkaṇ maṭal allatu maṭalmā eṉpatu paṉaṅkarukkāl ceyyappaṭṭa kutiriyik kuṟikkum. ik kutirikku āvirampū māliyum maṇiyum aṇital uṇṭu. (kuṟun 173) kāmuṟṟa peṇṇi eytappeṟāta āṭavaṉ mārpil elumpu māliyum taliyil erukka māliyum koṇṭu maṭalmāmēl ēṟit teruvil varuvāṉ (kuṟun 17, 182). ivvaḻakku maṭalēṟutal eṉappaṭum. itaṉāl taviṉiṉ tiṭpattiyum avaṉ kātaṟku ilakkāṉa naṅkiyiyum ūrār aṟivar; mēlum arampōlum paṉaṅkarukkup paṭṭuppaṭṭu avaṉ mēṉiyiliruntu ōṭivarum kurutiyoḻukkik kaṇṭu irakkaṅ koḷvar. iṉṉa kātaluṉukku makaḷik koṭukka maṟukkalāmā eṉṟu avaḷ peṟṟōrik kuṟiyuṅ kūṟuvar. ātaliṉ ūraṟiya maṭalēṟṟam nikaḻātapaṭi maṇaṅkaḷ muṭintuviṭum eṉa aṟiyalām. tōḻi, muṉ colliyapaṭi talivaṉatu kuṟikkuc cevicāykkātu, kiyuṟikkuk kikoṭātu kālan tāḻttumpōtu, iṉi maṭalēṟuvatu tāṉ eṇṇiyatu eṇṇiyāṅku eytuvataṟku vaḻi eṉṟu avaṉ avaḷiṭam kūṟuvāṉ.
māveṉa maṭalum ūrpa pūveṉak
kuvimukiḻ erukkaṅ kaṇṇiyum cūṭupa
maṟukiṉ ārkkavum paṭupa
piṟitum ākupa kāmaṅkāḻk koḷiṉē.	(kuṟun. 71)
ikkūṟuki tōḻikkuc ciṟitu kalakkatti uṇṭākkum. ataṉpiṉ tōḻi tuṇiyāl kaḷavu nīḷavum kāmam ceḻikkavum kāṇkiṉṟōm.
caṅkap pulavar mātaṅkīraṉār pāṭiya ceyyuṭkaḷ iraṇṭē (kuṟun 182, naṟ. 377) avviraṇṭum maṭal māt tuṟi paṟṟiyaṉa. `maṭalpāṭiya mātaṅkīraṉār’ eṉṟa ciṟappup peyar ippulavarkku vaḻaṅkiyiruttalik karutuṅkāl, iraṇṭu ciṟiya pāṭalkaḷukkāka ip peyar peṟṟirār eṉṟum, ivvoru tuṟiyil pala pāṭalkaḷ ākkiyiruppar eṉṟum tuṇiyalām. maṭalmāpporuḷ kuṟittup patiṉmūṉṟu pāṭṭukkaḷ caṅka ilakkiyattu uḷa. ivaṟṟuḷ, naṟṟiṇi 143, 152, 342, 377; kuṟuntoki 14, 17, 32, 173, 182 eṉṟa oṉpatum aintiṇikku uriyavi. kalittoki 138, 139, 140, 141 eṉṟa nāṉkum peruntiṇikku uriyaṉa. aintiṇi maṭalukkum peruntiṇi maṭalukkum nuṇṇiya periya vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu. maṭalmā ēṟuvēṉ eṉṟu vāyaḷaviṟ collutal aintiṇiyām. “maṭalmā kūṟum iṭaṉumār uṇṭē”(tol. 1047) eṉpatu ilakkaṇam. avvāṟaṉṟi māmēl ēṟiyē kāṭṭum ceyki peruntiṇiyāy viṭum. “ēṟiya maṭaṟṟiṟam”  (tol. 969) eṉṟa toṭaril `ēṟiya’ eṉavarum iṟanta kālaviṉi peruntiṇi yilakkaṇattic cuṭṭutal kāṇka.
pakal iravuk kūṭṭaṅkaḷ
kaḷavoḻukkam tiṭīreṉa niṉṟupōtaṟku iṭaṉuṇṭu. tōḻiyiṉ uṟutuṇiyāl atu nīṭittu ōṭukiṉṟatu. tōḻi peṇṇāyiṉum paṇimakaḷākaliṉ, taliviyiviṭat taṉittuc cellum urimiyum kaṭamiyum avaḷukku uṇṭu. kātalōrtam kūṭṭattukku takum iṭam etu? takuṅkālam etu eṉṟu avaḷāl kāṇamuṭiyum. eṉiṉum, avaḷum oru kumariyātalāṉum ēval makaḷātalāṉum cila ellikku uṭpaṭṭē utaviceyyak kūṭum. avaḷuṭiya cuṟṟuc celavum ōḷaravupaṭṭatē, maṟivoḻuk kattic camutāyap peruṅkaṇkaḷ pārttuk koṇṭirā; cuṟṟup puṟak kuḷavikaḷ koṭṭātu viṭā; eṇṇavūcikaḷ tuḷiyātu pōkā; ātaliṉ kātaṟkaḷavukku ulakil pukaliṭam illi. kaḷavik kaṟpākkiṉālallatu ulakukku uṟakkam illi.
`malarnta vēṅki maraniḻalil talivaṉoṭu okka iruntu katirtiṉṉa vanta kiḷikaḷi iruvarum eḻuntuoṭṭi aruviyāṭic cantaṉam pūci makiḻnta iṉpap puṇarvukku iṉi vāyppu uṇṭō? tiṉikkatirkaḷ muṟṟi viḷintaṉa. vēṭṭuvar koytu celvar. ātaliṉ puṉaṅkāvalic cākkāka vittut talivaṉoṭu aḷavaḷāvutul iṉimēl iyalātu’ (naṟ. 259) eṉṟu tōḻi talivikku aṟivikkiṉṟāḷ. talivaṉukkum atu kēṭkiṉṟatu. muṟṟiya tiṇi kaḷavoḻukkattiṟku mutal iṭiyūṟākum. talivi puṉaṅ kāttal illeṉavē, pakaṟkāṭciyumpuṇarcciyum illi yaṉṟē. piṉṉar iravukkuṟikku vaḻiceyka eṉṟu talivaṉ tōḻiyik kuṟiyuṟṟu niṟpāṉ. iravuk kaḷaviṉ toṭakkattuk kūṭṭat talivi vīṭṭiṉ puṟattu naṭakkum eṉṟum, cilanāḷikkuppiṉ accam oḻintu uḷmaṉiyilum ceṉṟu kaḷavu nikaḻttuvar eṉṟum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kuṟippiṭuvar (tol. 1076). “maṉiyōr kiḷavi kēṭkum vaḻi” eṉpataṉāl, vīṭṭiṉ puṟamāvatu vīṭṭār pēcuvatu kēṭkakkūṭiya aruku eṉpatu peṟappaṭum. putiyarāy illam vantār yārkkum viruntōmputal tamiḻartam illaṟamarapu. mālippōtu katavic cārttuvataṟku muṉ viruntiṉarāy veḷiyil niṟpār uṇṭō eṉakkēṭṭuc cārttal paṇṭip paḻakkamām (kuṟun. 118). oru camutāyatti atu nampum paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷik karuviyākak koṇṭē ēyttuviṭa muṭiyum. ātaliṉ kaḷavut talivaṉ taliviyiṉ illattukku māliyiṟ cellutalum, avaḷ tāy avaṉi viruntiṉaṉ eṉak karuti muṟiyāka varavēṟṟalum uṇṭu. eṉiṉum oru tāykku uṟakkam varavilli. kumari vāḻum vīṭṭakattup putiya ōr iḷiñaṉ varutalum, iravil taṅkutalum avaḷukku aiyatti uṇṭākkiṉa. tāyiṉ viḻippu iravup puṇarccikkuta taṭiyāyiṟṟu. etir pārttatu naṭavāmiyiṉ, makaḷukku orē ciṉam. aritāka oru nāḷ nam makiḻccikku uriya viruntiṉaṉ vantāṉ.pakivaṉ vanta ūriṉarpōla aṉṉi tūṅkavilli. kātalar tam iṉpattukkuk kuṟukkāka niṉṟa ivaḷ narakat tuṉpam eṉṟum peṟuka”.
peṇkoli purinta naṉṉaṉ pōla
variyā niriyattuc celīiyarē aṉṉi
orunāḷ nakimuka viruntiṉaṉ vanteṉap
pakimuka ūriṟ ṟuñcalō ilaḷē	(kuṟun 292)
eṉap peṇṇāka īṉṟu kumarivari vaḷartta tāyiyum makaḷ capikkak kāṇkiṉṟōm. paruvat tuṇiccal itu. peṟṟa makaḷik kāval ceyyum tāyip peṇ koli purinta naṉṉaṉōṭu oppiṭumpōtu, iḷam peṇṇiṉ ārāk kāma vuṇarcciyiyum, ataṉāl varum vekuḷiyuṇarcciyiyum, aṉṉiyi naṉṉaṉeṉak karutum veṟuppuṇarcciyiyum uṇarcci yuṭiyōr purintukoḷḷa muṭikiṉṟatu.
kātalarkaḷ kātalikkum toṭakkattut tammi etir nōkki yirukkuma tuṉpat tokiyip paṟṟi aṟiyār. atupōl tuṉpamaliyi iṉpak kaṭalukkuḷ tūkki eṟiyum vaṉmi kātaluyirkaḷukku uṇṭu eṉpatiyum aṟiyār. kālappōkku kātaliṉ pērāṟṟaliyum vaḻi tuṟikaḷiyum mella mella aṉṉōrkkuk kaṟṟuk koṭukkum. kātal veḷḷattu mūḻkkiya āṭavaṉ mata yāṉikaḷiyum cuḻittaṭittuk koṇṭōṭum kāṭṭāṟṟu veḷḷattip poruṭpaṭuttātu kuṟukkē pāyntu āṇ paṉṟi pōla nīntit taliviyik kāṇa naṭuyāmattup puṟappaṭukiṟāṉ. iravukkuṟiyiṭam vantu niṟkiṟāṉ (akam, 18) kūrtta matiyuṭiya maṭanti, murukaṉ pōluma muṉciṉam koḷḷum tanti vīṭṭil iruppavum, kāṟ cilampi ōciyiṭātapaṭi kaṭṭik koṇṭu, ēṇimēl ēṟit tōṭṭattu iṟaṅki, maḻi cō eṉap peyyum naḷḷiravil kātalaṉ akalattit taḻuvukiṟāḷ (akam. 158, 198) itupaṟṟik kēṭkum cevilikkum, nīkaṇṭatu kaṉavu eṉavum, vēṇṭiya vaṭivam eṭuttu malarcūṭi nam tōṭṭattu oru pēy vantu pōvati aṟiyāyō eṉavum tōḻi nakiyākavum nayamākavum vēṟoṉṟu colli maḻuppak kāṇalām. muṉpillāta āṟṟalum tuṇivum oṭpamum kāmattāl uṇṭākum eṉṟa aṭippaṭiyil iravuk kuṟippāṭalkaḷ amintuḷḷaṉa.
tōḻiyiṉ nōkkam
aintiṇi ilakkiyattut tōḻi eṉṉum āḷip paṭitta payaṉ eṉṉa? avaḷ tuṇiyiṉṟippakaṟkuṟi, iravukkuṟikaḷ nikaḻā; kaḷavoḻukkam nīṭikkātu; ātaliṉ tōḻi vēṇṭum eṉap palar karutuvar. ikkaruttup piḻipaṭṭatu. kaḷavu nīḷavēṇṭum eṉpatum, kātalāyiṉār iruvakik kuṟikaḷ nikaḻtta vēṇṭum eṉpatum akattiṇiyiṉ nōkkam illi. pāl vayattāl tāmē kaṇṭu tammuṭpuṇarnta kaḷavuk kātalarkaḷ kaṟpāka vēṇṭum; veḷippaṭiyāka maṇantu illaṟam naṭatta vēṇṭum eṉpatē ataṉ nōkkam. innōkkatti niṟivēṟṟa varupavaḷē tōḻi eṉa aṟika. itaṉāl tōḻi kaṟpoḻukkattiṟ kāṭṭilum kaḷavoḻukkattil iṉṟiyamiyātavaḷ eṉpatum viḷaṅkum. nōkkam niṟivēṟattakka kālam vāyāviṭṭāl, kaḷavu nīṭikkum eṉṟum, kaḷavu nīḷumpōtu pakaṟpuṇarcci, iravup puṇarccikaḷ naṭipeṟum eṉṟum koḷḷavēṇṭum.
kāmam kāḻkkoṇṭa iḷiya neñcaṅkaḷukkumēl ceyya vēṇṭuvaṉa pulappaṭā. eṇṇippārkkuma aṟivu cila nāḷikkut tōṉṟātu. ivviruvar ceykiyit tōḻi aṟivuniliyil niṉṟu nāṭukiṟāḷ. kaḷavu nīṭippiṉ, varattakum kēṭukaḷik kāṇkiṉṟāḷ. iḷiyaḷāyiṉum poṟuppu uṇarntavaḷātaliṉ, tirumaṇam ceytu koḷka eṉṟu talivaṉi iṭitturikkiṉṟāḷ. `ivaḷmītu vitta aṉpāl mutalikaḷ vaḻi maṟittuk kiṭakkum kaṭaṟkariyil mīṉ kūṭṭam tuḷḷum uppaṅkaḻikaḷi nīnti iraveṉṟu pārātu nīyō varukiṉṟāy. ivaḷō nī varum vaḻiyiṉ koṭumiyi eṇṇippārttut taṉ pētimiyāl kalaṅkukiṉṟāḷ. nañcuṇṭa iraṭṭik kuḻantikaḷukku varuntuvatupōlak kāmaṅkoṇṭa uṅkaḷ iruvar poruṭṭu nāṉ añcukiṟēṉ.’
koṭuṅkāl mutalik kōḷval ēṟṟi
vaḻivaḻak kaṟukkum kāṉalam peruntuṟi
iṉamīṉ iruṅkaḻi nīnti nīniṉ
nayaṉuṭi miyiṉ varuti ivaḷtaṉ
maṭaṉuṭi miyil uyaṅkum yāṉatu
kavimaka nañcuṇ ṭāaṅku
añcuval perumaveṉ ṉeñcat tāṉē	(kuṟun. 234)
eṉṟu talivaṉatu aṉpiyum taliviyiṉ aṟiyāmiyiyum taṉ accattiyum cuṭṭikkāṭṭit talivaṉukku varivuṇarcci ūṭṭak kāṇkiṉṟōm. “tirumaṇam tavirkka muṭiyātatu; iṉṟiyamiyātatu. itaṉip purintu koḷḷuvōmēl, nayamum poruttamum uṭiyatākat tirumaṇattic ceyya muyalvōm” eṉṟu aṟiñar peṉṉāṭcā `maṇaṅkōṭal’ eṉṉum nūliṉ muṉṉuriyil kuṟippiṭuvar. kaḷavoḻukkam tūyatu, kaḷḷavoḻukkam tīyatu. kaḷavuk kātalar maṉamācaṟṟavar, maṇantu koḷḷum uḷḷattiṉar, veḷippaṭṭa piṉṉum vāḻpavar. kaḷḷak kātalar tammuḷ aṉpaṟṟavar, maṇam eṉa niṉiyā vañcakar, veḷippaṭiṉ māypavar allatumāykkappaṭupavar. kātalukkut tirumaṇamiṉṟēl camutāyattukku vāḻvilli. ātaliṉ, akattiṇi kaṇṭa tamiḻc cāṉṟōr varivi maṉṟali valiyuṟuttiṉar eṉatteḷika. tōḻi kaḷavoḻukkattil pēriṭam peṟṟiruppataṟkum 882 kaḷavup pāṭalkaḷil pātikkumēl varivut tuṟikaḷāy iruppataṟkum tirumaṇak koḷkiyē ētuvākum.
naḷḷiravil talivaṉ varumvaḻi yāṉiyum puliyum pāmpum naṭamāṭuvatu. taṭam kuṟukalum vaḻukkaluma amintatu. maḻi iṭa miṉṉalōṭukoṭṭuvatu. marappontum ceṭittūṟum koṭip piṇakkamum uṭiyatu. naṭuyāmam eṉak kālam pārātum, uyarntu carinta ciṟiya iṭṭiṭi eṉa iṭam pārātuma, kātal valiyē valiyākat talivaṉ varuvatu tōḻikku nalla varavākap paṭavilli.
maṉṟal vēṇṭiṉum peṟukuvi oṉṟō
iṉṟutali yāka vāral	(akam. 318)
`tirumaṇam uṭaṉē ceytukoḷḷa virumpiṉālum ceytu koḷka; iṉimēl iravukkuṟi varātē’ eṉṟu tōḻi kaṭumiyāka muṉṉuṟuttak kāṇkiṉṟōm. vaḻiyaccam tōḻikkē irukkumpōtu, talivikku evvaḷavu irukkum? ceṅkuttāṉa yāṉi pōlum ōṅkaliṉmēl kayiṟupōlum oṟṟic ciṟutaṭattu iruḷil talivaṉ varukiṉṟāṉē, īram vaḻukkip paṭukuḻiyil viḻutalum kūṭumē aṅkiruntu aṭitāṅki aṇittaṟku āḷ vēṇṭumē eṉṟu eṉ neñcu eṉṉik kēḷātu uṉṉiṭamum collātu puṟappaṭṭup pōyviṭṭatu’;
evaṉkol vāḻi tōḻi mayaṅki
iṉṉa mākavum naṉṉar neñcam
eṉṉoṭum niṉṉoṭum cūḻātu kimmikku
iṟumpupaṭṭiruḷiya iṭṭaruñ cilampiṟ
kuṟuñcuṉik kuvaḷi vaṇṭupaṭac cūṭik
kāṉa nāṭaṉ varūum yāṉik
kayiṟṟuppuṟat taṉṉa kaṉmicic ciṟuneṟi
māri vāṉam talii nīrvārpu
iṭṭaruṅ kaṇṇa paṭukuḻi iyaviṉ
iruḷiṭi mitippuḻi nōkkiyavar
taḷaraṭi tāṅkiya ceṉṟa tiṉṟē	(akam. 128)
eṉat talimakaṉ kēṭkumāṟu tōḻikkuc colvatu pōlat talivi taṉ accattip pulappaṭuttukiṉṟāḷ. iravukkuṟi vanta talimakaṉi, `vaḻi koṭiyatu. uṉ uyirkku ūṟuvantāl ivaḷuyir eṉṉākum? iṉi iravil varuvatit tavirka. paḻaṅkaḷ toṅkum kāntaṭ cōliyil pakal nī variṉum puṇaralām (akam. 18), eṉṟu kūṟuvatum, pakal vantoḻukum talimakaṉi, `peruṅkaḷiṟu puliyoṭu porutu vali cōrum maliccāralil naṭuyāmattu varuka, ētattukku nāṉōm (kuṟu. 88) eṉṟu kūṟuvatum, `nī pakal variṉ ūrār alarukku añcukiṉṟōm; ātaliṉ iravum pakalum varātē” (akam 118) eṉṟu kūṟuvatum, aṭikkaṭi ivviṭam varuka, avviṭam varuka eṉa iṭamāṟṟi urippatum ellām, pārvikkut talivaṉi aliyavittu alikkaḻivu ceyvatupōl tōṉṟiṉum, avaṉ nāḷ nīṭikkāmal taliviyi maṇantukoḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉpatē poṟuppuṭiya tōḻiyiṉ uṭkiṭiyākum. tōḻiyiṉ ceykiyellām `varital vēṭkip poruḷ’ (tol. 155) eṉṟu tolkāppiyam eṭuttuk kāṭṭutal kāṇka. kātalarkaḷ aṉpuṭiyavar kaḷāyiṉum, kaḷavu eṉpatu eppaṭiyum kaḷavutāṉē. ataṟkuriya accamum iṭiyūṟum cūḻniliyum veḷippaṭa maṇantālaṉṟi eṅṅaṉam oḻiyum? itaṉi aṟivuṭit talivaṉ aṟiyātāṉallaṉ. maṇamē maṟivukku muṭivu eṉa avaṉ aṟintiruntum, accaniliyil ,kaḷavu tarum pēriṉpam karuti, ataṉil ciṉṉāḷ nīṭittoḻuka virumpuvāṉ. eṉiṉum tōḻiyiṉ muṭukkum vēṟupala iṭiyūṟukaḷum virintu maṇakkumāṟu talivaṉit tūṇṭum. caṅkap perum pulavar paraṇar kaḷuvu neṟikku varum muṭṭuppāṭukaḷi yellām aṭukki orupāṭṭil (akam. 122) colluvar. ippāṭṭu avalac cuvikkup pērilakkiyam. nāḷtōṟum etirpārttirunta kūṭṭam nāḷtōṟum vanta putiya iṭiñcalāl naṭavāmaṟ pōyiṟṟu eṉṟum, iṅṅaṉam ēḻunāḷ ēḻu iṭiyūṟukaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa eṉṟum, itu eṅkaḷ kuṟiyumaṉṟu, yār kuṟiyumaṉṟu, kaḷaviṉ kuṟi eṉṟum oru talivi ēṅki iraṅki moḻikiṉṟāḷ;
“viḻāk kālattuttāṉ ūhmakkaḷ tūṅkamāṭṭārkaḷ. ivvūrārō viḻā illāk kālattum uṟaṅkukiṉṟilar. orukāl ivvūriṉarum vāṇikam perutta kaṭitteruviṉarum turaṅkippōṉālum, eṉ tāyeṉa orutti irukkiṉṟāḷē, eppōtum veṭukkeṉṟu kottukiṉṟa avaḷukku uṟakkam eṉpatu oṉṟu varuvatēyilli. eṉṉi vīṭṭakattup pūṭṭi vikkum aṉṉi maṟantu oru vēḷi tūṅkiviṭiṉum, ūr kāvalarkaḷ viḻitta kaṇṇarāy vēlēnti mūli muṭukkellām kiṭukiṭeṉac cuṟṟit tirivar. oru camayam avarkaḷ tūṅkiṉum, vāl maṭaṅkāk kāval nāy viṭātu kurikkum. ōyātu kurittu ayarntu ak kūriya pal nāy orukaṇam kaṇ cāyiṉum, veṇṇilā vāṉattu niṉṟu pakalpōlap pēroḷi vīcum. matiyum taṇkatircurukki malikkuḷ maṟiyumpōtu, iṉṉāk kural koṇṭa kōṭṭāṉkaḷ vīṭṭelikaḷi irikoḷḷa vēṇṭi, pēy tiriyum naṭuccāmattu viṭātu kuḻaṟum. kōṭṭāṉum kattutal niṉṟapōtu, viṭivi aṟivikkum kōḻi kūvum. ivviṭiyūṟellām illāta nāḷoṉṟilli. ūrārum tāyārum kāvalōrum nāyārum tuṅkit tiṅkaḷum tōṉṟātu kōṭṭāṉ kuḻaṟātu kōḻi kūvātu oru nalliravu varap peṟṟēṉāyiṉ, aiyakō, nāḷtoṟum payaṉiṉṟi vanta avar aṉṟu varātiruntu viṭuvār. tittaṉ uṟantik kāṭṭuppāti evvaḷavu muṭṭuppāṭu uṭiyatu. avvaḷavu iṭarppāṭu nirampiyatu nam kaḷavu neṟi”.
ellām maṭinta kāli orunāḷ
nillā neñcat tavarvā ralarē
ataṉāl ......................
nocci vēlit tittaṉ uṟantik
kalmutir puṟaṅkāṭ ṭaṉṉa
palmuṭ ṭiṉṟāl tōḻinaṅ kaḷavē.	(akam. 122)
veṟiyāṭṭu
taṉmakaḷ mēṉi nāḷtōṟum melivatip pārttu naṟṟāy (cevilittāy) kavalkiṟāḷ. uṇmiyāṉa kāraṇam avaḷukkup pulaṉākavilli. marapuvaḻippaṭṭa tāyātaliṉ, immelivu murukaṉāl vantatu eṉṟu nampi, muruka pūci ceyyat tuṇintāḷ. ituvē vaḻiyeṉak kiḻavikaḷum kūṟiṉar. vīṭṭil nallatōr iṭattu naṭuyāmattup pūci toṭaṅkiṟṟu. muruka pūcāri vēlaṉ eṉappaṭuvāṉ. tiṉiyi irattattiṟ kalantu eṟintumurukaṉik kūvi aḻippatum, veṟiyāṭṭu eṉṉum āvēcak kūtti āṭuvatum, kaḻaṟcikkāyiṭṭu nōykkum murukeṉa moḻital avaṉ vāy marapu (akam. 22; aiṅ. 249). vaḻakkampōla, ivaḷ murukēṟappaṭṭavaḷ eṉṟu tāykkuk kūṟiṉāṉ. taṉ kātal nōykkup piṟitoru kāraṇam teyvaccārpu ākuka. kaṟpikkappaṭuvatik kaṟpuṭik kumari poṟāḷ aṉṟō? āyiṉum `eṉ nōy kātal nōy; talivaṉ mārput taḻuvalāl vanta nōy. kaṭavuḷāṉa nī itaṉi aṟintiruppāy. aṟintu vittum, marapaṟivu aṉṟi meyyaṟivu illāta pūcāri aḻikkat tōṉṟiya murukaṉē! eṉ colvatu? kaṭavuḷāyiṉum uṉakkut taṉṉaṟivu illi. ittakiya iṭaṅkaḷil iṉi vārātu vāḻka’;
aruvi yiṉṉiyat tāṭum nāṭaṉ
mārputara vanta paṭarmali yarunōy
niṉṉaṇaṅ kaṉmi yaṟintum aṇṇāntu
kārnaṟuṅ kaṭampiṉ kaṇṇi cūṭi
vēlaṉ vēṇṭa veṟimaṉi vantōy
kaṭavu ḷāyiṉum āka
maṭavi maṉṟa vāḻiya murukē	(naṟ. 94)
eṉat teyvatti eḷḷukiṉṟatu kaṟpu. kaṟpuc ciṉakkum pōtu teyvam muṉṉiṟkumō? kātal nōyik kaḻaṟcik kāyāl aṟiyalām eṉṟāl, ivaḷ kaṟpu mēṉmikku avamāṉam eṉpatu tōḻi karuttu; naṉṟāl ammaniṉṟa ivaḷ nalaṉē (aiṅ 248). eṉiṉum, ivveṟiyāṭu kālattuk kaḷavut toṭarpit tāy veḷippaṭiyākavō kuṟippākavō aṟiya vāyppu uṇṭu. aṟivittu viṭavum taliviyum tōḻiyum muyalvar.
orutāy kaṭṭuvicciyi aḻittut taṉ makaḷiṉ nōyk kāraṇatti viṉaviṉāḷ. muṟattil neṟkaḷip parappi eṇṇik kuṟi colvatu kaṭṭuvicciyiṉ vaḻakkam. kuṟi collumpōtu palvēṟu malikaḷic collip pāṭuvatu uṇṭu. ataṉpaṭi mutiya akavalmakaḷ (kaṭṭuvicci) tamiḻ nāṭṭu ovvoru maliyākap pāṭikkoṇṭu vantāḷ. taṅkaḷ talivaṉ vāḻum maliyip pāṭiyapōtu.
akaval makaḷē pāṭuka pāṭṭē
iṉṉum pāṭuka pāṭṭē
avar,
naṉṉeṭuṅ kuṉṟam pāṭiya pāṭṭē	(kuṟun. 23)
eṉṟu tōḻi iṭippukuntu urikkap pārkkiṉṟōm. piṟa malikaḷi mēlē pāṭātapaṭiyum, talivaṉatu maliyiyē tirumpat tirumpap pāṭikkoṇṭirukkumpaṭiyum kaṭṭu vicciyi vēṇṭiṉāḷ tōḻi. tōḻiyiṉ tuṇiccal tāyiṉ cintaṉiyit tūṇṭumaṉṟō? `immali yoṉṟil ivarkaṭku īṭupāṭu ēṉ? `naṉṉeṭuṅ kuṉṟam’ eṉṟu pārāṭṭuvatēṉ? makaḷ melivukkuk kāraṇam immalivāḻ oruvaṉō? tamiḻp peṇkaḷ kaṇavar peyar kūṟār ātaliṉ, avar eṉṟu cuṭṭiṉarō?’ eṉṟellām tāykku ārāycci tōṉṟukiṟatu. veṟiyāṭṭāl ivvāṟu tōḻi aṉṉikku aṟattoṭu niṟkavum, maṟi veḷiyākavum iṭam ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu. mēlum talivaṉi nōkki, niṉṉāl ivaḷ varuntavum, murukaṉāl vantatu eṉṟuvēlaṉ colla nī vittuk koḷḷalāmā? itu keḻutaki kol?’ (aiṅ 245) eṉṟutōḻi iṭitturikkavum varivu muṭukkavum veṟiyāṭṭu karuviyākiṉṟatu.
camutāyac caṭaṅkukkum kaṟpoḻukkattukkum oru pōrāṭṭatti veṟiyāṭṭiṟ kāṇalām. mutumi marapip piṉpaṟṟum, iḷami mutumiyi eḷḷum eṉṟa niliyil veṟittuṟip pāṭalkaḷ eḻuntuḷa. etirppuṇarccikaḷ pulavōr pāṭutaṟkuk kavarcciyāṉa iṭaṅkaḷām. ātaliṉ ittuṟiyil 40 pāṭalkaḷ caṅkavilakkiyattu uṇṭu. kāmakkaṇiyār mūṉṟu akacceyyuṭkaḷ pāṭiya peṇ pulavar. im mūṉṟum (akam 22, 98; naṟ. 268); veṟipporuḷaṉavē. ivvoru tuṟiyi āḻntu pāṭavalla pulami paṟṟi, `veṟi pāṭiya; kāmak kaṇiyār eṉṟu ivar ciṟappup peṟṟār. aṉṉi makaḷ pūcali oru peṇ pulatti pāṭuvatu ciṟapput tāṉē! kapilariṉ `veṟip pattup pāṭalkaḷ aṉṉiyiṉ aṟiyāmi, vēlaṉiṉ aṟiyāmi, tōḻiyiṉ etirppu, taliviyiṉ kaṟpu mutaliyavaṟṟit toṭarpāka namakku aṟivūṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. ellāp pāṭalilum kaṟpiṉ veṉṟiyē ilaṅkak kāṇkiṉṟōm.
alar
aintiṇi ilakkiyattukku aḻaku ceypavar uḷḷurp peṇṭukaḷ. avariṉṟēl avvilakkiyam uppiṉṟiyirukkum. `allatu ceytal ompumiṉ’ (puṟam. 195) eṉpataṟku nērmāṟāka ūrppeṇṭir ceyvatu allatēyāyiṉum, camutāya neṟikku nallatu eṉṟa karuttāl tamiḻc cāṉṟōr ūrāriyum akattiṇi yilakkiyattup pōṟṟik koṇṭaṉar. kaḷavilum kaṟpilum ūrārkku iṭaṉuṇṭu, (tol. 1107) ūrār eṉpatu āṇpeṇ irupāṟkum potuc collāyiṉum akattiṇiyil peṇṭiriyē kuṟikkum. vīṭṭakattuc ceytikaḷi aṭukkaḷip pukipōla nuḻintu kāṇum āciyum, kūṭiyiruntu muṇumuṇuttup pēcum tuṇivum eppaṭiyō peṇ camutāyattukkup piṟavikkuṇampōl amintuviṭṭaṉa. oru kumariyiṉ kātaloḻukkam paṟṟi ūrmakaḷir vāykkuḷ pēcikkoḷvatu ampal eṉavum, veḷippaṭiyākap pēcuvatu alar eṉavum peyarpeṟum. “ampal mūtūr alarvāyp peṇṭir” (naṟ. 143) “alarviṉi mēval ampaṟ peṇṭir” (akam. 203) eṉa iv vāyppaṭṭikaḷi akappulavarkaḷ kuṟippiṭuvar.
ūrār tūṟṟam alarāl pala nalaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. `maṇaṅkamaḻum kaṭaṟ cōliyil eṅkaḷ iṉpap puṇarvit taṭuttu viṭṭatē; nāḷtōṟum vatna tēri niṟuttiviṭṭatē’ (naṟ. 203) eṉṟu talivi cila palapōtu avarip paḻittālum `em aṉṉi eṉṉi vīṭṭiṉuḷ aṭittu vippataṟku ūrvāyaṉṟō kāraṇam; iṉi eṉ māniṟa aḻaku veṇṇiṟap pacaliyākip pōkumō? (naṟ. 63) eṉṟu ūrār vitālum, muṭivil alariṉ nalviḷivit taliviyum tōḻiyum aṟintē yiruntaṉar. orutāy taṉ makaḷic ciṟitu kaṭinturittāḷ. atu terinta mullinilap peṇṭukaḷ ivaḷukku ivaṉōṭu toṭarpu uṇṭu eṉa alar tūṟṟiṉar. ivvalar kēṭṭa talivi varuttappaṭavilli. taṉṉi avaṉōṭu cērttupa pēcappēca avaḷukkup perumakiḻcci. ūrukku uṇmi terintatu eṉṟum, iṉit taṉ vīṭṭār avviṭiyaṉukkē taṉṉi maṇappippar eṉṟum, kaṟpukku ūṟu illi eṉṟum naṉmuṟiyil alari matikkiṉṟāḷ.
oṇṇutāl,
iṉṉa uvaki piṟitiyātu yāyeṉṉik
kaṇṇuṭikkōlaḷ alittataṟ keṉṉi
malaraṇi kaṇṇip potuvaṉō ṭeṇṇi
alarceytu viṭṭativ vūr.				(kali. 105)
iṉi alarit tavirkkamuṭiyātu. ūr vāyi mūṭa muṭiyātu eṉṟa eṇṇattāl, kātalarkaḷukkut tirumaṇa niṉivu perukukiṉṟatu. maṇappatu uṟuti eṉṟa tuṇivālum, `iṉimēl kaḷavoḻukkam kiṭikkappeṟātu eṉṟa naciyālum’ cila āṭavarkaḷukkuk kaḷaviṉpatti nīṭikkum maṉappāṅku varutal uṇṭu. “māṇā ūr ampal alariṉ alarkeṉa, nāṇum niṟiyum nayappil piṟappili’ (kali, 60) eṉṟa pāṭṭāl alarukku nāṇāta āṇ piṟappukkaḷi aṟiyalām. mukiḻmukiḻtta ampal iṉṉum virintu malarpōla malaraṭṭumē eṉṟu oru talivaṉ tuṇivākap pēcukiṉṟāṉ. eṉiṉum ivvakit tuṇivu nāṇpiṟappu eṉattakka peṇpiṟappukku varuvatilli. `tuṇiyeṉat toḻuta teyvamē aṉpaṉukkuk koṭumi ceytatu eṉṉumāp pōla, kātalaṉākiya nī kātalikkuk koṭiyavaṉākiṉṟāy. uṉṉāl avaḷukkup paḻitōṉṟavum alar paravavum maṉam aliyavum kāṇkiṉṟēṉ. iṉiyēṉum varintu āṭkoḷ’ eṉṟu talivaṉ nāṇumāṟu tōḻi iṭitturikkiṉṟāḷ:
vaḻipaṭṭa teyvantāṉ valiyeṉac cārntārkaṇ
kaḻiyunōy kimmika aṇaṅkāki yatupōlap
paḻiparan talartuṟṟa eṉtōḻi
aḻipaṭar alippa akaṟalō koṭitē.			(kali. 132)
