ulaka eḻuttaṟivu āṇṭu 1990
va.cupa.māṇikkaṉār eṉṉum vaḻikāṭṭi
`nūṟukku mēl ūṟum’ eṉpatu oru paḻamoḻi. nūṟṟoṉṟu nūṟṟiraṇṭu oṉpatāka eṇṇappaṭum eṉpatu potuvāṉa karuttāṉālum av eṇṇikki kāṟṟōṭu kāṟṟāka illāmal ellōr eṇṇattilum eṇṇappaṭuma ciṟappittāṉ ippaṭi moḻi oruvariṉ vāḻnta vayatiṉ payaṉic cāṉṟāka kāṭṭum eṉpatu varuttu.
atāvatu ettaṉi āṇṭu vāḻntār eṉpatiṉum eppaṭi vāḻntār eṉpatē kaṇakkiṭappaṭum eṉṟa karuttiṉ aṭippaṭiyil maraṇamillāp peruvāḻvu kuṟippatē ippaḻamoḻikkāṉa ciṟappup poruḷ. antavakiyil  tōṉṟiṟ pukaḻuṭaṉ tōṉṟuka eṉṟapaṭi 17.04.1917 il tōṉṟit tamiḻkūṟu nallulakiṟkup palvēṟu vakiyil aṟimukamāṉavarē tamiḻcacemmal mūtaṟiñar va.cupa. mā. eṉṉum va.cupa.māṇikkaṉār āvār.
vāymiyit tāraka mantiramākak koṇṭavar, tamiḻ ilakkaṇa ilakkiyaṅkaḷil āḻaṅkālpaṭṭavar, etiyum maṟittōṭic cintikkum taṟcintaṉiyāḷar. aṉṉāriṉ nūṟṟāṇṭuk koṇṭāṭṭam 17.04.2017il niṟivaṭiyum niliyil avar paṟṟi urippatu camutāyattiṟkuc cālum eṉa eṇṇik kīḻkkaṇṭa avar tam pāṭali ārōvil ceyti maṭalukkuk kāṇikkiyākkukiṟēṉ. tarappaṭum pāṭal avartam uyirppāṭṭum eṉalām ām! uyil pāṭṭu eṉṟālum takum!
nallāviṉ pālmuḻutum kaṉṟukkilli
	naṟumpūviṉ maṇamuḻutum cōlikkilli
nellārum katirmuḻutum nilattiṟkilli
	niṟikiṉṟa nīrmuḻutum kuḷattiṟkilli
pallārum kaṉimuḻutum marattuk killi
	paṇṇarampiṉ icimuḻutum yāḻukkilli
ellāmē piṟarkkuḻikka kāṇukiṉṟēṉ
	eṉvāḻvum piṟarkkuḻikka vēṇṭum vēṇṭum!
itu avaratu viruppamuṟi (uyil) pōṉṟatoru pirakaṭaṉa vācakam! eṉ vāḻvum piṟarkku uḻikka vēṇṭum! vēṇṭum eṉṟatu ikkaviti eḻutiyavariṉ kuṟikkōḷ pōl tōṉṟiṉālum itip paṭippōr yāvarum tammit tāmē piṟarkku uḻikkum viruppit terivippatu pōṉṟa potumiyil uḷḷatāl va.cupa.māviṉ vāḻvu maṉita kulattiṟku muṉmātiri pōṉṟatām eṉalām! vāḻka! va.cupa.mā. nūṟṟāṇṭu.
- tamiḻākarar te.murukacāmi putuvi.


mūtaṟiñar cemmal va.cupa.mā. 
tuṉpamāli cevippulampukka centī!
ti.pi. 12.4.2020 cevvāy kāli (25.4.89) vaḻakkam pōl vāṉoli kēṭkuṅkāl mānilac ceytiyil vantatu centī! cevivaḻi pukka atu ceviyiyum, cintiyiyum orucērac cuṭṭatu! putuvip palkalik kaḻakattiṟkup pōnta mūtaṟiñarcemmal, muṉivar va.cupa. māṇikkaṉār amararāṉār eṉṟa aḵtu akamum mukamum aḻuṅka alaṟiyatu. “āṅkilamē veḷiyēṟu! aṉṉittamiḻē vēli peṟu!” eṉṟa muḻakkatti iṉikkēṭka iyalātē eṉavum, tirukkuṟaḷ vaḻi amiya irukkum camutāyamāṉa kuṟaḷāyattār, `tirukkuṟaḷ nam maṟi” eṉṟu kūṟuvatu, `nammāḻvār’, `nampiḷḷi’, `nañcīyar’ eṉpavi pōṉṟatōr vaḻakkeṉṟum valivu vaḻaṅkiya kural iṉi olikkātē eṉṟum uḷḷam maṟukik kavaṉṟatu. uṭaṉē ōr poṉṉāṭiyuṭaṉ kārikkuṭi nōkki vaḻikkoṇṭōm. pāvalar tiru. īvappaṉār 3-4-2020 ñāyiṟu (16-4-89) kāli mutal māli vari tirucci tamiḻakap pulavar kuḻuk kūṭṭattiṟ kaṇṭu kalanturiyāṭiyak kāl, kōvi nakarattil tamiḻvaḻik kalvi iyakkattic ciṟappākavum eṭuppākavum amikkattakka muyalvukaḷic ceyyumāṟu kūṟiya aṟivuriyum, ceviyuriyum cinti nirampappeṟṟa īvappaṉār vāṉolic ceyti vēḷ evvi maṟivu paṟṟi veḷḷerukkiliyār pāṭiya iraṅkaluriyāṉa `poyyākiyarō’  eṉap pūcal purintu vaḻikkoṇṭār. piṟpakal 3.45 maṇikkup putukkōṭṭi pukka piṉṉar māli ēṭukaḷil maṟivucceyti vantirukkakkaṇṭu maṉattuyar mikkōm. māli 6.30 maṇikkuk kārikkuṭik katirakattuḷ tamiḻkkeṉa uḻitta takicāṉṟa paruvuṭal amitiyākap peruntuyilil iruppakkaṇṭu neñcu koḷḷāta vimmaluṭaṉ kaṇṇīr perukki niṉṟōm. makkaḷum avartam uṭaṉ piṟantārkaḷum arukil vantu maṉam maṟukiṉārkaḷ. aḻakappā palkalik kaḻakak kallūri mutalvar pērāciriyar tiru. celvakkaṇapati, muṉivarkaḷ tiru. a.vicuvanātaṉ, tiru. ñāṉacēkaraṉ, pāvalar maṇi tiru. ā. paḻaṉi, muṉivar payiṟciyilirunta mutukaliñar palarum cūḻaviruntaṉar.
neṭuntuyililirunta mūtaṟiñar talimāṭṭiṟ palakaṇip pāṅkar iruntu paṭṭukkōṭṭip pulavar taṅkavēlāṉarum tamiḻakap pulavar kuḻu uṟuppiṉarāṉa pāvalar cittōṭu īvappaṉārum kuṟaḷāyamuṟi vaḻuvāmal tirukkuṟaḷ muṟṟōtal ceytaṉar. piṟpakal 3,15 maṇikku maturit tiru. pi.ṭi.ār. paḻaṉivēlrācaṉ avarkaḷ talimiyil iraṅkal kūṭṭam naṭantatu. maturi kāmarācar palkalik kaḻakat tuṇi vēntar muṉivar tiru. ilaṭcumaṇaṉ avarkaḷum, tañcit tamiḻp palkalik kaḻakat tuṇi vēntar muṉivar tiru.ca. akattiyaliṅkaṉārum, kārikkuṭi aḻakappā palkalik kaḻakat tuṇi vēntar tirumati. irātā tiyākarācaṉ avarkaḷum, tiruppattūrt tokutic caṭṭamaṉṟa uṟuppiṉar tiru. teṉṉaracu avarkaḷum, kārikkuṭi uṟuppiṉar tiru. irāma. nārāyaṇaṉ avarkaḷum, aṇṇāmalip palkalik kaḻakac cārpil muṉivar tiru. āṟu. aḻakappaṉārum, kuṭumpattāruḷ mā. pūṅkuṉṟaṉum, neñcam piḷakkum iraṅkaluri moḻintaṉar. tiru.celvakkaṇapati civapurāṇam muṟṟum ōti iraṅkal moḻintār. kuṟāḷayac cārpil īvappaṉārum, taṅkavēlaṉārum mūtaṟiñar uṭalil poṉṉāṭi pōrttiṉar. 4.30 maṇikku ūrvalam puṟappaṭṭatu. pacumpoṉ mutturāmaliṅkar māvaṭṭa āṭcit talivar tiru. paḻamali avarkaḷum, muṉivarkaḷ tiruvāḷarkaḷ irā. cāraṅkapāṇi, a.vicuvanātaṉ, civakurunātaṉ, mu. aptul kaṟīm, ñāṉacēkaraṉ, tamiḻaṇṇal, ci. nayiṉār mukammatu, cupa.aṇṇāmali, pā. namacivāyam, cavakarlāl, katirēcaṉ,maṇivācakar patippaka urimiyāḷar tiru. ca. meyyappar, pāvalar tiru. co.co.mi. cuntaram, muṭiyaracaṉ, ā. paḻaṉi ākiya aṟiñar perumakkaḷ kaṇṇīr coriyac cūḻa vantaṉar. maturip palkalik kaḻakattiliruntu iru pēruntukaḷ niṟiya vanta pērāciriyarkaḷum paṇiyāḷarkaḷum vantārkaḷ. māli 5 maṇikkuc centamiḻ māṇikkac ceḻum paḻam centī mūḻkiyatu.
ittaku kārikkuṭivāḻ perumakaṉār putuviyil 24.4.89 aṉṟu iravu nammiyellām peruntuyarattil āḻtti iyaṟki eytikaṉār. avar iṟutiyākak kalantu koṇṭa kūṭṭam tirukkuṟaḷ kūṭṭam eṉpatu aṟiya mēlum āṟṟāmik kāḷāṉōm.
tamiḻarkku oḷi kūṭṭi vaḻi kāṭṭiya oppilāk kalaṅkari viḷakkam maṟintatē! tamiḻ mūccum pēccum ōyntatē! kuṟaḷ neṟiyāy viḷaṅkiya perumali maṟintatē! taṉakṉe vāḻāp piṟarkkuriyāḷarāy viḷaṅkiya cāṉṟōr peruntaki maṟintārē!
iṉi avar pōṉṟa oruvari it tamiḻakam kāṇa ettaṉi nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ ākumō?
tēvāra ēṭukaḷit tillik kōyililiruntu kāttavaṉ aracaṉ irācarācaṉ eṉpatu muṉṉiya varalāṟu.tillit tirucciṟṟampalattil tēvārap pāṭalkaḷip pāṭa vitta perumi eṅkaḷ mūtaṟiñar cemmal va.cupa.mā. avarkaḷiṉ putiya varalāṟu!
tamiḻ nāṭṭil tamiḻ vaḻik kalvi maṭṭumē irukka vēṇṭum. ettaṉi vēṟṟu moḻikaḷi vēṇṭumāṉālum kaṟpikkalām. āṉāl pāṭamoḻi tamiḻtāṉ eṉṟa muḻakkatti māvaṭṭantōṟum mānāṭukaḷ naṭātti viḷakkam tantār. māṇpumiku kaliñar āṭciyil iḵtu eḷitāka muṭiyum eṉa nampiṉār. oruvēḷi muṭiyāviṭṭāl pōrāṭavum tayaṅkak kūṭātu eṉṟār. nām ceyya vēṇṭiya kaṭamikaḷil iḵtu taliyāyatu eṉa aṟikūval viṭuttār. iḵtē avaratu muḻuc cintaṉiyākavum iruntatu. ataṟkākat taṉ kip paṇattiyum celavu ceytu tamiḻ nāṭṭiluḷḷa aṉittut tuṟi aṟiñarkaḷōṭu nērilum, kaṭitavāyilākavum toṭarpu koṇṭār muttamiḻk kāvalar ki.ā.pe.vi. avarkaḷ ipperum paṇikku mūtaṟiñar cemmalōṭu tuṇiyāka iyaṅkiṉār.
muttamiḻk kāvalar amitta pulavar kuḻuviṉ talivarākavum nam mūtaṟiñar cemmalēpaṇiyāṟṟip perumi cērttār.
ceṉṉi, maturi, kōvi nakaraṅkaḷil naṭanta kuṟaḷāya mānāṭukaḷil kalantu poṟuppēṟṟu naṭāttiṉār. avaratu āyvum, ūkkuvippum kuṟaḷāya maṟumalarccikku vittiṭṭaṉa.
tirukkuṟaḷ nam maṟi eṉpatē nam uyirmūccu. āṉāl tamiḻakattiṉ tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷum, talivarkaḷum tirukkuṟaḷip potumaṟi eṉac colla virumapiṉarē oḻiya eṉmaṟi - nammaṟi - eṉac colla muṉ vantārilli. karaṇiyam avarkaṭku vēṟṟumaṟi iruntatē. inniliyil ciṉaṅ koṇṭa cilar emmi nāttikar eṉavum nākkūcāmal kūṟiṉar. nām maṉam kalaṅkit taṭumāṟiṉōm. inniliyil nammi āṟṟup paṭuttiyavi muttamiḻtuk kāvalariṉ - mūtaṟiñar cemmaliṉ pēccukkaḷum eḻuttukkaḷumē.
ceṉṉik kuṟaḷāya mānāṭṭil. “tirukkuṟaḷi nam maṟi eṉmaṟi” eṉa ēṟṟukkoḷḷāta tamiḻaṉi - nāṉ aiyappaṭukiṟēṉ... ām avaṉ piṟappiṉ kaṇ aiyappaṭukiṟēṉ eṉṟu kūṟiya coṟkaḷ emakkut teḷivu kūṭṭi uramūṭṭiṉa. nām cellum cāliyē tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟkum ulakiṟkum vaḻi kōlum cāli eṉṟa uḷḷat tiṇmi ēṟpaṭṭatu.
ivvāṟellām eṅkaṭkut tiṇmikaḷi avvap pōtu vaḻaṅkiya perumakaṉ mīḷāttuyilil uṟaṅkuvatu kāṇak kāṇa eṇṇa eṇṇa neñcu ellām vimmukiṟatē!
“īrōṭu eṉṟa niṉivu vantāl mutalil nam niṉivukku varupavar māṉa uṇarvukaḷi vaḻaṅkiya tanti periyār; aṭuttu niṉivukku varupavar nam aṉpiṟkuriya vēlā avarkaḷē” eṉa īrōṭṭil naṭanta uyarnīti maṉṟa naṭuvarkaṭkut tirukkuṟaḷ pēravi vaḻaṅkiya varavēṟpu viḻāviṉ pōtu kūṟiya mūtaṟiñar cemmaliṉ utaṭukaḷ iṉip pēcāp pērā iyaṟkiyāy viṭṭaṉavē! nām eṅku pōy aḻutālum ivvāṟṟāmi tīrātē! yām maruttava āyvil ceṉṉiyil iruntapōtu īrōṭṭil avar kūṟiyic coṟkaḷ emmi nekkurukac ceytaṉa.
yām uṭal nalam kuṉṟiya pōtu īrōṭṭiṟkum ceṉṉikkum nēril vantum, kaṭitaṅkaḷ vāyilākavum. īrōṭṭu aṉparkaṭku eḻutiya kaṭitaṅkaḷiṉ vāyilākavum avar ucāviya pāṅkum ūkkuvippum eṉ uṭal nalattiṟkē uṟutuṇiyākiṉa. tirukkuṟaḷukkāka vēlā nammōṭu nalamāka vāḻa vēṇṭum. uṭali maruttuvar coṟpaṭi tavaṟātu pēṇac collavum’ eṉa avar eḻutiya varikaḷi niṉikka niṉikkak kaṇkaḷ kuḷamākiṉṟaṉa. eṅkaṭkōr aṇṇal ivar pōl iṉi yār vāyppār?
ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ kālattil iḷaṅkō iruntār eṉpati viṭa iḷaṅkō kālattil ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ iruntār eṉpatē nām perumitam koḷḷum varalāṟu. atēpōl mūtaṟiñar cemmal kālattil kaliñar āṭci purintār eṉṟa varalāṟu nāḷi nām peṟum perumita varalāṟu. perum pulavar kuḻantiyiṉ irāvaṇa kāviyattiṟkup pēraṟiñar aṇṇāviṉ aṇinturic ciṟappum, mu.va.viṉ tiruvaḷḷuvar (a) vāḻkki viḷakkattiṟkut tamiḻt teṉṟal tiru.vi.ka. viṉ aṇinturic ciṟappumpōlak, kaliñariṉ kuṟaḷō viyattiṟku mūtaṟiñar cemmal aṇinturic ciṟappu kālattāl aḻiyāta kalveṭṭuppōṉṟatu.
ittaku perumikaḷi ellām uṭiya mūtaṟiñar cemmal avarkaḷiṉ iṟutip payaṇattil tamiḻaka araciṉ cārpil ōr amiccar kūṭak kalantu koḷḷātatu, kalantu koḷḷac ceyyātatu nām ellām tērnteṭutta tamiḻaka mutalvar kaliñariṉ kuṟṟamō, amiccarkaḷiṉ kuṟṟamō alla, iyalpākavē tamiḻarkaḷiṭam uḷḷa akkarip pacci mēluḷḷa mōkamē! kālaṅ kaṭantu uṇarvatilum ceyaṟpaṭuvatilum tamiḻaṉ tavaṟavē māṭṭāṉ!
mūtaṟiñar cemmalukku nām kūṟum uṟutiyāṉa vīra vaṇakkaṅkaḷ... tamiḻvaḻik kalvi iyakkamum, tirukkuṟaḷ nam maṟineṟi eṉum camutāyamumē.
aṇṇali vaṇaṅkuvōm; avar vaḻi naṭappōm.
- vēlā


Dr. V.S.P. Manickam - 
He who stood by his wisdom
We might have come across many scholars of Tamil, but none of them have the unique singularities that V.S.P. Manickam had. His sharp intellect, original and indigenous way of contemplating things the interpretation of the text in an unbiased way, the courage, that threw away all the unjustifiable commentaries and interpretations written by most famous and celebrated dignitaries, and above all his unwearying love of Tamil, mark his grandeur and lofty height of scholarship.
From the dawn of twentieth century, most of the critics of Tamil literature, applied the western rules of criticism to Tamil literary texts and interpreted what they could do in their light. A bulk of literature that belongs to the centuries earlier to Christian era, and several centuries before the birth of English, French and other western languages could have no justification to be viewed from the same angle of vision and with the same set of rules of criticism, V.S.P. Manickam knowing fully well that the classical period of Tamil literature was of a hoary past and it was of the birth when several countries did not emerge from the expensive oceanic surface. He clearly declared that Tolkappiam, the grammatical treatise most ancient among the avilable stock of Tamil works is the only authority and fundamental basis of all literary creations of that age. This is strongly advocated by V.S.P. Manickam and he is of strong opinion that Tolkappiam drawn from long Tamil tradition., stood unshaked through all centuries and even today it is the armour to protect aginst all intrusions and infiltrations of alien influences.
Many scholars in Tolkappiam have existed before V.S.P. Manickam, but he is of a different calibre. The other scholars followed the either Ilampuranar’s or Naccinarkiniyar’s or Cenavariyar’s or Perasiriyar’s or Theivaciliyar’s commentary and selecting one among them they discarded others. But V.S.P. Manickam in his exhustive study and research of Tolkappiam found out that all the commentaries in certin places did not echo the real meanings of the sutras. He insisted an honest impartial and unbiased interpretation will bring out the real meaning of the texts.
V.S.P. Manickam’s The Tamil Concept of Love’ is a monumental work in the research of the classical Tamil literature. Tolkappiam when describing the inner urge of love of a youth states thus:
To a damsel to whom love does not abound
The youth getting unimpounded distress
By prising and blaming
And composing the common features of himself and her
Telling her for not a reply
But rejoicing in his own
Is of one side love note
In this the youth approaches the damsel himself and utters exuberating love words to her. She did not reply. All the commentators sid that she had no passion towards him. But V.S.P. Manickam sid she had not attined puberty and no matured mind to accept or deny his love.
This is purely of men’s affair and the youth concerned will know that she is not the person for his anguish. Certin verses of Kalitoki provides good example for this. In the same way the unbounded love (Perunthini) is also interpreted as an unsuitable love by others. V.S.P. Manickam has clearly meant that is a love that rarely surpasses the convention and the expression in both hero and heroine excels the traditional way.
A clandestine love and the family life, the two major divisions in agam poetry have distinct way of expressions and Tolkappiam has lid down fitting rules for the composition of a poem. Most of the verses in Cankam classics adhere to these. But there are also exceptions where a few poets do not follow the convention of agam in their poems. In the agam poetry the name of the hero, heroine should not be mentioned, as it is the matter of strict privacy. Avviyar, Paranar and Velliveedhiyar by the way of simile have exposed the love of other poets by mentioning their names. This attitude of publicizing the privacy of others is vehemently  condemned by V.S.P. Manickam and he says that, this is not inkeeping with the Tamil agam tradition. Nobody else had chided this attitude like Dr. V.SP. Manickam, who points out the great poets like paranar and others had not depicted agam convention properly.
V.SP. Manickam’s research on the Tamil verbs and usages is a guiding work for all grammatical researchers to be done in the future. How the positive and negative meaning took place in verbs had been analysed in his work. The appellative and conjugative nouns and these functioning the active and passive voices. The subjunctive mood. infinitive mood and imperative mood are all brought forth extensively in his research.
His collected papers exhibit his untiring study and research of multifarious subjects.
His book, the Glimpses of Tamilology’ is the collection of articles by many scholars and he has edited it. In this, his article entitled ‘psychological approach to Tamil syntax’ shows how great poets purposely transgress ordinary grammatical rules to depict psychological mind set of the characters concerned.
Professor V.SP. Manickam is a puritian in the style of language, a Gandhian in principles of life, indigenous in research, a strict adherent of tradition and above all humanistic in behaviour.
Future researches of Tamil language and literature should refer professor V.SP.Manickam’s research works through which they can realize that this professor has contributed much and they could rightly be called archeological excavations of the classical stock.
His English books have to be viewed by all the Indian and foreign scholars which may trined them for a deep insight and inquisitive inquiry which will bring forth new concepts and theories of approach.
- Dr.K.V. Balasubramaniam, 
Former Professor of Tamil University, 
Thanjavur - 10.



kaviñarkaḷ
kaviyaracu muṭiyaracaṉ
pōtum eṉumaṉam pūtta neñciṉaṉ,
yātum avāvilaṉ, yāmiyeṉ ṟaṭaṅkiyōṉ,
payaṉila collāp pāvalaṉ, eṉṟum
nayaṉuḷa moḻiyum nāvalaṉ, eṉpāl
īṭilā aṉpaṉ, eḷiyaṉ, iṉiyaṉ,
kēṭilā maṉattaṉ, kiḷarccit talivaṉ,
eṅkaḷ caṅkap pulavaṉ ivaṉeṉap
poṅkum uṇarvāṟ polinta peṟṟiyaṉ,
perukkiya pukaḻnili pēṇivan tuṟiṉum
cerukkē aṟiyāc cīrmi yāḷaṉ,
arumi! arumi! eṉ ṟāṉṟōr pōṟṟum
perumitaṅ kuṉṟāp pēraṟi vāḷaṉ,
tolkāp piyanūl turuvit turuvip
palkāl ōtip pāṅkuṟa moḻintavaṉ,
uḷḷan teḷivuṟa ulakam makiḻvuṟa
vaḷḷuvan tanta māmaṇi, nāḷum
 cintaṉip putumiyic cevvitiṟ kāṭṭum
centamiḻ naṭiyiṟ cīriyaṉ, kūriyaṉ,
āḷvō rākiṉum avarkkum aṟivuri
kēḷpōl niṉṟu kiḷattum tuṇivaṉ,
ācāṟ ṟoḻuteḻum arumpeṟal māṇavaṉ,
pēcāṉ paḻimoḻi, ēcāṉ piṟari,
naṇpaṉ, paṇpaṉ, nayattaku tamiḻik
kaṇṇeṉa uyireṉak karutum iyalpiṉaṉ,
kalviyit tamiḻiṟ kaṟkat taṭiyeṉil
alviṉi yatutāṉ aḻikaveṉ ṟeḻuntu
valvil lampeṉac collamar toṭuttaṉaṉ,
amarkkaḷam ceṉṟavaṉ āṅkē māṇṭaṉaṉ,
emarkkelām tuyarē ēṟṟiṉaṉ, aiyō!
yāvaṉ avaṉeṉa viṉaviṉ, tīviṉi
ceyyāccemmal centamiḻp pemmāṉ
poyyā māṇikkap pulavaṉ avaṉē.		-kaviyaracar muṭiyaracaṉ

	kāṭcikaḷāyt tantē nāḷum

	māṇikka ēntal, innāḷ

	emiviṭuttum eṅkuc ceṉṟāṉ?

	venturukip pōṉa tantō!

	kāppiyam,nal tirukku ṟaṭku

itaḻkaḷ
muttuppantar
“nam aracu tirukkuṟaḷiṉ aṟattup pālukku ōr ilakkiyamāka oḷirkiṉṟatēyaṉṟi, poruṭpālukku ilakkiyamāka uyaravilli. ēṉ? taṉiyamum araciyamum oṉṟu peritum vēṟilli eṉpatu nam talivarkaḷiṉ tuṇipu. immaṉappāṉmiyāl, uṇṇāṭṭu ayalnāṭṭuk kōṭpāṭu kaḷi vakuttuḷḷaṉar. ataṉāl etirpārā iṉṉā viḷivukaḷ pukuntu viṭṭaṉa.”.
- mūtaṟiñar va.cupa. māṇikkam, tirukkuṟaṭ cuṭar, pa. 4.
“maṉa alic cuḻali nām virumpum aḷavukkuk kuṟittu anta niliyil niṉṟu maṉam ceyalpaṭuvataṟkāṉa payiṟciyē akattavam eṉappaṭukiṟatu. eppaṭi uṭaliṉ valuviṟku nām uṭaṟ payiṟci ceykiṟōmō atu pōlavē maṉatiṉ matinuṭpattip perukkuvataṟkāṉa uḷap payiṟciyē akattavam”.
- uḻavāram (ākasṭ 2016)
tamiḻ imayam va.cupa. māṇikkaṉār
peṟṟōri iḷa vayatil iḻantu ciṟu kaṇakkarāy iraṅkūṉ talinakaril vēli pārtata pōtu taṉatu patiṉeṭṭām vayatil vāymik kuṟikkōḷi vāḻvuk kuṟikkōḷākak kaṭipiṭitta va.cupa.mā. avarkaḷi ellōrum “poy collā māṇikkam” eṉṟē iṉṟum aḻikkiṉṟār kaḷ. muṟiyāṉa paḷḷikkalvi illāta niliyil paṇṭitamaṇi katirēcaṉār utaviyāl aṇṇāmali palkalikkaḻakattil paṭittu pulavar paṭṭam peṟṟār. piṉṉar pi.ō.el, em.ō.el., em.ē., paṭṭam peṟṟār. caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷil akattiṇik koḷkikaḷ kūhna kūyaaṭi ūṭinunayīva ṭika ṭuṭiena paṟṟi āyvu ceytu muṉivar paṭṭam peṟṟār. ituvē tamiḻkkātal eṉṟa nūlāka veḷi vantuḷḷatu. muppatukkum mēṟpaṭṭa tamiḻ nūlkaḷi iyaṟṟi tamiḻaṉṉikku aṇikalaṉāy cūṭṭiyavar.
va.cupa.mā. pukaḻ viruppamiṉṟi irunta pōtum, palvēṟu ciṟappup paṭṭaṅkaḷum virutukaḷum tāmākavē avari vantu cērntaṉa. mēliccivapuri caṉmārkka capi `cemmal’ eṉṟuma, kuṉṟakkuṭi ātīṉam `mutuperum pulavar’ eṉṟum, īppō pāvāṇar tamiḻccaṅkam `peruntamiḻkkāvalar’ eṉṟum ciṟappuppaṭṭaṅkaḷi vaḻaṅkiṉa. mēlum aṇṇāmali palkalikkaḻakam taṉatu poṉviḻā āṇṭil `mutu muṉivar’ (ṭi.liṭ.) paṭṭattiyum, tamiḻaka aracu tiruvaḷḷuvar virutiyum vaḻaṅkiṉa.
va.cupa.mā.viṉ tamiḻāl īrkkappaṭṭu tamiḻ payiṉṟōr ērāḷam. avaruṭiya tamiḻkkātal, vaḷḷuvam, kampar ākiya nūlkaḷip payilāmal tamiḻāyvu māṇavarkaḷ muṉivar paṭṭam peṟa muṭiyātu eṉpatu tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷ karuttu.
kōḻi taṉ kuñcukaḷi ciṟakukaḷukkuḷ vittuk kāppatu pōla māṇikkaṉār taṉ māṇākkarkaḷi kāttu vaḻi naṭattiṉārkaḷ. avarkaḷiṉ uyarvukku uṟṟa tuṇiyāka irunatārkaḷ. mikacciṟanta māṇākkar parampariyi uruvākki uḷḷārkaḷ. māṇikkat tamiḻ kaṇṭu vaḷḷuvamāy vāḻnata apperumakaṉāruṭiya nūṟṟāṇṭu viḻāvi avaruṭiya māṇavarkaḷum, palvēṟu palkalikkaḻakaṅkaḷum, tamiḻ ārvalarkaḷum, amerikkāvil pala tamiḻc caṅkaṅkaḷum veku ciṟappākak koṇṭāṭi varukiṉṟārkaḷ. cemmaliṉ nūṟṟāṇṭu viḻāvit tamiḻiṉa eḻucci viḻāvākavē aṉivarum naṭatti varukiṉṟārkaḷ.
paṭṭaṅkaḷ pala peṟṟālum, maturi kāmarācar palkalikkaḻakattiṉ tuṇi vēntar patavi vari uyarntālum, eṉṟum neṟikaḷ piṟaḻāta, vāymi tavaṟāta, eḷimi vāḻkki vāḻnta perumakaṉār va.cupa. tamiḻ vaḻikkalvi iyakkattukkāka taṉ iṟuti mūccu vari ayarātu uḻittavar. tilli tirukkōvilil tirumuṟikaḷ ampalam ēṟap pōrāṭiyavar. tam iṟuti mūccu vari tirukkuṟaḷiyum tiruvācakattiyum cuvācittavar va.cupa. avarkaḷ. avaruṭiya inta nūṟṟāṇṭil apperumakaṉāriṉ niṉivip pōṟṟi nāmum tamiḻ vaḷarppōmāka!
- alli naṭēcaṉ. (namatu ceṭṭināṭu- mē 2017)
`putiya collākkaṅkaḷi tamiḻ ulakiṟkut tantavar va.cupa. māṇikkaṉār’
putiya collākkaṅkaḷi tamiḻ ulakattiṟku tantavar va.cupa.māṇikkaṉār eṉa kuṉṟakkuṭi poṉṉampala aṭikaḷār terivittār.
kārikkuṭi aḻakappā palkalikkaḻakattil, maturi kāmarājar palkalikkaḻakattiṉ muṉṉāḷ tuṇivēntar mūtaṟiñar va.cupa. māṇikkaṉār nūṟṟāṇṭu toṭakka viḻā tiṅkaḷkiḻami naṭipeṟṟatu. itil, va.cupa. māṇikkaṉāriṉ taṟcintaṉikaḷ eṉum nūli veḷiyiṭṭu aṭikaḷār pēciyatu: va.cupa. māṇikkaṉār tamiḻukkuk kiṭittamāṇikkam. paṉmukap parimāṇam koṇṭavar
vāḻkkiyil vāymiyāy vāḻvataṟkuta tamiḻ oṉṟē vaḻiyeṉa, atit tērnteṭuttavar. taṉatu vāḻnāḷ muḻuvatum uḻippu, vāymi, nērmi ākiya mūṉṟiyum mutaṉmiyākak koṇṭavar. putiya collākkaṅkaḷi tamiḻ ulakattiṟku tantavar. avariṭamiruntu tamiḻ aṟiviyum, tamiḻ uṇarviyum iḷam talimuṟiyiṉar kaṟṟukkoḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉṟār. viḻāvil, tuṇivēntar co. cuppiyā talimi vakittup pēciṉār. va.cupa.māṇikkaṉāriṭam payiṉṟa māṇavarkaḷ pērāciriyarkaḷ tē. cokkaliṅkam, ku.ve. pālacuppiramaṇiyam, paḻa.muttu vīrappaṉ, irā. mōkaṉ ākiyōr pēciṉar. muṉṉatāka va.cupa.māṇikkaṉāriṉ iṇiyattaḷam toṭaṅkap paṭṭatu. ṭākṭar umiyāḷ rāmanātaṉiṉ vāḻttuk kaviti vācikkappaṭṭatu. va.cupa. mā. tolkāppiyaṉ aṟimuka uriyāṟṟiṉār. va.cupa. māṇikkaṉāriṉ putalvarkaḷ, putalviyarkaḷ, avariṭam payiṉṟa māṇavarkaḷ, nakariṉ mukkiya piramukarkaḷ uḷḷiṭṭa palar kalantu koṇṭaṉar.
viḻāvil tamiḻttuṟit talivar mu. pāṇṭi varavēṟṟār. pērāciriyar cu. rājārām naṉṟi kūṟiṉār.
- tiṉamaṇi - 18.04.2017
-kārikkuṭi, ēp. 18
va.cupa.mā.viṉ muttiri patitta kavitiyāka iraṇṭik kūṟalām. oṉṟu vaḷḷal aḻakappar paṟṟiyatu. maṟṟatu toṇṭu paṟṟiyatu
1.	kōṭi koṭutta koṭiñaṉ kuṭiyirunta
	vīṭum koṭutta viḻuccelvaṉ - tēṭiyum
	aḷḷik koṭutta aḻakaṉ, aṟivūṭṭum
	veḷḷi viḷakkē viḷakku.
2.	nallāviṉ pāl muḻutum kaṉṟuk killi
	naṟumpūviṉ maṇamuḻutum cōlik killi
	nellākum katir muḻutum nilattuk killi
	niṟikiṉṟa nīrmuḻutum kuḷattuk killi
	pallārum kaṉimuḻutum marattuk killi
	paṇnarampiṉ icimuḻutum yāḻuk killi
	ellāmē piṟarkkuḻikkak kāṇu kiṉṟēṉ
	eṉvāḻvum piṟakkuḻikka vēṇṭum! vēṇṭum!
	vāḻka! va.cupa.mā. nūṟṟāṇṭu!
	vaḷarka! viyattu avartam eṇṇam!
- appacci malar. navampar 2016.
vaṇṭamiḻ va.cupa.mā. nūṟṟāṇṭu
paṇpuṭimi eṉpatu tirukkuṟaḷil nūṟāvatu atikāram. paṇpu eṉpatik kaṇṇāl kāṇa muṭiyātu. karuttālē uṇaravallatu. āṉāl appaṇpuṭiya poruḷ paṇpi eṉappaṭum aḷavil kaṇṇāl kāṇum oruvarip paṇpuṭimikkāṉa aṭiyāḷamākak kāṭṭalām. aṅṅaṉam cuṭṭi uṇarttum oruvartāṉ ivvāṇṭu nūṟṟāṇṭu kāṇum vaṇṭamiḻ va.cupa. māṇikkaṉārāvār! ippaṇpiyē kaviyaracar muṭiyaracaṉ tam kaviti oṉṟil va.cupa.mā.vi,
“tīviṉic ceyyāc cemmal centamiḻp pemmāṉ, poyyā māṇikkap pulavaṉ” eṉap pārāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
itil kūṟiya poyyā māṇikkam eṉṟa pukaḻurikku īṭē illi. maturi kāmarācar palkalik kaḻakattiṟkut tuṇivēntarākat tērvu ceyta āṇiyi mētaku āḷunar avarkaḷ tam kikaḷāl māṇikkaṉārukkut tantu vāḻttum pōtu poy collā meyyar eṉa uṅkaḷip paṟṟik kēḷvippaṭṭa paṇpum uṅkaḷi uyar patavikku uyarttiyatu eṉṟārām. āka nūṟām atikārap paṇpuṭimikkēṟṟa poruttamāka nūṟṟāṇṭu viḻāk kāṇum aṉṉārip paṟṟi aṟivatu tamiḻip paṟṟi aṟitalāka amiyum. ēṉeṉil avarē tamiḻ, tamiḻē avar eṉpatē muṟṟilum uṇmiyām eṉka!
kaṭi ēḻu vaḷḷal pāri vāḻnta paṟampumalic cūḻal cārnta poṉṉamarāvati ūriṉ pakutiyil uḷḷa mēliccivapuri eṉṉum ciṟṟūril 17.4.197il avvūr ceyta tavattāl va.cuppiyā ceṭṭiyār - teyvāṉi ācci tampatiyarukku iraṇṭāvatu āṇmakavāyt tōṉṟiya kuḻantikkup peṟṟōr aṇṇāmali eṉap peyariṭṭaṉar. ceṭṭi nāṭṭu nakarattār kula marapuppaṭi 11 vayatil parmā ceṉṟu vaṭṭik kaṭiyil vēli pārttār. appōtu oru nāḷ kaṭi mutalāḷi collac coṉṉatoru poyyic colla maṟuttatāl poy collā meyyarāyt tāyakam tirumpiṉār.
tam peyarukkēṟpa aṇṇāmalip palkalikkaḻakattil pulavar payiṉṟu vaḷar piṟi pōl kalviyil vaḷarntu niṟi nilāp pōl kaṟṟōrkkut tām varampāy paṟpala uyarpaṭippāl uyarntār.
pārati coṉṉatu pōla eḻuttum teyvam eṉa eḻutukōlum teyvamāy va.cupa.mā.viṉ ovvoru tiṟaṉāyvu eḻuttum aṉṉāri māṇikkam pōl oḷira vittār va.cupa.māṇikkaṉār.
eḻuttup paṭippil avaratu tūriki pala putumikaḷip paṭittatu, `vaḷḷuvam’ eṉṟa aṉṉāratu nūlukku īṭu iṇiyilli. palvēṟu āyvukaḷ, nāṭakam, kaviti eṉap paṟpala paṭittār tolkāppiyam avaratu uṭal eṉṟāl caṅka ilakkiyam avaratu uyir eṉalām.
etiyum maṟittōṭic cintikkum tiṟaṉāḷi. putiya collāṭcikaḷi uruvākkam ceyta coṟciṟpi. kāṭcikku eḷiyavar. ārpparippu illā āḻkaṭal pulamiyar, etiyum etaṟkākavum virumpāta tamiḻ maṭṭumē virumpiya illaṟa vivēki.
1979il ākasṭu 17 ām nāḷaṉṟu, tuṇivēntar poṟuppik koḷḷa palkalik kaḻakattiṟkuk kālaṭi vittapōtu cātāraṇa veḷḷi arikkic caṭṭiyum, vēṭṭiyum tōḷil tuṇṭumāyt tōṟṟamaḷittār. avarik kaṇṭa palkalik kaḻakattār, `ittaṉi periya palkalik kaḻakatti ivar eppaṭik kaṭṭik kāppār?’ eṉa aiyuṟṟatit tam mēlāṇmit tiṟattāl māṟṟik kāṭṭiṉār. uruvukaṇṭu eḷḷāmi vēṇṭum (669) eṉṟa ayyaṉ tiruvaḷḷuvar vākkuppaṭi va.cupa.māviṉ ceyal tiṟaṉ paḷicciṭṭatu.
itaṟkuc cāṉṟākavē tuṇivēntariṉ nērmuka utaviyāḷarāka irunta tiru.ār.ār. cattiyamūrtti eṉpavar 1993-vikāciyil, maturiyiliruntu veḷivanta tamiḻmārutam eṉṟa itaḻil va.cupa.mā.vi.ṉa ceyaltiṟaṉ paṟṟi eḻutiya kaṭṭuri coṟcittiram pōliruntatu eṉṟē kūṟalām. atil eppōtum palkalikkaḻaka nāṭṭam uṭiyavar. itaṉāl kālatti virayappaṭuttāmal potu nikaḻccikaḷukkup pōvatit tavirttār. maṭalō, kōppō etuvāṉālum tāmē paṭittup pārttē kiyoppam iṭuvār. vīṇ celavillāta vakiyil palkalik kaḻakap poruḷ niliyil taṉitta kavaṉam celuttiṉār. entap periya iṭattupa parinturikkum cevicāykka māṭṭār. etilum taram oṉṟē nirantaram eṉṟa kōṭpāṭṭuṭaṉ naṭattic ceṉṟa āḷumi avariṉ mēlāṇmiyik kāṭṭiyatu - eṉṟa poṉṉeḻuttāṉa kuṟippiṟku mēl va.cupa.mā. avarkaḷi aṟiya vēṟoṉṟu vēṇṭumō? ikkuṟippē maṟṟavarkku muṉmātiri (cuṭiṭaṭa āṭiṉanaṭa) yāṉatu eṉalām.
va.cupa.mā. avarkaḷ kārikkuṭiyil aḻakappā kallūrit tamiḻttuṟit talivarāy, mutalvarāyp paṇiyāṟṟiṉār. aṇṇāmalip palkalikkaḻakat tamiḻttuṟip pula mutaṉmi yarāyp paṇiyāṟṟiṉār. kārikkuṭiyil kārikkuṭit tamiḻc caṅkattit tōṟṟuvittut tiṅkaḷ tōṟum tamiḻilakkiya ilakkaṇap poḻivukaḷi vallunarkaḷik koṇṭu āṟṟuvittut tamiḻ parappum toṇṭil tammi īṭupaṭuttik koṇṭār. ciṟuvarkaḷukkāṉa aṟanūlkaḷi acciṭṭut tantatōṭu annūlkaḷil pōṭṭikaḷ naṭattip paricukaḷ vaḻaṅka ēṟpāṭu ceyta attoṇṭu iṉṟum kārikkuṭiyil toṭarntu naṭipeṟukiṟatu.
avarkaḷuṭiya ottuḻippuṭaṉtāṉ kārikkuṭiyil irāmacāmi tamiḻkkallūri uruvāṉatu. itaṉi niṟuviya centamiḻc celvar irā.peri. periyakaruppaṉ ceṭṭiyāriṉ tamiḻ uṇarvukku uram ūṭṭi vaḷartta kāraṇattāltāṉ tamiḻka kallūriyiṉ tōṟṟattiṟku muṉa intiyap pal tamiḻkkaḻakam eṉṟa oṉṟi 1962il toṭaṅkiṉār. piṉṉar akkaḻakam 1967il tamiḻkkallūriyāka uruvāka va.cupa.mā. utaviṉārkaḷ.
makāvitvāṉmīṉāṭci cuntaram piḷḷikkuk kiṭitta tamiḻttāttā u.vē.cā. pōl paṇṭitamaṇikkuk kiṭitta tavaccīṭarāka viḷaṅkukiṟār va.cupa.mā. eṉṟāl atu poruttamē ākum.
cemmal avarkaḷip palavāṟākat tamiḻkūṟu nallulakam aṟintiruntālum avartam kavitikaḷ cila avaric caṅkap pulavarāka aṭiyāḷaṅ kāṭṭiyatu eṉpati maṟuttaṟkilli. avar eḻutiya `nellikkaṉi’ nāṭaka nūlil, katāpāttira uriyāṭaliṉ ūṭē eḻutiya kīḻkkaṇṭa pāṭaliṉ ciṟappē ciṟappu eṉalām appāṭal iyaṟkit toṇṭu eṉum talippil taṉiyākavum avartam māmalarkaḷ eṉṟa kaviti nūlilum iṭam peṟṟuḷḷatu.
nallāviṉ pālmuḻutum kaṉṟuk killi!
	naṟumpūviṉ maṇammuḻutum cōlik killi!
nellākum katirmuḻutum nilattuk killi!
	niṟikiṉṟa nīrmuḻutum kuḷattuk killi!
pallārum kaṉimuḻutum marattuk killi!
	paṇṇarampiṉ icimuḻutum yāḻuk killi!
ellāmē piṟarkkuḻikkak kāṇu kiṉṟēṉ
	eṉvāḻvum piṟarkkuḻikka vēṇṭum! vēṇṭum!
ippāṭalil va.cupa.mā.viṉ, āṉmā uḷḷatāltāṉ `eṉvāḻvum piṟarkkuḻikka vēṇṭum vēṇṭum’ eṉṟār. ituvē aṉṉāriṉ aṟivuriyākum.
aṉṉārtam nūṟṟāṇṭil avariṉ vāymiyum tūymit toṇṭum ellōrkkum eṭuttukkāṭṭāy amivatākuka!
- tamiḻākarar te. murukacāmi (putuccēri)
 tilakavatiyār tiruvaruḷ ātīṉac ceyti maṭal, pip-mār - 2017
muttup pantar
1. “maṇivācakar tamiḻ akattiṇiyil peritum īṭupāṭṭiṉar; ataṉip parappum ārvattiṉar. `uyar matiṟ kūṭaliṉ āynta oṇtīn tamiḻiṉ tuṟivāy nuḻintaṉiyō’ eṉṟu pāṅkaṉi viṉavac ceyyum mukattāl akat tamiḻil tām ceyta ārāycciyip pulappaṭuttiyavar. - mūtaṟiñar va.cupa. māṇikkam, ilakkiyac cāṟu. pa. 98
- juli 2016

2. “ulakap poruḷkaḷi aṟitaṟkuk kaṇṇiṟ ciṟanta uṟuppilli. atu pōla nāṭṭuppaṟṟu uyiriṟ paṭivataṟku moḻiyil ciṟanta vaḻiyilli. `pārata tēcam eṉṟu colluvār’ eṉṟa tamiḻ eṉ uyiriṟ pukuvatu pōla em moḻiyiṟ colliṉum puka māṭṭātu ēṉiya moḻi vāyilāka nāṭṭup paṟṟu ūṭṭuvōm eṉpatu `kallāp pullarkku nallōr coṉṉa poruḷpōla ākiviṭum. ivvoru uṇmiyiyēṉum uṇarntavarkaḷ nāṭu moḻi eṉṟa iraṇṭiyum uyir meyyākavē pōṟṟuvārkaḷ; nāṭu peritu moḻi ciṟitu eṉap piriviṉi pēcārkaḷ”.
- mūtaṟiñar va.cupa. māṇikkam, intiya āṭci moḻi, pa. 80 (mē 2016)

3. “vāḻviṉ ōṭṭattil eṇṇip pārttāl nām nammiyaṟiyāmalē evvevaṟṟiyō viṭṭuc celkiṉṟōm. palar viṭṭuc cellavum kāṇkiṉṟōm. ivaṟṟi niṉivupaṭuttuvatutāṉ tiruvācakam. tiruvācaka niṉippu mēṉmēlum nam uḷḷattu vaḷarntāl, viṭātu vittuk koṇṭiruppavaṟṟi uriya uriya kālattil viṭṭu viṭṭu uyarnta uyirp pakkuvam peṟuvōm. immip peruvāḻvukkē tiruvācaka niṉippu ellōrkkum vēṇṭum.”
- mūtaṟiñar va.cupa. māṇikkam, ilakkiyac cāṟu, pa. 83,  mārc 2016

4. tirukkuṟaḷil moḻi viḷakkattiyē kaṇṭum kāṭṭiyum varum nām iṉi taṉṉilip pārviyik kāṇumāṟu muyalavēṇṭum. im muyalvu ovvoruvariṉ taṉi muyalvākum. taṉ vāy tiṟāviṭṭāl taṉ vayi nirampātatu pōlat taṉippārvi illāviṭṭāl taṉ vaḷam irātu. 
- mūtaṟiñar va.cupa. māṇikkam, tirukkuṟaṭcuṭar pak. 49.

5. 35 āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ perumakaṉ kānti innāḷ cuṭṭuk kollappaṭṭār. aṭikaḷāriṉ vāḻkki veḷippaṭi. aticayam; aṟputama eṉṟa katikaḷ illāta nampikkikkuriya varalāṟu. uṇmiyilē meyyāṉa aṟputa vāḻkaki. ulakam peṟṟa periyavarkaḷuḷ mikap periyavar. māṉiṭavāṟṟal nala vaḻiyil elliyillātatu eṉṟu vāḻntu kāṭṭiyavar. ulakap pōrāṭṭaṅkaḷi iyakkiyavar, intiya viṭutali ulakam niṉikkiṉṟatu. intiyā maṟakkiṉṟatu. piṉpaṟṟuvatāka ēykkiṉṟatu.
-pēraṟiñar va.cupa. māṇikkaṉār, taṟcintaṉikaḷ pak. 21

11 tamiḻaṟiñar kuṭumpattukku rū. 66 laṭcam parivut tohki
ceṉṉi mār. 22: ciṟanta tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷ 11 pēriṉ kuṭumpattukku, rū. 66 laṭcam parivut tokiyi, mutalvar karuṇāniti vaḻaṅkiṉār.
māyūram vētanāyakam piḷḷi, cakti vi. kōvintaṉ, te. po.mīṉāṭcicuntaraṉār, ta.nā. kuppucāmi, kā.cu.piḷḷi, pulavar kulām kātiṟu nāvalar, ṭākṭar ci. ilakkuvaṉār, ti.vi. catācivap paṇṭārattār, makāvitvāṉ taṇṭapāṇi tēcikar, ti.ja.raṅkarājaṉ, nāraṇa. turikkaṇṇaṉ, ṭākṭar mā. rācamāṇikkaṉār, ṭākṭar va.cupa. māṇikkam, pulavar kā.kōvintaṉ ākiya 14 tamiḻ aṟiñarkaḷiṉ nūlkaḷi nāṭṭuṭamiyākki avarkaḷiṉ kuṭumpattukku talā rū.6 ilaṭcam vītam rū. 84 laṭcam parivut tokiyi, mutalvar karuṇāniti nēṟṟu vaḻaṅkiṉār.
- tiṉakaraṉ maturi 22 mārc 2007.
vāymiyāl uyarntavar!
maturi palkalik kaḻaka tuṇi vēntar tiru. va.cupa. māṇikkam kalvit tuṟikku vantatil cuviyāṉa varalāṟu uṇṭu.
patiṉeṭṭāvatu vayatil avar raṅkūṉil oru kaṭiyil vēli ceytu vantār. avaratu mutalāḷiyip pārkka oru nāḷ oru viyāpāri vantār. āṉāl avarip pārkka virumpāta mutalāḷi, māṇikkattiṭam `nāṉ iṉṟu vīṭṭil illi” eṉṟu kūṟi viṭu’ eṉṟārām. 
māṇikkattukkup poy kūṟa viruppamilli. “nāṉ poy kūṟamāṭṭēṉ. ataṟku vēṟu āḷpārttukkoḷḷuṅkaḷ” eṉṟu kūṟi akkaṭiyi viṭṭu vilakiviṭṭār. piṉpu kalvit tuṟiyil kavaṉam celutti pi.ō.el. em. ō.el, pi.liṭ. mutaliya paṭṭaṅkaḷip peṟṟu iṉṟu tuṇi vēntarāka uyarntirukkiṟār. `vāymi’ avari uyarttiyirukkiṟatu.
- 1982 cūṉ 27 kalki itaḻil veḷivantatu.
tamiḻvaḻik kalviyiyakkam
aṉṉit tamiḻē vēli peṟu:
āṅkilamē veḷiyēṟu
eṉṟa perumuḻakkattōṭu, tamiḻk koṭikaḷi ēntik koṭu, palatuṟip potumakkaḷum, toḻilatiparkaḷum vaḻakkaṟiñarkaḷum, eḻuttāḷarkaḷum, āciriyarkaḷum, māṇavarkaḷum muṉṉit tuṇi vēntar mūtaṟiñar cemmal va.cupa. māṇikkaṉār talimiyil, maturik kūṭalaḻakar perumāḷ kōyil muṉpiruntu 12-6-88 ñāyiṟu māli 6 maṇikku ūrvalam toṭaṅkiṉārkaḷ. kāvalarkaḷ tuṇi vantaṉar. ūrvalam nāṉku mācivīti vaḻiyākavum tamiḻē vēli peṟu: āṅkilamē veḷiyēṟu eṉṟa muḻakkamiṭṭukkoṇṭu ceṉṟu cāṉcirāṇit tiṭali aṭintatu. vītiyeṅkum tiraḷāṉa makkaḷ kūṭiniṉṟu, tamiḻ muḻakkatti amitiyāka varavēṟṟatōṭu, kaṭcivūrvalam, vēli niṟutta ūrvalaṅkaḷiyē kaṇṭu kacappaṭinta potumakkaṭkup perun tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷiṉ moḻi ūrvalam iṉimiyākavum ēṟpākavum iruntatu. kalvikkuḻut talivar tiru. a. caṅkaranārāyaṇaṉ ūrvalattit toṭaṅki vittār. mūtaṟiñar va.cupa. māṇikkaṉār talimiyil tamiḻ vaḻik kalviyiyakkak kūṭṭam 12-6-88 ām nāḷ iravu 7.30 mutal 10.30 maṇivari naṭantatu. maturik kāmarācar palkalik kaḻakap pērāciriyarkaḷ aṟiñar tamiḻaṇṇal, aṟiñar cāmpacivaṉār, tamiḻppāvi āciriyar karuṇitācaṉ, mutalvar aṟiñar cupa. aṇṇāmali, pērāciriyar cāttiyā, pulavar iḷaṅkumaraṉ antamāṉ periṇāṉṭar, mutalvar aṟiñar tamiḻkkuṭimakaṉ vaḻakkaṟiñar irāmacāmi ellōrum uriyāṟṟiṉar. ivarkaḷ colliya karuttukkaḷ varumāṟu.
1. irumoḻikkoḷki, mummoḻik koḷki eṉpavi kuḻappamāṉavi, poruntātavi. tamiḻakattil maḻali mutal palkalik kaḻakam vari ellā niliyilum ellāt tuṟiyilum toḻiṟ kalviyilum tamiḻ oṉṟē payiṟṟumoḻi. inta oru moḻik koḷkiyē uṇmik koḷki. ēṉiya moḻikaḷik kaṟpatellām tuṇimoḻik koḷkiyeṉa iraṇṭāvatākak karutappaṭavēṇṭum. tamiḻakattil tamiḻ moḻikku uriya mutaliṭattiyum mēlāṇmiyiyum camamāka vēṟu enta moḻikkum vaḻaṅkaviyalātu. piṟa moḻikaḷi evvaḷavu vēṇṭumāṉālum tuṇi moḻikaḷākak kaṟkalām, kaṟkavēṇṭum, kaṟkum vāyppukaḷ vēṇṭum; eṉṟālum ivi payiṟṟu moḻiyākak kūṭātu, tamiḻukku uriya urimiyip paṟikkakkūṭātu eṅkum etilum tamiḻ oṉṟē payiṟṟu moḻi. ituvē oru moḻik koḷki. itukāṟum āṅkilam mutaṉmoḻiyākavum tamiḻ paṉmoḻiyuḷ oṉṟākavum uḷa. paṉmoḻi eṉṟa niliyil vēṟu moḻikaḷi eṭuttuk koṇṭu, tamiḻip puṟakkaṇittu viṭukiṉṟaṉar. ciṟu tamiḻkūṭa illāmal, paṭṭamum pataviyum peṟum muṟiyaṟṟa tīyūḻ it tamiḻ nāṭṭil uṇṭu ātaliṉ tamiḻnāṭṭil tamiḻ oṉṟē maḻali mutal palkali vari payiṟṟu moḻi eṉṟa orumoḻik koḷkiyit tamiḻvaḻik kalviyiyakkam tuṇintu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
2. tamiḻvaḻik kalvi eṉpatu1939 il uyar nilippaḷḷi kaḷil toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu; eṉṟālum aṅṅaṉam tamiḻvaḻip payiṉṟavarkaṭku ērāḷamāṉa taṭikaḷ iṭiñcalkaḷ uṇṭākkappaṭṭaṉa tamiḻ vaḻiyiṉarukkup paṭṭa vakuppup paṭikkumpōtu āṅkila vaḻiyāka māṟṟappaṭṭatu uyarnili vakuppil tamiḻvaḻi, kallūrip putumuka vakuppil tamiḻvaḻi mutukali vakuppil mīṇṭum āṅkilavaḻi eṉa vaḻikaḷ māṟikkoṇṭē yiruntaṉa. mēlē āṅkilam āṭciyaḻuttam ceyta kāraṇattāl, kīḻvakuppiliruntē āṅkilavaḻip paṭippatu eṉṟa eṇṇam vērūṉṟiyatu. maḻali mutal palkali vari taṭaṅkaliṉṟi orēvaḻiyiṟ kaṟkum neṭṭōṭṭatti aracum palkalikkaḻakaṅkaḷum vakuttiruntāl, tamiḻvaḻiyil māṇavar kaḷum, peṟṟōrkaḷum nampikki koṇṭiruntiruppar. mēlum iṅṅaṉam tamiḻ kaṟṟōrkku vēli vāyppum, mēṟpaṭippum eṉṟa uṟutiyilli. muṉṉurimi kūṭa entak kaṭciyum koṭukkavilli cōṟṟukkum vēlikkum ulaka vāḻvukkum poruḷātarattukkum utavātapōtu, tamiḻvaḻik kalviyi vaṭamoḻi pōl muttikkā paṭippārkaḷ? vaṭamoḻi paṭittiruntāṟkūṭak kōyilkaḷilum, kuṭamuḻukkukaḷilum aruccarākip pūci varumāṉam kiṭikkum.
3. mēlum tamiḻvaḻi eṉṟu poruḷiyal, varalāṟu, araciyal eṉak kalit tuṟikaḷi vittārkaḷēyaṉṟik kālattiṟkēṟṟa aṟiviyaṟ pāṭaṅkaḷi vikkavilli. tamiḻil nūlkaḷ illi eṉṟa poyppaḻiyi viṭātu collik koṇṭu varuvatu mētip paḻakkamākiviṭṭatu. nūṟṟāṇṭu kaḷāka evvaḷavō nūlkaḷum pala ilaṭcakkaṇakkāṉa putuc collākkaṅkaḷum vantuḷa. intiya nāṭṭilēyē mutaṉ mutal 7565pakkam koṇṭa kalik kaḷañciyam 10 tokutikaḷ vantuḷatu. āyirattukku mēlāṉa paṭṭattuṟi nūlkaḷ vantuḷa. iṉṉum avvaḷavu nūlkaḷum accāki vanta piṉpu tāṉ pāṭaṅkaḷ vikka muṭiyumā? appaṭi enta moḻikkāvatu ulaka varalāṟu uṇṭā? pāṭam vippatum, nūlkaḷ eḻutuvatum, patippippatum iṇintutāṉē pōkum. iṉṉa āṇṭumutal ellāt tuṟiyum ellā vakuppum tamiḻvaḻi eṉṟu āṇṭuk kuṟippiṭṭu viṭṭāl kaṟṟavarkaḷum patipparkaḷum, vēṇṭum nūṟcelvaṅkaḷi nampikkiyōṭum, tuṇivōṭum veḷiyiṭuvar. tirumaṇanāḷ eṉṟu tēti vitta piṉpu tāṉē pala ēṟpāṭukaḷ mummuramāka innāṭṭil naṭappatuṇṭu. ilaṅkiyil poṟiyiyal maruttuvam ellām tamiḻvaḻi kaṟpikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. vēṇṭiya uyar nūlkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ātaliṉ tamiḻakattil tamiḻvaḻik kalvikku  nūlkaḷ ila eṉpatu kuruṭṭuriyākum. uyarnilippaḷḷi, mēṉilippaḷḷivari tamiḻvaḻi vantapiṉ ippōtu evvaḷavō pāṭa nūlkaḷ vantuviṭṭaṉavē. intiyā urimi peṟṟu 40 āṇṭukaḷ ākiyum iṉṉum nūlkaḷ illi eṉṟu kiḷippitaṟṟik koṇṭiruppatu nāṭṭiṉ kaṭamiyik kaḻippatākum.: tamiḻurimiyip paḻippatākum. āṅkila ātikkam koṇṭa kalvic cātiyār tamiḻik kīḻppaṭuttuvatākum. kāntiyaṭikaḷ tākūr, irātākiruṭṭiṇaṉ, pāratiyār, kāmarācar, aṇṇā mutalāṉa perumakkaḷ maḻali mutal palkali vari, tāymoḻitāṉ ellāt tuṟiyilum payiṟṟu moḻiyāka irukkavēṇṭum eṉavum, iṉṟiya āṅkila ātikkam ciṟupāṉmiyari mēṟcātiyākavum perumpāṉmiyarik kīḻccātiyākavum vēṟṟumip piḷavu paṭuttukiṉṟatu eṉavum teḷivākak kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar.
4. pārāḷ maṉṟaṅkaḷum, caṭṭa maṉṟaṅkaḷum tāymoḻik kalvik koḷki paṟṟit teḷivāṉa tīrmāṉaṅkaḷ ceytuḷḷaṉa. tamiḻakac caṭṭa maṉṟattilum, pēraviyilum, āṭcimoḻiyākavum, payiṟṟu moḻiyākavum tamiḻtāṉ irukkavēṇṭum eṉṟa muṭivuṇṭu. kalviyamiccarkaḷāka viḷaṅkiya perumakkaḷ ti.cu. aviṉāciliṅkaṉārum ci. cuppiramaṇiyaṉārum tamiḻp payiṟṟumoḻik koḷkiyi uṟutiyāka veḷiyiṭṭaṉar. tamiḻakattiṉ ellāk kaṭcikaḷumē ikkoḷkiyil uṭaṉpāṭu koṇṭaṉavē. eṅkaḷ tamiḻvaḻik kalviyiyakkam kūṟum tamiḻ oṉṟē muḻuppayiṟṟu moḻi eṉṟa koḷki putiyataṉṟu: ellāp perumakkaḷum ellāk kaṭcikaḷum muṉpē collimuṭitta paḻaṅkoḷkiyē yākum.
5. koḷkiyil uṟutiyum teḷivum iruntālum, tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ aracukaḷum, kaṭcikaḷum aimpatu āṇṭukaḷāka ataṉic ceyaṟpaṭuttiya muṟikaḷ mikat tavaṟāṉavi: iṭiñcalāṉavi. koḷkik kēṭāṉavi. muṟiyaṟṟa pōkkāl tamiḻit tamiḻ makkaḷiṭam matippiḻakkac ceytu viṭṭārkaḷ: āṅkilap pakkam mikavum cāyumpaṭic ceytu viṭṭārkaḷ. `makkaḷ āṅkilattiyē virumpukiṉṟārkaḷ; tamiḻi virumpavilli’ eṉṟu colvatu muḻuppuraṭṭākum. makkaḷ āṅkila mōkam koṇṭavarkaḷ illi. āṅkilatti vaḷarppatu avarkaḷ eṇṇamum illi. intip pakkam cāykiṉṟārkaḷ. intimēl mōkamā? intiyit tēciya moḻiyāka vaḷarkkum āciyā? illavē illi. etu cōṟu pōṭum; etu vēli tarum; etumēl vāyppu tarum; etu uṭaṉaṭiyākap poruḷātāram tarum; etu ulaka vāḻvu tarum eṉpatuvē iyalpāṉa makkaḷiṉ potu nōkkam. ituvē `poruḷillārkku ivvulakam illi’ eṉpaṟapaṭi, vāḻvu nōkkam. tamiḻvaḻi ettuṟiyum kaṟṟōrukkut tamiḻ nāṭṭil vēlikaḷ ūtiyaṅkaḷ vāyppukkaḷ. patavikaḷ. pataviyuyarvukaḷ, uyark kalvikaḷ eṉṟu tamiḻakavaracukaḷ poruḷātāra vāḻvu vaḻaṅkiyiruntāl tamiḻvaḻik koḷkiyi ellāt tamiḻmakkaḷum āvalōṭu taḻuvi yiruppārkaḷ vāḻvellām āṅkila vaḻikku; vaṟumip piṇiyellām tamiḻvaḻikku eṉṟāl, picciyeṭukkavā tamiḻvaḻik kaṟpārkaḷ? ulaka vāḻvu koṭukka aṟiyāta  tamiḻakavaracukaḷ tamiḻ makkaḷ mēl paḻipōṭṭu viṭṭut tamiḻvaḻiyik kēli ceytu varukiṉṟaṉa; iṉṉum mēlum kēlikku āḷākkukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻmakkaḷ kuṟṟamō mōkamō uṭiyavarkaḷ illi; tamiḻ vaḻikku vēliyum vēli vāyppum piṟa vāyppukaḷum ciṟitum nalkāta tamiḻakavaracukaḷē kuṟṟamuṭiyaṉa paḻiyuṭiyaṉa, maṭamiyuṭiyaṉa, iṉṉum ivvāṟu cellumō? eṉpatē kēḷvi.
6. iṉṉoru karuttiyum tamiḻvaḻi iyakkattārākiya nāṅkaḷ teḷivākac collukiṉṟōm. tamiḻvaḻi kaṟṟavar kaṭkē vēlikaḷum vēli vāyppukkaḷum tamiḻnāṭṭil vaḻaṅka vēṇṭum eṉṟu collumpōtu, makkaḷi nāṅkaḷ pirikkavilli. tamiḻarkaṭkuttāṉ vēli koṭukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu collavilli. tamiḻakattil palamoḻiyāḷarkaḷ vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. teluṅkar, kaṉṉaṭar, maliyāḷar, curāṭṭirar  vaṭanāṭṭiṉar eṉap pala iṉattārum ēṉi mānilaṅkaḷiṟ pōlavē niliyāka iṅkum vāḻntu varukiṉṟaṉar. itu intiyavorumip pāṭākum. ātaliṉ intiya makkaḷi ivviyakkam pirikkavilli eṉṟum tamiḻ vāḻa vēṇṭu mallavā! tamiḻ nilattil vaḷara vēṇṭumallavā? tamiḻnāṭṭil matippōṭu celvākkāka irukkavēṇṭumallavā? tamiḻ ēṉi mānilaṅkaḷil urimiyōṭum, vaḷattōṭum kālūṉṟamuṭiyumā? ātaliṉ tamiḻakattil enta moḻiyiṉar tamiḻvaḻi kaṟṟālum, avarkaṭkellām tamiḻakam vēliyum, piṟavāyppukkaḷum camamāka vaḻaṅkavēṇṭum eṉpatuvē ivviyakkattiṉ teḷivāṉa kōṭpāṭu. tamiḻvaḻikkup piḻippu vāyppu; tamiḻukku vāḻvuvaḷam eṉpatuvē eṅkaḷiṉ moḻi mantiram.
7. tamiḻnāṭṭil maḻali mutal palkali vari ellāt tuṟiyilum tamiḻ oṉṟē payiṟṟumoḻi eṉṟa orumoḻik koḷkiyi valiyuṟuttum nāṅkaḷ ēṉi enta moḻikaḷiyum yārum tuṇi moḻikaḷākak kaṟpati varavēṟkiṉṟōm. kaṟpataṟku niṟinta vāyppu irukkavēṇṭum eṉavum valiyuṟuttukiṉṟōm. iṉṟiya ulakac cūḻniliyil tamiḻp payiṟṟumoḻiyōṭu vēṟu cila intiya moḻikaḷiyum ayalnāṭṭu moḻikaḷiyum kaṟṟuk koḷvatu intiya vorumippāṭṭiṟkum ulakavorumip pāṭṭiṟkum nallatu. āṉāl ellām tamiḻukku aṭuttu amiyavēṇṭumēyaṉṟit tamiḻi viṭuttu amiyakkūṭātu. ituvē uṟutippāṭu.
8. `eṉṟumuḷa teṉ tamiḻ’ eṉṟu kamparum, `cīraḷamit tiṟam’ eṉṟu cuntaraṉārum `yāmaṟinta moḻikaḷilē tamiḻmoḻi pōl iṉitāvatu eṅkum kāṇōm’ eṉṟu pāratiyārum ivvāṟē palarum pōṟṟiya kaṉṉit tamiḻukku āṅkilap paḷḷikaḷil cāvuvāṭi vīcakkaṇṭu maṉaṅkotikkiṉṟōm. kirēkkam, ilattīṉ pōla nam centamiḻum varuṅkālattil vaḻakkiḻantu viṭumō eṉṟu uyirtuṭikkiṉṟōm. itaṟkuk kāraṇam eṉṉa? nam pālvāyp pacuntamiḻk kuḻantikaḷ iraṇṭari vayatilēyē ṭu.mu.ḻu. eṉṟa āṅkila vaḻip paḷḷikaḷ eṉṉum toḻuvaṅkaḷil aṭikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. aṅkut taṉ peyar, tāyppeyar, tanti peyar kūṭa olikka varāta ippaccitaḻk kuḻantikaṭku aṅku āṅkilac coṟkaḷum āṅkilavoliyum āṅkilat toṭip pāṭalkaḷum tiṇikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa; tāymoḻi tamiḻ eṉṟa uṇarvu ivarkaṭkup pocukkappaṭukiṉṟatu; nāṭṭuppaṟṟu muḷiyilē kiḷḷappaṭukiṉṟatu; itaḻum pallum muṟṟāta, uṇavu ūṭṭiviṭavēṇṭiya, uṭi māṭṭiviṭavēṇṭiya iraṇṭari vayatil āṅkilamoḻi māṟṟam ceyvatu matamāṟṟattilum koṭiyatu; kolikku oppāṉatu. itaṉkēṭu collum taramaṉṟu. kuḻaliṉum yāḻiṉum iṉiya tamiḻ pēca vēṇṭiya maḻalikaḷ, iraṇṭari vayatil āṅkila vāyppaṭutal eṉpatu iyaṟkiyaṉṟu; aṟamaṉṟu; etirkāla nalamaṉṟu. mēlum āṅkilap paḷḷikaḷ, piḷḷika cumakka muṭiyātapaṭi nūl kaḷiyum, kuṟippu nūlkaḷiyum vitikkiṉṟaṉa. ikkuḻantikaḷ cumitūkkikaḷākak kāṭciyaḷikkiṉṟaṉa. kallūri māṇavaṉkūṭa mikac cila nūlkaḷiyē vakuppukkuk koṇṭu varukiṉṟāṉ. inta āṅkilap paḷḷik kaṭikaḷil aṭikkappaṭum kuḻantikaḷō nāḷtōṟum taḷḷāṭic cumakkum nūl mūṭṭi kaṉamāṉatu; pala vīṭukaḷil peṟṟōrkaḷum, vēlikkārikaḷum ivaṟṟic cumantu koṭukkiṉṟaṉar. ituvā kalvi? mēlum inta āṅkilak kaṭikaḷil cērppataṟkup pallāyiram vaṉkoṭikaḷ koṭukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. mutal vakuppiṟkē rū. 5000 aḷavu ākiṉṟatu. mūṉṟu nāṉku viḷḷikaḷ uṭiya kuṭumpam ivvāṟu ciṟu kalvikkuc celavaḻittāl, ōṭu eṭukkavēṇṭiya nili varum. ivi āṅkila mōkattiṉ māyikaḷ, taṅkaḷ kuḻantikaḷ naṉṟākap paṭikkavēṇṭum eṉṟa peṟṟōriṉ āciyi, āṅkilap paḷḷikaḷ paṇamākkic curaṇṭukiṉṟaṉa. iviyellām aracu pārttuk koṇṭiruppatutāṉ vēṭikkiyākum.
9. oru ciṟu peṭṭik kaṭi veṟṟili pākkuk kaṭi vippataṟkum, kāykaṟik kaṭi vippataṟkum, ciṟṟuṇṭik kaṭi vippataṟkum, ciṟu toḻil naṭattuvataṟkum, ciṟu accakam naṭattuvataṟkum icivu vēṇṭum eṉṟu kaṭiya vitikaḷ ceyyum aracu mūli muṭukku maṟuku ciṟṟūr tōṟum ṭu.mu.ḻu. ru.mu.ḻu. eṉa āṅkilap paḷḷik kaṭikaḷ vippataṟku icivu uṇṭā eṉṟu kēṭpatilli; ciṟu kuḻantikaḷukku āṅkila nañcūṭṭum ippaḷḷikaḷit taṭiceyvatumilli. oru puṟam `nīrārum kaṭaluṭutta nilamaṭanti’ eṉṟa tamiḻ vāḻttu: iṉṉorupuram mammi ṭāṭi eṉṟa āṅkilap payiṟci ātaliṉ eṅkaḷ tamiḻvaḻik kalviyiyakkam aracukkuc colvatu yātu? pacciḷam tamiḻk kuḻantiyiṉatti iraṇṭari vayatilēyē āṅkila moḻimāṟṟam ceyyum paḷḷikaḷit taṭi ceyya vēṇṭum. kalviyiṉ pērāl kuḻantikaḷip puttakac cumiyāl vāṭṭip peṟṟōrkaḷiṭam paṇam kaṟakkum koṭumiyi akaṟṟa vēṇṭum; itumaṭṭum pōtātu; ciṟu maḻalit tamiḻp paḷḷikaḷi ūrtōṟum aracē toṭaṅkavēṇṭum. kuḻantikaḷik kāppatu araciṉ poṟuppu. ēḻāvatu aintāṇṭut tiṭṭattil tēciya eḻuttaṟiviyakkam eṉṟa mutiyōr kalvittiṭṭam toṭaṅki ataṟku 600 kōṭi rūpāy otukkappaṭiruntatu. 50, 60 vayatu vanta mutiyōr kalvikkup perumpayaṉiṉṟic celavaḻippativiṭa, pūñceṭipōla vaḷarum pacciḷaṅ kuḻantikaḷiṉ tamiḻ maḻalip paḷḷikkut tiṭṭamiṭṭu niti celavu ceyvatu tāṉē nāṭṭukku niliyāṉa etirkāla muṉṉēṟṟamākum. tamiḻ nāṭṭu aracuk kalvit tiṭṭa āyvukkuḻu mukkiyamāka āṅkilac ciṟu paḷḷikaḷiṉ kalvi muṟikaḷiyum nūṟcumikaḷiyumāvatu uṭaṉē kavaṉittu oḻuṅkupaṭuttavēṇṭum. ataṉmēl, tamiḻ maḻalip paḷḷikaḷi aracu tāṉē niṟuvuvatōṭu, taṉiyār toṭaṅkum ippaḷḷikaṭku vaḻakkampōl māṉiyattoki vaḻaṅka vēṇṭum. inta neṟikaḷi mēṟkoṇṭāl peṟṟōriṉ cumi kuṟivatōṭu, kuḻantikaḷiṉ tāymoḻik kalvi vaḷarvatōṭu, tamiḻukkuc cāvu vāṭi nīṅkum. iṉṟu uṇavu ilavacam kālaṇi ilavacam, puttakaṅkaḷ ilavacam eṉṟa peyarēyaṉṟi, paḷḷikaḷiṟ cērvatu ilavacamāka irukkavilli. paṇam kaṟakkum tīmiyik kaṇṭuvaruntukiṉṟōm. inta ūḻali niṟuttuvatu araciṉ taliyāya poṟuppu.
10. iṉṟiya kalvi naṭimuṟiyoṉṟi veḷippaṭutta virumpukiṉṟōm. āṅkilavaḻi eṉṟu cērntupaṭikkum māṇavarkaḷil palar paṭṭa vakuppukaḷil tērvu eḻutumpōtu tamiḻiltāṉ eḻutukiṉṟārkaḷ. itaṟkup palkalik kaḻakamum icivu vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷatu. muḻutum āṅkilamāka eḻutum āṟṟalō collik koṭukkum āṟṟalō varavarak kuṟintu viṭṭatu. āṅkilavaḻi eṉṟa poyt tōṟṟam iṉiyum ēṉ? cāṉṟitaḻkaḷil ippōtu āṅkilavaḻi eṉṟō tamiḻvaḻi eṉṟō kuṟippatilli. kuṟippatu tāṉ nallatu. appōtutāṉ uṇmi veḷippaṭum. tamiḻvaḻi kaṟṟavarkkē etirkālattil tamiḻnāṭṭil vēliyum piṟa vāyppukkaḷum varappōvatāl, cāṉṟitaḻkaḷil entavaḻi eṉpataṉi iṉiyēṉum kuṟittāka vēṇṭum. vaḻiyi maṟippatu nalamākātu. 
11. tamiḻaka āḷunar aracu ivvāṇṭumutal tamiḻukku mēlum oru putikuḻiyit tōṇṭiyirukkiṟatu. mutal vakuppiliruntē āṅkilam vāymoḻip pāṭamākac collik koṭukkappaṭumām. aintuvayatuk kuḻantikku āṅkilap pēccu etaṟku? yārōṭu āṅkilam pēcappōkiṟatō, teriyavilli. tamiḻ maḻaliyākap pēcum ivvayatil āṅkilappēccu ūṭāṭiṉāl, tamiḻ olikaḷ keṭṭuviṭum. kuḻantiyiṉ itaḻkaḷ eṭṭu vayatukku mēlē tāṉ olic cemmiyum, naṭic cemmiyum peṟum. aintu vayatilē maṇippiravāḷamā? tamiḻ oliyum, col vaṭivum evvaḷavu pāḻpaṭṭu viṭum? tamiḻi vērilē keṭuppataṟku itiviṭa vēṟu cūḻcci vēṇṭiya tilli. mutal vakuppilēyē āṅkilam pukuttuvatu ēṟkaṉavē kāḷāṉ pōlap peruki irukkum āṅkila maḻalip paḷḷikaṭkut tūpam pōṭuvatākum; pātukāppuk koṭuttu valuppaṭuttuvatākum. āṅkilam kaṟpippatupōla ōr āci kāṭṭik koṭukkum pōkku itu. oruvar parinturi ceytār eṉṟāl parinturiyellām aracu ēṟṟukkoḷkiṉṟatā? intap parinturiyic cilakālamuḷḷa āḷunar aracu niṟivēṟṟa vēṇṭumā? āṅkila āṭci irunta kālattiṟ kūṭa aintām vakuppukkuppiṉpu tāṉ āṅkilam paḷḷiyiṟ collik koṭukkappaṭṭatu. iṉṟu pakkattu mānilaṅkaḷiṟkūṭa aintāvatu vakuppukku mēl tāṉ kaṟpikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. ātaliṉ tamiḻaka kuḻantikaḷik keṭukkum mutal vakuppu āṅkilattik kiviṭumāṟu āḷunar aracik kēṭṭukkoḷkiṉṟōm.
12. tamiḻvaḻik kalviyiyakkattiṉ muṭivāṉa koḷki kaḷi mīṇṭum aracukkum, makkaṭkum teḷivuppaṭuttukiṟōm.
a. maḻali mutul palkali vari ellāt tuṟiyilum tamiḻ oṉṟē payiṟṟu moḻiyāka vēṇṭum. ituvē oru moḻik koḷki.
ā. enta intiya moḻikaḷiyum āṅkila mutalāṉa ayaṉ moḻikaḷiyum tuṇi moḻikaḷākak kaṟkalām, kaṟka vēṇṭum, kaṟkumpoḻutu vāyppup peruka vēṇṭum itu tuṇi moḻik koḷki.
i. tamiḻnāṭṭil tamiḻvaḻi kaṟṟavarkkē vēliyum vēlivāyppum, uyar kalviyum, piṟa vāyppukkaḷum uṇṭu. itu vāḻvuk koḷki, cōṟum, poruḷum ulaka matippum tarāta tamiḻ tīṇṭā moḻiyākic cettuviṭumallavā! ātaliṉ vēlip piḻippu tamiḻvaḻikkē vaḻaṅkappaṭum. enta intiyaṉ tamiḻvaḻi kaṟṟālum avaṉukkum avaḷukkum ellām tamiḻnāṭṭil vēli kiṭikkum.
ī. āṅkila vaḻiyil uyar kalvi kaṟpār kaṟkaṭkum; ataṟku ivviyakkam taṭiyilli. veḷi mānilaṅkaḷilum veḷināṭu kaḷilum paranta aḷavil vēlivāyppuk kiṭikkum eṉṟu tāṉē āṅkila vaḻiyiṟ kaṟkiṉṟārkaḷ. ātaliṉ avarkaḷ vēlikaḷip parantavulakiṟ peṟaṭṭum. tamiḻ vaḻi kaṟṟavarkaṭku, tamiḻ nāṭṭil vēlikaḷi viṭṭuk koṭukkaṭṭum. ituvē vāḻvaṟam, ulakamoḻiyāṉa āṅkila vaḻiyiṟ paṭṭam peṟṟuviṭṭut tamiḻnāṭṭil vēli kēṭpatum, tamiḻnāṭṭi nampit tamiḻ kaṟṟavariṉ vēli vāyppiṟ paṅku pōṭuvatum muṟiyaṉṟu. tamiḻ mānilattil vēli vēṇṭumeṉṟāl, tamiḻvaḻippaṭi; vēṟu vaḻikaḷiṟ paṭittāl vēṟiṭaṅkaḷil vēli tēṭu’ ituvē ivviyakkattiṉ nalluri.
u. tamiḻnāṭṭil ciṟu maḻalip paḷḷikaḷiyum aracē toṭaṅkavēṇṭum; toṭaṅkum taṉiyār paḷḷikaṭku uriya niṟimāṉiyam vaḻaṅka vēṇṭum. āṅkila vaḻip paḷḷikaḷiṉ naṭimuṟikaḷic cemmip paṭuttuvataṟkum, avaṟṟit tamiḻvaḻip paḷḷikaḷāka māṟṟuvataṟkum atikāram mikka uyar kuḻu oṉṟu aracu uṭaṉaṭiyāka amikka vēṇṭum. vaṉkoṭi kaḷ vāṅkuvataṟkup peṟukic cīṭṭuk koṭuttuviṭṭāl pōtātu. atu muttiriyiṭṭa ūḻalākum. ātaliṉ nērmiyum, nāṇayamum, eḷimiyum kalviyulakiṟ kaṭippiṭikka vēṇṭum.
ū. ivvāṇṭu mutalāmvakuppu mutal āṅkilam collik koṭukkum takāta kalvimuṟiyi uṭaṉaṭiyāka āḷunar aracu niṟuttavēṇṭum. appōtu tāṉ kuḻantip paruvattut tamiḻ piḻiyaṟṟa cemmiyāṉa tamiḻāka vaḷarntu patiyum.
e. it tamiḻvaḻik kalviyakkam kāntīya muṟiyilum vaḷḷuva neṟiyilum ceyalpaṭṭu oḻukum. ellārum oppuk koḷḷappaṭṭa tamiḻ payiṟṟu moḻik koḷkiyum, vāḻvutarum vēli vāyppum ettakiya pōrāṭṭamumiṉṟi virivil niṟivēṟum eṉṟu nampukiṟōm. makkaḷmēl paḻi pōṭāmal, aracu tāṉ teḷintu tuṇintu ceyalpaṭutta vēṇṭum eṉṟu kēṭṭu;kakoḷkiṉṟōm. `mellat tamiḻiṉam cākum eṉṟa collukku iṭamillāmal cāvut ticiyiṉiṉṟu kaṉṉit tamiḻiṉik kāppatu ovvoruvariṉ piṟappuk kaṭami. ikkaṭamiyi niṉivuṟuttavum niṟivēṟṟavum tōṉṟiyatu ivviyakkakam. vēṇṭumāṉāl, kāntiyaṭikaḷ kāṭṭiya aṟap pōrāṭṭaṅkaḷiyellām ivviyakkam ettuṉpam variṉum mēṟkoḷḷum eṉpatu vāymi.
`vāymiyē vellum’ eṉpatu nam araciṉ kōpura moḻi.
 - mūtaṟiñar cemmal va.cupa. māṇikkaṉār
maturi ūrvalam nikaḻcci viḷakkam
`kuṟaḷiyam’ tiṅkaḷitaḻ veḷiyīṭu
kuṟaḷiyam (01-05-1989)
mūtaṟiñar cemmal va.cupa.mā. maṟintārē!
yār, yāri, yārāl, evarāl, eppaṭi āṟutal paṭuttuvatu?
tamiḻiyē tam uyir mūccākak koṇṭum, tirukkuṟaḷiyum, tolkāppiyattiyum tam iru kaṇkaḷākak koṇṭum vāḻntu kāṭṭiya perumakaṉār tamiḻc cāṉṟōr mūtaṟiñar cemmal muṉivar va.cupa. māṇikkam avarkaḷ āvar. tamiḻāciriyarākat toṭarnta tam paṇiyit tam aṟiviṉ tiṟattālum, paṇpālum, oḻukkattālum, uṇmip piṭippālum, tuṇivēntar takutiyōṭu muṭitta perumi ivarkkē uṇṭu.
ivā tamiḻaṉṉikkup paṭitta taṉṉikarillāt `tamiḻk kātalum’ vaṇmi mikum “vaḷḷuvamum” olkāp perum pukaḻt tolkāppiyak kaṭalum”, cīroḷi parappum “tirukkuṟaṭ cuṭarum” ākiya nūlkaḷ kālattāl maṟiyātaṉa. tamiḻ uḷḷaḷavum pukaḻ parappi niṟpaṉa. etirkālat tamiḻiṉa makkaṭkuk kalaṅkari viḷakkamāka vaḻi kāṭṭi vāḻac ceyvaṉa.
taṉittamiḻt tanti tamiḻkkaṭal maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ, tamiḻt teṉṟal tiru.vi.ka., tamiḻt tiṅkaḷ mu.va. ākiya tamiḻc cāṉṟōrkaḷiṉ pēccākavum mūccākavum avar nammiṭiyē naṭamāṭiṉār. tali ciṟanta tamiḻp paṟṟāḷarākavum, tamiḻp paṟṟi ūṭṭupavarākavum, tamiḻk kāppāḷarākavum viḷaṅkiṉār. mūtaṟiñar cemmal aiyā avarkaḷ tolkāppiya āyvilum tōyvilum, caṅka ilakkiya ārāycciyilum, nam maṟiyām tirukkuṟaḷ paṟṟilum aṉittu ilakkaṇa ilakkiya āyvilum taṉṉikaraṟṟu ḷaṅkiṉār.
kuṟaḷiyam (01-05-1989)
tamiḻ ulakattukku putiya collākkaṅkaḷi tantavar mūtaṟiñar va.cupa. māṇikkaṉār
- kuṉṟakkuṭipoṉṉampala aṭikaḷār pukaḻāram
tamiḻ ulakattukku putiya collākkaṅkaḷit tantavar mūtaṟiñar cemmal va.cupa.māṇikkaṉār eṉa kuṉṟakkuṭi poṉṉampala aṭikaḷār pukaḻāram cūṭṭiṉār.
civakaṅki māvaṭṭam kārikkuṭi aḻakappā palkalik kaḻakattil va.cupa.māṇikkaṉār nūṟṟāṇṭu toṭakkaviḻā nēṟṟu naṭipeṟṟatu. palkalikkaḻaka tuṇivēntar co. cuppiyā talimi vakittu pēciyatāvatu:
mūtaṟiñar va.cupa. māṇikkaṉār mikac ciṟanta āciriyar. mikac ciṟiya kirāmattil piṟantu, mikap periya palkalikkaḻakamāṉa maturi kāmarācar palkalikkaḻakattil tuṇivēntarāka uyarntiruppatu ivaruṭiya uḻippukku cāṉṟākum. ivar mikac ciṟanta tamiḻaṟiñar, kaviñar, pēccāḷar, eḻutatāḷarākat tikaḻntirukkiṟār. mēṭikaḷil pēcuvatu pōla vīṭṭilum kalappaṭamillāta tūya tamiḻil pēcum iyalpiṉar. taṉṉuṭiya paṇikaḷi tāṉē ceytukoḷḷum eḷimip paṇpiṉar. taṉṉuṭiya cottil āṟil oru pakutiyi potunalattukkuc celaviṭa viruppam terivittavar. ivar ovvoru nāḷum ati kāliyil eḻuntu, tiṉamum 4 maṇi nērattukku mēl nūlkaḷip paṭikkum iyalpiṉar. ivaruṭiya ittakiya paṇpukaḷi iṉṟiya māṇavarkaḷ piṉpaṟṟa vēṇṭum. ivvāṟu avar kūṟiṉār.
kuṉṟakkuṭi poṉṉampala aṭikaḷār pēcumpōtu, “tamiḻukkuk kiṭitta māṇikkam, vaṟṟāta tamiḻak kaṭal, poy collā māṇikkam, nīṇṭa neṭiya varalāṟṟup peṭṭakam, paṉmukap parimāṇam koṇṭavar va.cupa. māṇikkaṉār.
avar vāḻum kālattil ceyta paṇikaḷ avar kālattukkup piṟakum avar kālattukkup piṟakum niṉittup pārkkappaṭuvatu ciṟappukkuriyatu. vāḻkkiyil vāymiyāy vāḻvataṟku tamiḻ oṉṟē vaḻiyeṉa atit tērnteṭuttavar. taṉ vāḻnāḷ muḻuvatum uḻippu, vāymi, nērmi, ākiya mūṉṟiyum mutaṉmiyākak koṇṭavar. parantupaṭṭa marapuk kaṇṇōṭṭattil ārāyum ciṟappiṉar.
putiya collākkaṅkaḷit tamiḻ ulakattukkut tantavar. iḷiya talimuṟiyiṉar aṟivi vaḷarttuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum, cintikka vēṇṭum eṉṟa eṇṇatti taṉ eḻuttuk kaḷilum, pēccukkaḷilum toṭarntu veḷippaṭuttiyavar. iḷam talimuṟiyiṉar entap pātiyil payaṇikka vēṇṭum eṉpati eṭutturittavar. tilli ampalattil tirumuṟikaḷ ēṟa maṟukkappaṭṭapōtu, ataṉi etirttup pōrāṭiya tamiḻp pōrāḷi va.cupa.māṇikkaṉār. tamiḻ aṟiviyum, tamiḻ uṇarviyum iḷam talimuṟiyiṉar va.cupa. māṇikkaṉāriṭa miruntu kaṟṟuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum” eṉṟār.
nikaḻcciyil, va.cupa. māṇikkaṉāriṉ māṇavarkaḷāṉa pērāciriyarkaḷ tē.cokkaliṅkam, ku.ve. pālacuppiramaṇiyam, paḻa. muttu vīrappaṉ, pērāciriyar irā. mōkaṉ ākiyōr karutturi vaḻaṅkiṉar.
muṉṉatāka, va.cupa. māṇikkaṉāriṉ iṇiyataḷam toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu. va.cupa. māṇikkaṉāriṉ taṟcintaṉikaḷ eṉṉum nūli kuṉṟakkuṭi aṭikaḷār veḷiyiṭa tuṇi vēntar co.cuppiyā peṟṟukkoṇṭār.
va.cupa. māṇikkaṉāriṉ mūtta makaṉ va.cupa. mā. tolkāppiyaṉ aṟimuka uriyāṟṟiṉār. va.cupa.māṇikkaṉāriṉ putalvarkaḷ pāri, pūṅkuṉṟaṉ, putalviyar teṉṟal, mātari, poṟṟoṭi maṟṟum va.cupa. māṇikkaṉāriṉ māṇavarkaḷ tiraḷāṉōr kalantukoṇṭaṉar.
muṉṉatāka tamiḻt tuṟit talivar mu. pāṇṭi varavēṟṟār. pērāciriyar cu. rācārām naṉṟi kūṟiṉār.
- tiṉamaṇi 18.04.2017 ceyti.
arumpērācāṉ va.cupa. māṇikkaṉār
1975 `mutukalit tamiḻ vakuppil cēravēṇṭi, ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakam cārnta iru kallūrikaḷukkum aṇṇāmalip palkalikkaḻakattiṟkuma viṇṇappittiruntēṉ. kallūrikaḷ iraṇṭilumiruntu cērkkik kaṭitam vanta niliyil, nāṉ mikavum virumpiya aṇṇāmalip palkalikkaḻakattiliruntu aḻipapu varavilli. terintu varalāmeṉṟu nēril ceṉṟu, tamiḻttuṟit talivarik kaṇṭu vaṇaṅki, `aiyā, nāṉ em.ē. vakuppil cēra viṇṇappittirukkiṟēṉ’ eṉṟu colli muṭippataṟkuḷ, `em.ō.el. vakuppil cērukiṟāyā?’ eṉṟu viṭi viṉā vantatatu. atuvari nāṉ aṟintirāta oru paṭippil cērac coṉṉatāl, `aiyā, nāṉ em.ē..’ eṉṟu iḻuppataṟkuḷ, `paṉṉippaṉṉip pēcakkūṭātu; em.ō.el. cērukiṟāyā?’ eṉṟa atē viṉā! orunoṭi nāṉ vāyaṭitta niliyil uṭaṉpaṭṭut taliyacittēṉ pōlum. `kaṭitam varum’. `uṭaṉē vantu cēr’ eṉṟu āṇi piṟantatu. veḷiyil vantutāṉ viṉavit terintukoṇṭēṉ, em.ō.el. eṉpatu `māsṭar āḵp ōriyaṇṭal lērṉiṅs’ (kīḻikkalvi vallunar - tamiḻ) eṉṟum, atippaṭikkac coṉṉavartām pērāciriyar va.cupa. māṇikkaṉār eṉṟum!
aṇṇāmalip palkalikkaḻakattil antakkālat tamiḻ em.ē., yi viṭavum akalamum āḻamum koṇṭa tamiḻaṟivup pāṭattiṭṭattuṭaṉ payiṟṟuvikkappaṭṭa em.ō.el. tamiḻp paṭippip paṟṟi māṇavarkaḷ palarum aṟiyāmiyālum aṟintu miraṇṭatālum avvakuppil māṇavar cērkki aruki yiruntatu. āyiṉum, pērāciriyar appaṭippi vaḷarkkum koḷkip piṭippōṭu, em.ē. vakuppiṟku viṇṇappikkum māṇavarkaḷi maṭimāṟṟam ceytu em.ō.el. vakuppil cērttup payiṟṟuvittār. anta āṇṭil mutal māṇavaṉākac cērnta eṉṉit toṭarntu 10 māṇavarkaḷuma 5 māṇavikaḷumāka 16 pēric cērttu veṟṟi kaṇṭār va.cupa. aṭutta āṇṭum toṭarnta em.ō.el. 1977il pērāciriyariṉ ōyvukkuppiṉ toṭara iyalāmal tuvaṇṭatu.
iṉṟupōl karuttaraṅkukaḷ mikavumnaṭipeṟāta akkālaṅkaḷil, `tolkāppiyak karuttaraṅku’ eṉpatupōl ovvoru potuttalippil turiyāciriyarkaḷ aṉivarum taṉittaṉi āyvukkaṭṭurikaḷ paṭikka vēṇṭum eṉṟa kaṭṭāyattuṭaṉ, āṇṭiṟku iru muṟiyāka ēḻāṇṭukaḷil 14 karuttaraṅkukaḷi mummūṉṟu nāḷkaḷil kōlākalat tiruviḻākkaḷip pōl naṭattiya cātaṉi kuṟippiṭattakkatu.
1976-1977 kalviyāṇṭil pērāciriyar va.cupa.viṉ talimip perumitam veḷippaṭumāṟu nikaḻntatōr nikaḻvu iṅkē niṉivukūrat takkatu. naṭuvaṇ aracil muṉaṉāḷ amiccarāyirunta āṟṟalum celvākkum mikka oruvar aṇṇāmalip palkalik kaḻakattiṉ tuṇivēntarāy vantiruntār. orunāḷ muṉṉaṟivippu ētumiṉṟit tamiḻt tuṟiyip pārviyiṭa vantirunta tuṇivēntar, aṅkē makā vittuvāṉ, ca. taṇṭapāṇi tēciyar, perumpulavar cevēṅkaṭarāmac ceṭṭiyār pōṉṟa mutuperum pērāciriyarkaḷ amarntirunta āyvirukkip pakutikkuḷ ceṉṟu, pārampariyat tōṟṟattilirunta accāṉṟōrkaḷi matikkāmal, `nīṅkaḷ yār? iṅkē eṉṉa ceykiṟīrkaḷ?’ eṉṟu viṉavavē, tammi aṟimukappaṭuttikkoṇṭu `nāṉ kamparāmāyaṇa āyvuceykiṟēṉ’ eṉṟu kūṟiya aṟiñariṭam, `mikavum avaciyantāṉ” eṉṟu ikaḻcaciyākak kūṟip pōy, aṭutta aṟiyil paṇṭitamaṇiyiṉ makaṉār kati. tiyākarācaṉ pāṭam naṭattik koṇṭirunta em.ō.el. mutalāmāṇṭu vakuppiṉuḷ nuḻintavar, tariyil kiṭantatōr kākita kiḻicalik kaṇṭu kotippaṭintu, ataṉi eṭukkumāṟu āciriyariyē ēviṉār.
avar tikittu niṉṟa niliyil, māṇavarkaḷ poṅkiyatu kaṇṭa tuṇivēntar aṅkiruntu pōyviṭṭār. uṭaṉē, māṇavarkaḷ aṉivarum vakuppi viṭṭu veḷiyē vantu tuṇivēntarukku etirāka muḻakkamiṭavē, avarkaḷi uṭaṉaṭiyāka amitippaṭuttiya pērāciriyar va.cupa. tāmē virintupōyt tuṇivēntaric cantittu, nuṇmāṇ nuḻipulattōṭu nayantu uriyāṭi, avarē varuttam terivikkumaḷavu avartam mikicceyalkaḷi uṇarttiviṭṭu mīṇṭār. aṉṟiya nikaḻvil arumpērācāṉ va.cupa.maliya māṉiṉ makkaḷi yāṉikkālkaḷāl iṭaṟat tuṇinta puṟanāṉūṟṟuk kiḷḷivaḷavaṉiṉ mikicceyali māṟṟiya kōvūrkiḻārākavē eṅkaḷukkuta tōṉṟiṉār.
kārikkuṭiyil tamiḻccaṅkam oṉṟi niṟuvit tām ataṉ talivarāyiruntu aruntamiḻt toṇṭukaḷ pala āṟṟiyuḷḷār va.cupa. aṇṇāmalip palkalikkaḻakap paṇi ōyvukkuppiṉ maturi - kāmarācar palkalikkaḻakattiṉ tuṇivēntarākap poṟuppēṟṟup purinta cātaṉikaḷ palavaṟṟuḷ, appalkalik kaḻakattōṭu iṇintirunta vevvēṟu ūrkaḷic cērnta aintu kallūrikaḷiṉ tamiḻttuṟikaḷi uyarāyvu miyaṅ kaḷākat tara mēmpāṭu ceytatu iṅkē niṉivukūrattakkatu. ammuṟiyil kārikkuṭi aḻakappā kallūriyiṉ tamiḻttuṟi mutalil uyarāyvu miyamāṉataṉ toṭarcciyākavē atu palkalikkaḻakamāy vaḷarcciyuṟṟatu. tām ēṟkaṉavē pērāciriyarakavum tuṟittalivarākavum mutalvarākavum paṇiyāṟṟiya aḻakappā kalvi niṟuvaṉattiṉ vaḷarccikkum avvakiyil ciṟanta vaḻivakuttār va.cupa.
va.cupa. tamiḻakam uḷḷiṭṭa intiyat tirunāṭṭiṟkum ulakat tamiḻarkaḷukkum koṭuttuḷḷa tamiḻkkoṭikaḷ uccimēṟ koṇṭu meccattakkavi. āyvunūlkaḷākavum aruntamiḻp paṭippilakkiyaṅkaḷākavum nērmuka araṅkukaḷilum kaṟpaṉi araṅkukaḷilum āṟṟiya coṟpoḻivu nūlkaḷākavum kaṭita ilakkiyamākavum avaraḷittuḷḷa nūṟkoṭikaḷ āḻntakaṉṟa karuttuk karuvūlaṅkaḷāy amintuḷḷaṉa.
āyvu nūlkaḷuḷ talimi cāṉṟa tamiḻk kātal, tolkāppiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷōṭu caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷil pāṭappeṟṟa akattiṇikaḷiṉ taṉittaṉmikaḷi āyntu viḷakkum arumiyuṭiyatu. annūl veḷivanta nāḷmutal ataṉ āyvuk karuttukaḷi mēṟkoḷḷāmal ōr akattiṇi āyvum ciṟappuṟa aminta tilli. avvakiyil iṉṟiyami yāta akattiṇi āyvākat tamiḻkkātali vaṭittuḷḷār va.cupa.
aṇṇāmalip palkalikkaḻaka aṟakkaṭṭaḷi araṅkil kāppiyap pārvi, kāppiyakkaḷaṅkaḷ, kāppiya nērmi eṉṉum mūvaki nōkkil kamparāmāyaṇattiṉ amippum kampariṉ ciṟappum kuṟittu va.cupa. nuṭpamuṭaṉ nikaḻttiya mūṉṟu coṟpoḻivukaḷiṉ nūlvaṭivē `kampar’ eṉpatām. ulakappotumaṟi tirukkuṟaḷ karuttukaḷip paṉṉiru talippukkaḷil pakuttukkoṇṭu kaṟpaṉi araṅkukaḷil nikaḻttiya coṟpoḻivu nūlākak kuṟikkōḷ naṭiyuṭaṉ `vaḷḷuvam’ eṉṟa nūliyum vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷār.
tolkāppiyak kaṭal, caṅkaneṟi, tirukkuṟaṭcuṭar, kāppiyappārvi, ilakkiyac cāṟu ākiya aintu tokuppukaḷil avvapporuṇmi paṟṟi mottam 111 āyvuk kaṭṭurikaḷi aḷittuḷḷa pērāciriyariṉ āyvu nuṭpaṅkaḷ aṟintupōṟṟat takkavi.
maṉiviyiṉ urimi, nellikkaṉi, uppaṅkaḻi, oru noṭiyil eṉum nāṭaka nūlkaḷ nāṉkiṉip paṭittuḷḷa pērāciriyar, avaṟṟiṉ vāyilāy ilakkiya marapilum camūka - kuṭumpa nilikaḷilum kaṭippiṭikka vēṇṭiya karuttu viḷakkaṅkaḷik kaviṉuṟak kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
iḷami mutalē tōynta colnayam, poruḷ nayaṅkaḷōṭu tām pāṭiya pāṭalkaḷ, kavitikaḷi māmalarkaḷ eṉṟa tokuppu nūlāka veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār va.cupa. vaḷḷal aḻakapparip paṟṟi avar pāṭiya 171 veṇpākkaḷiṉ tokuppu koṭiviḷakku eṉṟa nūlāka oḷirkiṉṟatu. aṇṇāmali aracar, muttiyavēḷ, aḻakappar ākiyōr paṟṟi, avaravar kalvi niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷil pāṭappaṭṭu varukiṉṟa vāḻttup pāṭalkaḷ ellām va.cupa.viṉ vārppukkaḷēyām.
ivvāṟu āyvāḷarāka, āciriyarāka, nāṭakap paṭippāḷarāka, kaviñarāka, iṉṉum paṉmukappaṭṭa āḷumittiṟaṉkaḷōṭu āṟṟal vāynta neñcuram mikka nirvākiyākavum tikaḻntavar arumpērācāṉ va.cupa. māṇikkaṉār
- muṉivar va. kurunātaṉ.
tiṉamaṇi (tamiḻmaṇi 23.4.2017)
